6.	camaṇarkaḷ iyaṟṟiya pūrvīkac ceyyuḷ vakiyoṉṟiṉ peyarākap paṇṇatti eṉṉuñcol vaḻaṅki vantuḷatu. pakavati vyāka paṇṇatti, cūrya paṇṇatti, canta paṇṇatti eṉpavaṟṟi utāraṇamākak kāṭṭalām. camaṇa nūlkaḷil tavira iv ariya peyar vēṟōriṭattuṅ kāṇappeṟavilli. vijñāpaṉam viṇṇāṇam eṉap pākatac citivāṉāṟpōla prakñapti eṉpatu paṇṇatti eṉa vantuḷatu. pākata nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum ceyyuḷ vakiyil ilakkaṇattit tamiḻc ceyyuḷ vakikkuc cārttit tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟutalāl avarum camaṇar eṉpatu pōtarum.
7.	vitika camayattavarāl toṉṟutoṭṭu mēṟ koḷḷappaṭātatu aintira viyākaraṇamākum. puṟaccamayattavaruḷ camaṇar ivviyākaraṇattip peritum pōṟṟi vantaṉar. aintira niṟinta tolkāppiyaṉ, eṉak kūṟappaṭutalāl tolkāppiyarum camaṇa camayattic cārntavareṉa uyt tuṇaralākum.
iṉi, ikkāraṇaṅkaḷi muṟiyē ārāyvōm.
(1)	paṭimā eṉpatu camaṇaccamayac collēyeṉpa taṟkup paṇṭik kālattut tamiḻ nūlkaḷilum vaṭamoḻi nūlkaḷilum mēṟkōḷ illi. ki. pi. āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṟpaṭṭa camaṇ camaya nūlkaḷē ippoḻutuḷḷaṉa eṉa ārāycciyāḷar kūṟuvar. piṟkālattu nūlkaḷil eṅkēyō aruki vaḻaṅkum paṭimā eṉṉum pākatac collic camaṇ camayattavarkkē urimi ceytal kūṭātu. vaṭanāṭṭilē kutama cākkiyar kālattil vāḻnta putta camayattavarum camaṇa camayattavarum tam kālattukku muṟpaṭṭa tava muṉivarkaḷiṉ oḻukalāṟṟiyē aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu tamakkuriya oḻukalāṟukaḷi variyaṟuttuk koṇṭaṉar eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar tuṇipākum.1 aṉṟiyum camaṇa putta mataṅkaḷ innāṭṭil tōṉṟutaṟkup paṉṉūṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭut tōṉṟiyavar āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉārāvar. avvāciriyarik kuṟittup paṉampāraṉār kūṟiya paṭimiyōṉ eṉṉum col taṉmiyeṉap poruḷtarum paṭiyeṉṉum mutaṉiliyaṭiyākat tōṉṟiya taṉittamiḻc collākum. itaṉip pratimā eṉṉum vaṭacolliṉ tiripeṉṟal poruntātu. paṭimi eṉṉum ittaṉit tamiḻccol teyva vaṭivattiṉiyum antaṇar cāṉṟōr aruntavattōr mutaliya perumakkaḷatu tava vēṭattiṉiyum uṇartti niṟṟal muṉṉar viḷakkappeṟṟatu. ākavē paṭimiyōṉ2 eṉat tolkāppiyaṉār ciṟappikkappaṭutal koṇṭu avaratu camayam camaṇamē eṉat tuṇitaṟku vaḻiyilli yeṉka.
(2)	mayili nātaruriyiṟ kuṟikkappaṭum akattiyac cūttiraṅkaḷum avinayac cūttiraṅkaḷum kālattāṟ piṟpaṭṭaṉa veṉpatu avaṟṟiṉ col naṭiyāl naṉku viḷaṅkum. iccūttiraṅkaḷi iyaṟṟiya akattiyaṉārum avinayaṉārum tolkāppiyaṉārkkuk kālattāṟ mikamikap piṟpaṭṭavarāvar. ivarkaḷ iyaṟṟiya cūttiraṅkaḷik koṇṭu ivarkaḷatu camayam ituveṉat tuṇitaṟkum iṭamilli. ākavē tolkāppiyaṉār camayam iḵteṉat tuṇitaṟku mayilinātaruriyil mēṟkōḷākak kāṭṭappaṭṭa akattiyac cūttiraṅkaḷum avinayac cūttiraṅkaḷum oru ciṟitum cāṉṟātalilliyeṉka.
(3)	ippoḻutuḷḷa camaṇ camaya nūlkaḷil ācāraṅka cūttiram, curuti kantam iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu, nāṉku, ēḻām attiyayaṉaṅkaḷil uyirkaḷ ulavu taṟkiṭamākiya nilam, nīr, neruppu, kāṟṟu eṉṉum iṭavakiyāl uyirkaḷi nālvakip paṭuttu avvuyirkaḷukku oruciṟitum tuṉpam nērātapaṭi makkaḷ oḻukavēṇṭiya muṟi viritturikkap peṟṟuḷatu. annūliṉ āṟām attiyayaṉattil īraṟivu, mūvaṟivu, nālaṟivu, aiyaṟivu eṉap pakutturikkappaṭum ellāvuyirkaḷukkum iṉpa tuṉpaṅkaḷum accamum uḷavākaliṉ avvuyirkaḷukku ettakiya tīṅkum ceytalākātu eṉṟa aḷavē aṟivuṟuttappaṭṭuḷatu. aṟiviṉ ēṟṟat tāḻvu karutātu ellā vuyirkaḷiyum oppanōkkip pātukāttal vēṇṭumeṉpatum, nilaṉ, nīr, neruppu, kāṟṟu ākiya nāṉkiṭaṅkaḷilum uyirkaḷ viravi vāḻtalāl avaṟṟiṉ iruppiṉi naṉkaṟintu evvuyirkkun tīṅkiṉṟi iyaṅkutal vēṇṭumeṉpatum camaṇ camayak kōṭpāṭukaḷām. ki.pi. āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatākiya ācāraṅka cūttiram uyirkaḷi iṭavakiyāṟ pakutturittatāka. itaṟkup piṉ tōṉṟiya uttarāttiyayaṉam eṉṉum nūl uyirkaḷiṉ poṟiyuṇarvu paṟṟi avaṟṟi aintu vakiyākap pakutturik kiṉṟatu. in nūlkaḷellāvaṟṟukkum kālattāl mikamika muṟpaṭṭatu tolkāppiyamākum. piṟkālattuc camaṇar vakutta uyirp pākupāṭukaḷiṉ nōkkamum tolkāppiyaṉār uyirkaḷi aṟuvakiyākap pakuttaṟkuriya nōkkamum vēṟu vēṟākum. ācāraṅka cūttirattiṟ colliyapaṭi uyirkaḷi iṭavakiyāṟ pakutturikkum muṟi tolkāppiyattiṟ kāṇappaṭavilli. tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟiyapaṭi aṟivu vakiyāl uyirkaḷi āṟu vakiyākap pakutturikkum muṟi paṇṭic camaṇ camaya nūlkaḷil kāṇappaṭavilli.1 iyaṟṟamiḻukku ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟat toṭaṅkiya tolkāppiyaṉār coṟporuḷārāycciyiṉ tuṟi niṉṟu ulakiluḷḷa uyirt tokutikaḷiṉ uṭampukaḷiyum avvuṭampukaḷiṉ vikiya uyirkaḷ mey, vāy, mūkku, kaṇ, cevi maṉam eṉṉum vāyilkaḷip paṭippaṭiyāka muṟiyē peṟṟamiyāl tām peṟṟa appoṟikaḷiṉ vāyilāka muṟiyē ūṟṟuṇarvu, cuviyuṇarvu, nāṟṟavuṇarvu, oḷiyuṇarvu, ōciyuṇarvu, uyattuṇarvu eṉpavaṟṟip paṭippaṭiyē peṟṟuc ciṟakkum iyalpiṉiyum naṉku kaṇṭu, ōraṟivuyir mutal āṟaṟivuyirīṟāka uyirkaṭku aminta vaḷarcciyiṉi āṟu vakiyākap pakuttu viḷakkiyuḷḷār. ippākupāṭu tamiḻukkēyuriya poruḷilak kaṇattiṉ pāṟpaṭuvatallatu oru cārārkkuriya camayak kōṭpāṭṭiṉ pāṟpaṭṭataṉṟeṉka. camaṇ camaya nūlāciriyarkaḷ maṉavuṇarvuṭiya makkaḷiyum, tēvar narakar eṉpavarkaḷiyum, vilaṅku paṟavi mutaliya aiyaṟivuṭiya uyir vakiyuḷ aṭakkuvar.
		vāṉavar makkaḷ narakar vilaṅku puḷ
āti ceviyaṟivō ṭiyaṟi vuyirē	(naṉ-uyiriyal.8)
eṉap pavaṇantiyār kūṟutal kāṇka. uttarāttiya yaṉam eṉṉum camaṇnūlum iṅṅaṉamē uyirkaḷip poṟiyuṇarvu kāraṇamāka aivakiyākap pakutturikkiṉṟatu. ivvintaṟiviṉ vēṟāka āṟāvataṟivākiya maṉa aṟivu oṉṟuṇṭeṉpatu paḻiya camaṇ nūlkaḷil teḷivākak kuṟikkappaṭavilli.
māvum puḷḷum aiyaṟi viṉavē
makkaḷ tāmē āṟaṟi vuyirē
eṉavarum cūttiraṅkaḷāl nāṟkāl vilaṅkum paṟavi mutaliyaṉavum aiyaṟivuyirkaḷeṉṟum makkaḷ āṟaṟivuyireṉap paṭuvareṉṟum tolkāppiyaṉār pakutturittuḷḷār. tolkāppiyatti naṉku payiṉṟu vaḻinūlceyta pavaṇan tiyār, mutaṉūlāciriyarākiya tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟiyavāṟu aiyaṟivuyiriṉ vēṟāka makkaḷi āṟaṟivuyirākat tam nūlil eṭutturittilar. naṉṉūlār camaṇa camayattavarātaliṉ tam camayac cāṉṟōr kūṟiya aivakiyuyirp pākupāṭṭiṉiyē viṭāppiṭiyākak koṇṭu ataṉiyē tamatu nūluḷḷum viḷakkippōntā āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟiyavāṟu makkaḷi āṟaṟivuyireṉap piritturittal camaṇaratu paḻaṅkoḷkiyāka iruntirukkumāṉāl piṟkālattavarākiya naṉṉūlāciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār koḷkiyit tamnūliṟ kūṟātu puṟakkaṇittu viṭutaṟku oru ciṟitum iṭamilliyeṉka. uṇarccivāyilkaḷiṉ kuṟivu mikutikāraṇamāka uyirkaḷi ōraṟivuyir mutal āṟaṟivuyirīṟākap pakutturikkum muṟi tamakku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻc cāṉṟōratu ārāycciyiṉ payaṉāka vakuttamikkappeṟṟa teṉpataṉi nēritiṉuṇarntōr neṟippaṭutatiṉarē eṉavarun toṭarāl tolkāppiyaṉār teḷivu paṭuttiṉami kāṇka.
(4)	paṭṭāhaḷaṅkar eḻutiya kaṉṉaṭa captānu cācaṉattil mēṟkōḷāka eṭuttāḷappeṟṟa vaṭamoḻic culōkam māttiriyiṉ iyalpurippatākum. itaṉi mēṟkōḷāka eṭuttāṇṭa āciriyar piṟkālattavar. ivarāl mēṟkōḷākak kāṭṭappaṭṭa icculōkam innūlāciriyarkkuk kālattāl muṟpaṭṭatu eṉak kūṟutal poruttamuṭiyatēyaṉṟit tolkāppiyattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa toṉmiyuṭiyateṉṟu kūṟutal poruntātu. vaṭamoḻivāṇarkaḷāl matikkattakka paḻiya viyākaraṇa nūlic cērntatu icculōkam eṉak kūṟutaṟ kiṭamillāmi yoṉṟē icculōkam mikamikat toṉmiyuṭiyataṉṟeṉ pataṉi valiyuṟuttum. icculōkat tiṉic camaṇa camaya āciriyaroruvartam nūlil mēṟkōḷāka eṭuttāṇṭamiyāl icculōkatti iyaṟṟiya āciriyarum camaṇarākavē iruttalvēṇṭum eṉattuṇinturittal viyappākavuḷatu. teḷinta civacamayac cāṉṟōrākiya tiruvārūr vittiyanāta nāvalarum civañāṉa cuvāmikaḷum tattam nūlurikaḷil naṉṉūṟ cūttiraṅkaḷi mēṟkōḷāka eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉami koṇṭu apperumakkaḷāl eṭuttāḷap peṟṟa naṉṉūṟ  cūttiraṅkaḷum civacamaya āciriyarāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭaṉavē eṉat tuṇintu kūṟutal poruntumā?
(5)	iyaṟṟamiḻ nūlākiya tolkāppiyattiṟ kūṟappaṭum vitikaḷyāvum tamiḻmoḻikkēyuriyavaṉṟi vaṭamoḻi mutaliya piṟamoḻikaḷi nōkkiyeḻuntaṉa alla. ākavē tol kāppiyattil nilimoḻi varumoḻikaḷāka vittup puṇarkkappaṭum eṇkaḷyāvum tamiḻ eṇkaḷē eṉat teḷitalvēṇṭum. ikkaruttiṉi uḷaṅkoḷātu tolkāppiyap poruḷi ārāya muṟpaṭutalāl uḷavām vaḻukkaḷ pala. āmpal, tāmari, veḷḷam eṉṉum eṇkaḷ āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār kālattukku muṉṉarttōṉṟi avvāciriyar kālattiṟ payiṉṟu vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻeṇkaḷākum. ai am pal eṉa varūum iṟuti eṉavaruñ cūttirattāl ivveṇkaḷukkut tolkāppiyaṉār puṇarcciviti kūṟiyuḷḷār. āciriyar vaṭa coṟkaḷi eṭuttōti ilakkaṇaṅkūṟār1 eṉpa ākaliṉ, āciriyarāl eṭutturik kappaṭṭa ivveṇkaḷ tamiḻeṇkaḷē eṉpatu naṉku teḷiyappaṭum. āmpal, tāmari eṉṉum ivveṇkaḷiyē piṉvantōr kumutam, patumam eṉa muṟiyē moḻipeyarttuk koṇṭaṉar. toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻeṇkaḷiyum iṭikkālattil vantu pukunta vaṭamoḻi eṇkaḷiyum piṅkalanti yeṉṉum nūl piṉvarumāṟu varicippaṭuttut tokutturikkiṉṟatu.
ēkam eṇmaṭaṅku koṇṭatu kōṭi
kōṭi eṇmaṭaṅku koṇṭatu caṅkam
caṅkam eṇmaṭaṅku koṇṭatu vintam
vintam eṇmaṭaṅku koṇṭatu kumutam
kumutam eṇmaṭaṅku koṇṭatu patumam
patumam eṇmaṭaṅku koṇṭatu nāṭu
nāṭeṇ maṭaṅku koṇṭatu camuttiram
camuttiram eṇmaṭaṅku koṇṭatu veḷḷam
immuṟippaṭi nōkkuṅkāl eṭṭān tāṉattatu kōṭi, patiṉintān tāṉattatu caṅkam, irupattiraṇṭān tāṉattatu vintam, irupattoṉpatām tāṉattatu kumutam (āmpal), muppattāṟān tāṉattatu patumam (tāmari), nāṟpattumūṉṟāt tāṉattatu nāṭu (kuvaḷi), aimpatām tāṉattatu camuttiram (neytal), aimpattēḻān tāṉattatu veḷḷam eṉpaṉa pulaṉām. caṅkiṉatu uruvam irukōṇam. ātaliṉ kōṭi kōṭiyic caṅkam eṉṟaṉar. vintattiṉ uruvam mukkōṇam. ātaliṉ kōṭi kōṭi kōṭiyi vintam eṉṟaṉar. āmpaliṉ uruvam nāḷitaḻ. ātaliṉ kōṭi kōṭi kōṭi kōṭiyi āmpal eṉṟaṉar. tāmariyiṉ uruvam aiyitaḻ. ākavē kōṭi kōṭi kōṭi kōṭi kōṭiyit tāmari yeṉṟaṉar. kuvaḷiyiṉ uruvam āṟitaḻ ātaliṉ kōṭi kōṭi kōṭi kōṭi kōṭi kōṭiyik kuvaḷi yeṉṟaṉar. camuttiram ēḻu eṉpavākaliṉ kōṭi kōṭi kōṭi kōṭi kōṭi kōṭi kōṭiyic camuttiram eṉṟaṉar. veḷḷam eṭṭut ticiyilum paravum iyalpiṉatātaliṉ kōṭi kōṭi kōṭi kōṭi kōṭi kōṭi kōṭi kōṭiyi veḷḷam eṉṟaṉar.1
piṅkalanti nūlāciriyar kūṟiyavāṟu camuttiram eṇ maṭaṅku koṇṭa pēreṇ veḷḷamākum. ivveḷḷam eṉṉum pēreṇṇiṉi, itaṉiṉ eṇ maṭaṅku tāḻnta ciṟṟeṇṇākiya camuttiram eṉpataṉ moḻi peyarppākak kūṟutal muṉṉōr nūliṉ muraṇpaṭa moḻitaleṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamām. pākarācāriyar iyaṟṟiya līlāvatiyiṟ kuṟikkappaṭum apjam eṉṉum eṇnūṟu kōṭiyik kuṟippatākum. tamiḻeṇṇākiya tāmari yeṉpatu kōṭi kōṭi kōṭi kōṭi kōṭiyākiya muppattāṟān tāṉattu eṇṇākum. itaṉip patumam eṉṟa collāl piṅkalanti kūṟum. pattāntāṉattu eṇṇākiya apjam eṉpataṉiyum muppattāṟan tāṉattu eṇṇākiya patumam (tāmari) eṉpataṉiyum oṉṟeṉṟal māṟukoḷak kūṟalāmeṉka.
tirumālip paraviya 2-ām paripāṭalil āmpal eṉṉum eṇ eṇṇappaṭṭirukkavum ataṉic camaṇar kaṇṭatākac colvatu ēṟpuṭittaṉṟu. camaṇarkkaḷukkuriya tattuvārttikam cūttirattiṟku upāvāti iyaṟṟiya vōpajña pāṣyam tolkāppiyatti nōkka neṭuṅkālam piṟpaṭṭut tōṉṟiyatākum. camaṇa putta camayaṅkaḷ innāṭṭil tōṉṟuvataṟku muṉṉiruntavar tolkāppiyaṉār eṉpata tamiḻakat toṉmi varalāṟaṟintār aṉivarukkum uṭaṉ pāṭākum. aṅṅaṉamākavum tolkāppiyaṉārkku neṭuṅkālam piṟpaṭṭut tōṉṟiya tattuvārttika cūttira pāṣyattit tolkāppiyattiṉum paḻamiyuṭiyatākap piṟaḻakkoṇṭu annūluriyiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭa kumutam eṉpataṉiyē tol kāppiyaṉār āmpal eṉa moḻi peyarttāreṉṟum ataṉāl avarum camaṇa camayattavarē yeṉṟum kūṟutal evvāṟṟāṉum poruntāteṉka.
(6)	paṇṇit tōṟṟuvikkum ceyyuṭkaḷip paṇṇattiyeṉa vaḻaṅkuvar. pāṭṭiṉkaṇ kalanta poruḷi yuṭiyatākip pāṭṭukkaḷiṉ iyalpiyuṭiyaṉa paṇṇit tōṟṟuvikkuñ ceyyuṭkaḷ eṉpār,
		pāṭṭiṭik kalanta poruḷa vākip
pāṭṭi ṉiyala paṇṇat tiyyē
eṉṟār tolkāppiyaṉār. paṇṇit tōṟṟuvittalāṟ paṇṇattiyeṉap peyarāyiṟṟeṉṟum paṇṇattiyāvaṉa icit tamiḻil ōtappaṭum ciṟṟiciyum pēriciyu mutalāyiṉa eṉṟum ivi ici nūliṉ pāviṉam eṉṟum iḷampūraṇar teḷivāka viḷakkiyuḷḷār. aṅṅaṉamākavum prakñapti eṉṉum vaṭacollē paṇṇattiyeṉap pākatac citivāyiṟ ṟeṉpatu, ivviruvēṟu coṟkaḷukkum aminta poruḷ vēṟupāṭṭiṉi ūṉṟi niṉiyāmiyāl vanta piḻiyākum. prakñapti eṉpataṟkup putti, caṅkētam, oruvakip peṇ teyvam eṉpaṉa poruḷkaḷām. ivi tavira ippeyariṉatākiya ceyyuḷ vaki yoṉṟilli yeṉpatu vaṭanūl vallār aṉivarkkum terinta ceyti, camaṇa nūlkaḷil vaḻaṅkum paṇṇatti yeṉṉum col prakñapti eṉṉum āriya moḻiyiṉ pākatac citivākum. tolkāppiyattiṟ kuṟikkappaṭum paṇṇattiyeṉṉum col icittamiḻc ceyyuḷkaḷik kuṟittu vaḻaṅkum taṉit tamiḻc collākum ivviruvēṟu coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷ vēṟṟumiyiṉic ciṟitum eṇṇippārātu eḻuttoppumi yoṉṟē koṇṭu iraṇṭum oṉṟeṉat tuṇital kūṭātu. camaṇarkaḷāl pākatac citivāka vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa paṇṇatti yeṉṉum coltāṉum pūrvīkac ceyyuḷ vakiyoṉṟiṉ peyarāka vaḻaṅkiyateṉpa taṟkuc ciṟitum ātaravilli. aḵtu eṅṅaṉamāyiṉum camaṇarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiya paṇṇatti yeṉṉum pākatac collum tolkāppiyattil varum paṇṇatti eṉṉun taṉit tamiḻc collum vevvēṟu moḻiyic cārntaṉa eṉṟē tuṇital vēṇṭum.
(7)	aintira viyākaraṇam vitika cāyattavarāl toṉṟu toṭṭu mēṟkoḷḷap peṟṟateṉpatum, puṟac camayattavarākiya camaṇar ivviyākaraṇap payiṟciyi veṟuttotukkiṉami cilappatikārattil kavuntiyaṭikaḷ vāymoḻiyāl naṉku teḷiyappaṭumeṉpatum aintiram niṟinta tolkāppiyaṉ eṉṉun toṭarp poruḷi ārāyum vaḻi viritturikkappaṭṭaṉa.  ākavē aintiram niṟinta tolkāppiyaṉ eṉpatu koṇṭu tolkāppi yaṉāratu camayam camaṇamē eṉṟu kōṭaṟku eḷḷaḷavum teraṭarpilli yeṉka.
itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl camaṇa putta camayaṅkaḷ in nāṭṭil tōṉṟutaṟkup pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ muṟpaṭṭa toṉmik kālattavar tolkāppiyar eṉpatum, tolkāppiyattuḷ camaṇa camayattip paṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷ ciṟitum illiyeṉpatum, māyōṉ, cēyōṉ, varuṇaṉ, vēntaṉ eṉṉum nilat teyva vaḻipāṭukaḷiyuṅ koṟṟavi vaḻipāṭṭiṉiyum ciṟappāka eṭuttōtiya tolkāppiyaṉār, camaṇam, puttam mutaliya puṟac camayak kōṭpāṭukaḷit tam nūluḷ yāṇṭuṅ kuṟippiṭavilli yeṉpatum, avvāciriyar tam kālattil nilaviya tamiḻnāṭṭu vaḻipāṭṭu muṟikaḷiyum cāṉṟōr koḷkikaḷiyum otta matippuṭaṉ eṭutturittu viḷakkutalāl muḻumutaṟ poruḷākiya kaṭavuḷ oṉṟē yeṉṉun teḷivuṭiya koḷkiyiṉareṉpatum naṉku pulaṉākum.
ulakam, uyir eṉpavaṟṟiṟku vēṟākak kaṭavuḷ eṉṉum pēraṟivup poruḷ oṉṟu uṇṭeṉpatum, kaṭavuḷi vaḻipaṭum vāyilākap paḻitīr celvamoṭu vaḻivaḻi ciṟakkum nalvāḻviṉi makkaḷ peṟalām eṉpatum, teyva vaḻi pāṭṭiṉāl viṉip payaṉkaḷi veṉṟu mēṉmēṟ ciṟattalkūṭumeṉpatum tolkāppiyaṉār koṇṭoḻukiya kaṭavuṭ koḷkiyākiya oḻukka neṟiyiṉ muṭinta poruḷākum. immuṭipiṉit teḷivāka ēṟṟuk koḷḷā camayam etuvāyiṉum atu tolkāppiyattukku muṟpaṭṭa toṉmi yuṭiyatāyiṉum āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār karuttukku ēṟṟataṉṟeṉa uṇartalvēṇṭum.
tamiḻ makkaḷ aṉivarkkum payaṉpaṭa iyaṟṟamiḻ mutal nūlākiya tolkāppiyatti iyaṟṟiya āciriyar tol kāppiyaṉār, tammāl iyaṟṟappeṟum tolkāppiyam eṉṟa nūl ellārkkum potuvākap payaṉpaṭutal vēṇṭumeṉṟa karuttiṉālēyē annūṉmukattil tamatu vaḻipaṭu teyvattiṟku vaṇakkam etuvumē kūṟātu viṭṭār. teyvap pulamit tiruvaḷḷuvarum āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār karutti naṉku uṇarntavarākaliṉ avarip piṉpaṟṟiyē tām eccamayattārkkum potuvāka iyaṟṟiya tirukkuṟaḷ nūliṉkaṇ tamatu vaḻipaṭu teyvattiṟku vāḻttuk kūṟāmal ellāc camayattārum ēṟṟukkōṭaṟkuriya potu niliyil vittuk kaṭavuḷ vāḻttuk kūṟiyuḷḷār. ivviruvaratu uḷakkaruttiyum naṉkuṇarnta tolkāppiya uriyāciriyarkaḷākiya iḷampūraṇar, pērāciriyar, cēṉāvariyar, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar mutaliyōrum tirukkuṟaḷuriyāciriyar parimēlaḻakar mutaliyōrum eccamayattārum ēṟṟukkoḷḷum potu niliyil niṉṟu uriyeḻuti yiruppatu aṟiñarkaḷāl viyantu pārāṭṭat takkatākum.
tolkāppiyam nutaliya poruḷ
vaṇpukaḻmūvar taṇpoḻil varippilē vaḻaṅkum centamiḻmoḻiyiṉ ulakavaḻakkiyum ceyyuḷ vaḻakkiyum aṭippaṭiyākakkoṇṭu eḻuttu, col, poruḷ eṉṉum mūvakiyilakkaṇaṅkaḷiyum muṟippaṭa ārāyntu ivaṟṟiṉ iyalpukaḷiyellām tolkāppiyaṉār tām iyaṟṟiya tolkāppiya nūliṉ kaṇṇē tokuttuk kūṟiyuḷḷār. innūl cūttiram, ōttu, paṭalam eṉṉum mūṉṟuṟuppuk kaḷiyuṭiya piṇṭamāka amintuḷatu. āciriyappāvip pōṉṟa yāppilaminta nūṟpā cūttirameṉappaṭum. āciriyappāvukku aṭivariyaṟiyuṇṭu. innūṟpāvukku aṭivariyaṟiyilli. aṭivariyaṟi illāta ceyyuḷvaki āṟu eṉpar tolkāppiyar. avaṟṟuḷ nūṟpāvum oṉṟu. kaṇṇāṭiyiṉakattē etirppaṭṭa poruḷ iṉitu viḷaṅkumāṟu pōṉṟu paṭitta aḷavilēyē ārāyāmaṟ poruḷ eḷitil viḷaṅka iyaṟṟappaṭuvatē nūṟpāvākiya cūttiramākum.


avaṟṟuḷ,
cūttirantāṉē
āṭiniḻaliṉ aṟiyat tōṉṟi
nāṭuta liṉṟip poruḷnaṉi viḷaṅka
yāppiṉuṭ ṭōṉṟa yāttamip patuvē(162)
eṉavarum ceyyuḷiyaṟ cūttiram cūttirattiyalpiṉi naṉku viḷakkutal kāṇka. otta iṉattaṉavākiya maṇikaḷi oru māliyākak kōttamippatupōṉṟu ōriṉamāka varum poruḷkaḷi orucēra iyitturittaṟku iṭamāka amivatu ōttu eṉappaṭum. itaṉi iyal eṉṟa peyarālum vaḻaṅkutaluṇṭu. palvēṟu vakippaṭavarum poruḷellāvaṟṟiṟkum vēṟu vēṟu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟuvatāy aviyellāvaṟṟiyum taṉṉuḷḷē yaṭakki niṟpatu paṭalam eṉappaṭum. cūttiram, ōttu, paṭalam eṉṉum im mūṉṟuṟup piṉiyum aṭakki niṟpatu piṇṭam eṉappaṭum. ivvāṟu mūṉṟuṟuppaṭakkiya piṇṭamāka amintatē ittol kāppiyamākum. itaṉkaṇṇuḷḷa eḻuttatikāram, collatikāram, poruḷatikāram eṉpaṉa paṭalam eṉṉum uṟuppukkaḷām. paṭalattiṉ uḷḷuṟuppāka amintavi ōttukkaḷ. ōttiṉ uḷḷuṟuppāka viḷaṅkuvaṉa cūttiraṅkaḷ, cūttiramākiya uṟuppoṉṟēyuṭiyanūl iṟiyaṉārkaḷaviyal. cūttiram, ōttu ākiya iraṇṭuṟuppuṭiya nūl paṉṉirupaṭalam, cūttiram, ōttu. paṭalam ākiya mūṉṟuṟuppum oruṅkuṭiyanūl tolkāppiyam. immūvaki nūlkaḷiyum muṟiyē ciṟunūl, iṭinūl, perunūl eṉavum vaḻaṅkutaluṇṭu.	
	(tol-cey-iḷam - 165)
tolkāppiyam eṉṉum innūl eḻuttu, col poruḷ ākiya mūṉṟatikāraṅkaḷiyuṭiyatāy ovvō ratikāraṅ kaḷukkum oṉpatoṉpatu iyalkaḷāka irupat tēḻiyalkaḷāl iyaṉṟatu. innūṟ cūttiraṅkaḷ 1595-eṉa iḷampūraṇarum, 1611-eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum vakuttu uriyeḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ.



eḻuttatikāram
eḻuttiṉatu atikārattiyuṭiyatu eḻuttatikāram eṉak kāraṇappeyarāyiṟṟu. atikāram-muṟimi. eḻuttukkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇatti muṟimippaṭa viritturikkum paṭalam eḻuttatikāram eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟuvatāyiṟṟu. eḻuttāvatu makkaḷāṟ pēcappaṭum moḻikku mutaṟkāraṇamākiya oliyākum. tolkāppiyaṉārāl eḻutteṉac collappaṭṭavi akara mutal ṉakara mīṟākavuḷḷa muppatum kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukām, āytam eṉṉum mūṉṟum āka muppattu mūṉṟākum. ivaṟṟiṟku eḻutteṉṉum peyar tamakku neṭuṅkālattiṟku muṉṉē tōṉṟi vaḻaṅkiyateṉpataṉi eḻutteṉappaṭupa, akara mutal ṉakara iṟuvāy muppaḵtu eṉpa eṉavarum cūttirattāl tolkāppiyaṉār kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār.
vilaṅku mutaliya aḵṟiṇiyuyirkaḷiṉṟum makkaḷi vēṟu pirittu uyartiṇi māntarāka uyarttum aṟitaṟ karuviyāka viḷaṅkuvatu moḻi. attakiya moḻikaḷuḷ pēccu vaḻakkoṉṟē peṟṟu eḻutturup peṟātaṉavum uḷḷaṉa. pēccu moḻi ōriṭattum orukālattumē payaṉpaṭum. eḻuttumoḻiyō taṉmi, muṉṉili, paṭarkkiyākiya mūviṭattum iṟappu, nikaḻvu, etirvu ākiya mukkālattum oppap payaṉ taruvatākum. paṉiyōlikaḷilum kallilum piṟa poruḷkaḷilum eṇṇaṅkaḷi eḻuttāṟ poṟittuvikkum vaḻakkam pallāyira āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē nam tamiḻ makkaḷāl kikkoḷḷappeṟṟu varukiṉṟatu. cellun tēettup peyarmaruṅ kaṟimār, kallēṟin teḻutiya nallari marāatta, kaṭavuḷōṅkiya kāṭēcu kavali (malipaṭu - 394-395) eṉavum, peyarum pīṭum eḻuti yatartoṟum, pīlicūṭṭiya piṟaṅkunili naṭukal (akam - 131) eṉavum peṟikaṇṭaḻikkum āvaṇamākkaḷiṉ (akam - 77) eṉavum varum caṅka ilakkiyat toṭarkaḷāl icceyti pulaṉātal kāṇalām.
makkaḷ tammāṟ pēcappaṭum moḻiyilaminta olikaḷit taṉittaṉiyākap pirittaṟiyum uṇarvupeṟṟa piṉṉarttāṉ karutturuvākiya avvolikaḷik kaṭpulaṉāka varivaṭivil eḻututalkūṭum. aṟiñarkaḷatu naṉmuyaṟciyāl olikaḷukkuriya varivaṭivaṅka ḷaminta piṉputāṉ avvolikaḷukku eḻut teṉṉuṅ kāraṇappeyar vaḻaṅkiyiruttal vēṇṭum. iv vuṇmi eḻutappaṭutaliṉ eḻuttē1 eṉavarum paḻiya cūttirat toṭarāl aṟivuṟuttappaṭṭami kāṇka.
meyyeḻuttukkaḷ puḷḷipeṟutalum ekara okarak kuṟilkaḷ puḷḷipeṟutalum kuṟṟiyalukaram puḷḷipeṟutalum makarakkuṟukkam meykkuriya mēṟpuḷḷiyōṭu uḷḷēyoru puḷḷipeṟutalum ākiya eḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟit tolkāppiyaṉār tam nūlakattu viḷakkiyuḷḷār. ākavē tolkāppiyaṉārkku neṭuṅkālattiṟku muṉpē tamiḻ muṉṉōr akara mutal ṉakara viṟuvāyuḷḷa eḻuttukkaḷukkuriya varivaṭivaṅkaḷi yamittut tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkiṉi oḻuṅku ceytuḷḷami naṉku teḷiyappaṭum.
eḻuttilakkaṇa vaki
tolkāppiyaṉār tām kūṟa eṭuttukkoṇṭa eḻut tilakkaṇattiṉi nūṉmarapu, moḻimarapu, piṟappiyal, puṇariyal, tokimarapu, urupiyal, uyirmayaṅkiyal, puḷḷimayaṅkiyal, kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal eṉa oṉpatiyalkaḷāṉ uṇarttu kiṉṟār. eḻuttu iṉitteṉṟalum, avi iṉṉa peyariṉa eṉṟalum iṉṉa muṟimiyaveṉṟalum, iṉṉa aḷaviṉa veṉṟalum, iṉṉa piṟappiṉaveṉṟalum, iṉṉa puṇarcciya veṉṟalum, iṉṉa vaṭiviṉaveṉṟalum, iṉṉa taṉmiya veṉṟalum eṉa eṭṭu vakiyākavum, eḻuttiṉatu uṇmit taṉmi, kuṟivu, kūṭṭam, pirivu, mayakkam, moḻiyākkam. nili iṉam, niliyiṟṟeṉṟal, niliyāteṉṟal, niliyiṟṟum nili yātumeṉṟal eṉa iṉṉōraṉṉa eṭṭiṟanta palavakiyākavum eḻuttilakkaṇattiṉi āciriyar vakip paṭuttuṇarttiṉāreṉavum ivvatikārattiṟ kūṟappaṭum ivvilakkaṇaṅkaḷ yāvum karuviyum ceykiyum eṉa iru vakiyāy aṭaṅkumeṉavum avviraṇṭaṉuṭ karuviyi nūṉmarapu mutaliya nāṉkiyalkaḷālum ceykiyit toki marapu mutaliya aintiyalkaḷālum tolkāppiyaṉār uṇarttiṉā reṉavum uriyāciriyar iḷampūraṇa aṭikaḷ vakippaṭuttu viḷakkiyuḷḷār.



1. nūṉmarapu
ivvatikārattāṟ collappaṭum eḻuttilakkaṇattiṉi ōrāṟṟāṟ ṟokuttuṇarttutaliṉ nūṉmarapeṉṉum peyarttu eṉa iḷampūraṇarum, ittolkāppiyameṉum nūṟku marapān tuṇikku vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟit tokut tuṇarttutaliṉ nūṉ marapeṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum, aḵtāvatu nūliṉatu marapu paṟṟiya peyar kūṟutal..................... malikaṭalyāṟu eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattu ulakamarapu paṟṟiya peyar pōlātu īṇṭuk kūṟappaṭum eḻuttu, kuṟil, neṭil, uyir, mey eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattup peyarkaḷ nūliṉkaṇ āḷutaṟporuṭṭu mutaṉūlāciriyaṉāṟ ceytukoḷḷap paṭṭamiyiṉ ivi nūṉmarapu paṟṟiya peyarāyiṉa eṉac civañāṉamuṉivarum ivviyaliṉ peyarkkāraṇam kūṟiṉār. ivviyaluṭ kūṟappaṭum eḻuttukkaḷiṉ peyar mutaliyaṉa aṉittum tolkāppiyaṉārkku muṟkālattavarāṉa paṇṭit tamiḻc cāṉṟōr nūlkaḷiṟ collappaṭṭa eḻuttiyal marapukaḷāy innūlil āciriyarāl eṭuttāḷap paṭṭaṉavām. eṉpa, pulavar, moḻipa, eṉmaṉār pulavar eṉṟāṅku muṉṉiyōr karuttāka ivviyalil varuṅ kuṟiyīṭukaḷi āciriyar eṭutturittalāl ivvuṇmi viḷaṅkum. ivviyaliṟ kūṟappaṭum ilakkaṇam moḻiyiṭi eḻuttiṟkaṉṟit taṉiniṉṟa eḻuttiṟkuriyatākum.
kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, āytam eṉṟa mūṉṟum yātāyiṉum oru collic cārntuvariṉallatu taṉiyeḻuttāka olittu niṟkum iyalpuṭiyaṉa alla. ivaṟṟiṉ iyalpiṉi naṉkaṟinta tamiḻ muṉṉōr immūṉṟiṉiyum cārpeḻutteṉap peyariṭṭu vaḻaṅkiṉārkaḷ. cārntuvaraṉ marapiṉmūṉṟu eṉavum, cārntu variṉallatu tamakkiyalpilaveṉat tērntu veḷippaṭutta ēṉi mūṉṟum eṉavum varun toṭarkaḷāl tolkāppiyaṉār ivaṟṟiṉ iyalpiṉit teḷivāka viḷakkukiṉṟār.
taṉittiyaṅkum iyalpuṭiyatu uyir. avviyalpiṉṟi uyiriṉāl iyakkappaṭuvatu mey. akara mutal aukāra mīṟāka vuḷḷa paṉṉiraṇṭeḻuttum taṉittiyaṅkum āṟṟaluṭimiyāl uyireṉappaṭṭaṉa. kakara mutal ṉakara mīṟākavuḷḷa patiṉeṭ ṭeḻuttukkaḷum taṉittiyaṅku miyalpiṉṟi akara mutaliya uyirkaḷāl iyakkappaṭutaliṉ meyyeṉappaṭṭaṉa. uyirvaḻiyāyaṭaṅki ataṉatu viḷakkam peṟṟu niṟkum uṭampip pōṉṟu meyyeḻuttukkaḷum tammēl ēṟiya uyireḻuttiṉ māttirikkuḷ aṭaṅki ataṉ oliyōṭu otticippaṉavākum. iṅṅaṉam meyyum uyirum kūṭiyolikkum eḻuttoliyiṉi uyirmey eṉṟa niṟiyuvamap peyarāl vaḻaṅkutal paḻiya tamiḻ marapākum.
ivviyalil 33 cūttiraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. eḻuttiṉṉa teṉpatum, ataṉ vakiyum, eḻuttukkaḷ peṟum māttiriyum, avaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṉ vaṭivam, kuṟṟeḻuttu, neṭṭeḻuttu, uyir, mey, uyirmey, valleḻuttu, melleḻuttu, iṭiyeḻuttu, eṉa avi peṟum peyarkaḷum, mey taṉṉoṭum piṟitoṭum kūṭiyolikkum meymayakkamum, kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷuḷ a i u eṉpavaṟṟukkuc cuṭṭeṉṉuṅ kuṟiyum neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷuḷ ā ē ō eṉpavaṟṟukku viṉāveṉṉuṅ kuṟiyum, eḻuttukkaḷ muṟkūṟiya māttiriyiṉ nīṇṭolikku miṭamum ivviyalil uṇarttappaṭṭaṉa.


2. moḻi marapu
moḻikaḷukku eḻuttāṉvarum marapuṇarttiṉamiyiṉ moḻimarapeṉappaṭṭatu eṉa iḷampūraṇarum eḻuttāṉām moḻiyatu marapuṇarttiṉamiyiṉ ivvōttu moḻimarapeṉak kāraṇappeyarttāyiṟṟu eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum ivviyaliṉ peyarkkāraṇaṅ kūṟiṉar. ivviyaluṭ kūṟukiṉṟa ilakkaṇam taṉiniṉṟa eḻuttiṟkaṉṟi moḻiyiṭi yeḻuttiṟku eṉa iḷampūraṇar kūṟuvar. eṉavē eḻuttukkaḷi moḻippaṭut ticikkuṅkāl moḻiyil niṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷukku uḷavām iyalpiṉi yuṇarttuvatu ivviyaleṉpatu peṟappaṭum. ivviyaliṉ cūttiraṅkaḷ 49.
moḻiyiṉic cārntu olittaliyē iyalpākavuṭiya cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟiṟkum avitām kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiya lukaram āytam eṉa muṉṉaruritta muṟiyē ivviyalil ilakkaṇam kūṟukiṉṟār. ammūṉṟum oru moḻi puṇarmoḻi ākiya īriṭattum varumeṉakkoṇṭu avaṟṟi orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalikaram, puṇarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalikaram, orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram, puṇarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram, oru moḻiyāytam, puṇar moḻiyāytam eṉa āṟākappirittu muṟiyē 1-mutal 6-vari yuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷil uṇartti 7-ām cūttirattil avvoru moḻi yāytattiṟku ōr ilakkaṇamuṅ kūṟiyuḷḷār. ivi moḻiyiṭic cārttiyuṇarappaṭuvaṉa ākalāṉum oruvāṟṟāṉ eḻutteṉak koḷḷappaṭuvaṉa ākalāṉum nūṉ marapiyaṭuttu moḻimarapiṉ mutaṟkaṇ viḷakkappaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟiyaṭuttu uyiraḷapeṭi, moḻi yākkam, meykaḷiṉ iyakkam, īroṟ ṟuṭaṉili, makarak kuṟukkam, eḻuttuppōli, aikārak kuṟukkam, moḻi mutaleḻuttukkaḷ, moḻikkīṟāmeḻuttukkaḷ, eṉpaṉa uṇarttappaṭṭaṉa. iviyellām taṉi yeḻuttukkaḷilaṉṟi moḻikaḷil vittu aṟintukoḷḷutaṟkuriyaṉavātaliṉ ivviyaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.



3. piṟappiyal
akara mutal ṉakaraviṟutiyākavuḷḷa mutaleḻuttukkaḷ muppatiyum nūṉmarapilum cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟiyum moḻi marapilum vittuṇarttiya āciriyar. ammuppattu mūṉṟeḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappu muṟiyiṉi ivviyalāl uṇarttukiṉṟār. ataṉāl ivviyal piṟappiyaleṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu.
untiyilirunteḻukiṉṟa kāṟṟāṉatu tali, miṭaṟu, neñcu eṉṉum mūṉṟaṭaṅkaḷilum nilipeṟṟu avaṟṟuṭaṉ pal, utaṭu, nā, mūkku, aṇṇam (mēlvāy) ākiya uṟuppukkaḷ tammiṟ porunti amitipeṟa vēṟuvēṟuruvākiya eḻuttukkaḷāyp piṟantu pulappaṭa vaḻaṅkutalē eḻuttukkaḷuk kuriya potuvākiya piṟappu muṟiyākum itaṉi ivviyaliṉ mutaṟ cūttirattu āciriyar viritturikkiṉṟār. uyireḻuttukkaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭum tattam nilitiriyātu miṭaṟṟu vaḷiyāṟ piṟakkum. eṉavē uyireḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappiṭam miṭaṟu eṉpatu pulaṉām. ōriṭattē oru muyaṟciyāṟ palaveḻuttukkaḷ piṟakkumeṉap potuvakiyāṟ kūṟiṉum avveḻuttuk kaḷiṭiyē nuṇṇiya vēṟupāṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉaveṉa āciriyar teḷivupaṭuttukiṉṟār.
ivviyaliṉkaṇ 7-mutal 11-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷil kaṅa, caña, ṭaṇa, tana, ākiya meyyeḻuttukkaḷukku neṭuṅ kaṇakku muṟi paṟṟip piṟappuk kūṟukiṉṟār. 12 mutal nāvatikāram paṟṟip piṟappukkūṟat toṭaṅki ṟaṉa, raḻa, laḷa, pama, vaya, eṉṉum meyyeḻuttukkaḷukkup piṟappuk kūṟiyuḷḷār. iṅkup piṟappukkūṟiya meyyeḻuttukkaḷuḷ melleḻut tāṟum tattam piṟappiṉatu ākkañ colliya iṭattē nili peṟṟaṉavāyiṉum avi mūkkiṉkaṇ uḷatākiya kāṟṟōciyāl iyipupeṟat tōṉṟiyolippaṉavām. ivvāṟu melleḻuttāṟukkum mūkkuvaḷiyiṉ toṭarpuṭimiyiṉic collavē iṭiyeḻuttāṟum miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyiyum valleḻuttāṟum talivaḷiyiyum peṟṟu olippaṉaveṉak koṇṭār iḷampūraṇar.
mutaleḻuttukkaḷic cārntutōṟṟiṉallatu taṉit tolikkum iyalpillātaṉa eṉa ārāyntu veḷippaṭuttap paṭṭa cārpeḻuttukkaḷ mūṉṟum tattamakkuc cārpākiya muta leḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappiṭattiyē tamakkuriya piṟappiṭamākap porunti icippaṉavām. ivvāṟu eḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟap puṇarttiya tolkāppiyaṉār avveḻuttukkaḷ peṟum māttiriyiṉik kuṟittukkūṟum viḷakkam ivaṇ karutattakuva tākum. ellāveḻuttukkaḷiyum veḷippaṭac collappaṭṭa iṭattiṉkaṇ eḻuṅkāṟṟiṉālē olikkumiṭattu avveḻuttukkaḷ yāvum koppūḻaṭiyilirunteḻuṅ kāṟṟāṉatu taliyaḷavuñ ceṉṟu mīṇṭum neñciṉkaṇ nilipeṟutalākiya tiritaruṅ kūṟu pāṭṭiṉiyuṭiyaṉa. ivveḻuttukkaḷukku iṅṅaṉam uṟaḻcci vārattiṉāl uḷatām akatteḻuvaḷiyiciyiṉi nuṭpamāka ārāyntu māttiri variyaṟiyāl aḷantu koḷḷutal antaṇaratu maṟinūṟ kaṇṇatākiya muṟiyākum. ammuṟiyiṉi innūliṟ collātu ellārkkum pulaṉākap puṟattē veḷippaṭṭicikkum meyterivaḷiyiciyākiya eḻuttuk kaḷukkē yāṉ iṅku māttiri kūṟiṉēṉ eṉpatu āciriyar kūṟiya viḷakkamākum. untiyil eḻuntakāṟṟu muṉṉart talikkaṭ ceṉṟu piṉṉar miṭaṟṟilēvantu ataṉpiṉṉar neñcilē niṟṟal uṟaḻccivāram eṉappaṭum. mūlātārattilirun teḻuṅ kāṟṟōci akatteḻuvaḷiyici yeṉappaṭṭatu. ellārkkum eḻutturuvam naṉku pulaṉāka vāyiliruntu puṟattē veḷippaṭ ṭicikkum kāṟṟiṉālākiya eḻuttōciyē meyterivaḷi yici yeṉappaṭum. antaṇar maṟiyiṟ kūṟumāṟu akat teḻuvaḷiyiciyākiya eḻuttukkaḷukku aḷapukūṟiṉ aḵtu ellārkkum naṉṟāka viḷaṅkāteṉak karutiya tolkāppiyaṉār puṟatteḻunticikkum meyterivaḷi yiciyākiya eḻuttukkaḷukkē māttiri kūṟuvārāyiṉar.
untuyileḻunta kāṟṟiṉik kūṟupaṭutti māttiri kūṭṭikkoḷḷutalum, mūlātāram mutalākak kāṟṟeḻumāṟu kūṟutalum, antaṇar maṟikku uḷateṉṟu kūṟiya ivvāciriyar, avar matam paṟṟip peṟuvatōr payaṉiṉṟeṉa ic cūttirattāl uyttuṇara vittaliṉ, iccūttiram piṟaṉ kōṭkūṟal eṉṉum uttikkiṉam eṉṟār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.



4. puṇariyal
moḻikaḷ tammiṟ puṇartaṟkuriya karuviyiṉ iyalpiṉik kūṟutaliṉ itu puṇariyal eṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. ivviyaliṟ kūṟappaṭum vitikaḷ piṉvarum iyalkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭum ceykipaṟṟiya vitikaḷukkup payaṉpaṭutaliṉ karuvikaḷeṉap paṭṭaṉa.
tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkiluḷḷa muppattu mūṉṟeḻuttukkaḷuḷ irupattiraṇṭeḻuttukkaḷ moḻikku mutalāmeṉavum, irupattu nāṉkeḻuttukkaḷ moḻikku īṟāmeṉavum, ellā moḻikaḷukkum iṟutiyum mutalumāvaṉa mey, uyir eṉṉum ivviruvakiyeḻuttukkaḷeṉavum, moḻiyiṟutiyil niṉṟa meyyellām puḷḷipeṟṟu niṟkumeṉavum, moḻiyiṟutiyi luḷḷa kuṟṟiyalukaramum meyyiṉ taṉmiyiyuṭiyatā meṉavum, moḻiyīṟāy niṉṟa uyirmey uyirīṟṟiṉ taṉmiyiyuṭiyatā meṉavum ivviyalil 1-mutal 4-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
nilimoḻiyi niṟuttacol eṉṟum varumoḻiyik kuṟittuvarukiḷaviyeṉṟum tolkāppiyaṉār vaḻaṅkuvar. niṟutta colliṉ īṟṟeḻuttōṭu kuṟittuvarukiḷaviyiṉ mutalaleḻuttup porunta avvirumoḻikaḷum iyintu varutalē puṇarcciyeṉappaṭum. appuṇarcci uyirīṟṟuc col muṉ uyir varumiṭam, uyirīṟṟuc colmuṉ meyvarumiṭam, meyyīṟṟuc colmuṉ uyir varumiṭam, meyyīṟṟuc colmuṉ mey varumiṭam eṉa eḻuttuvakiyāl nāṉku vakippaṭum; peyaroṭu peyar, peyaroṭu toḻil, toḻiloṭu peyar, toḻiloṭu toḻil eṉac col vakiyāl nāṉkākum. moḻikaḷ puṇaruṅkāl iṭiniṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷ oṉṟu maṟṟeṉṟākat tiritali meypiṟitātal eṉṟum, avviṭattup putiyaveḻuttut toṉṟutali mikutal eṉṟum, aṅku muṉṉirunta eḻuttuk keṭutalik kuṉṟal eṉṟum, ivvēṟu pāṭetuvumiṉṟi avvirumoḻikaḷum muṉṉuḷḷavāṟu puṇarntu niṟṟali iyalpeṉṟum, innāṉkiṉiyum moḻipuṇariyalpu eṉṟum āciriyar kuṟiyiṭṭu vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār. niṟuttacollum kuṟittu varukiḷaviyum taṉittaṉiyē aṭimoḻi peṟṟuvariṉum nilimoḻi varumoḻiyākap puṇartaṟku uriyaṉavām. muṉ piṉṉāka māṟi niṉṟa marūu moḻikaḷum nilimoḻi varumoḻiyākap puṇarkkappaṭutaluṇṭu.
moḻippuṇarcci vēṟṟumip puṇarcciyeṉavum alvaḻip puṇarcciyeṉavum poruḷ vakiyāl irutiṟappaṭum ivaṟṟuḷ vēṟṟumip puṇarcciyi vēṟṟumi kuṟitta puṇarmoḻi niliyeṉṟum alvaḻip puṇarcciyi vēṟṟumiyalvaḻip puṇarmoḻi niliyeṉṟu āciriyar vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār. ivviruvakip puṇarmoḻikaḷum eḻuttu mikutalum cāriyi mikutalum ākiya irutiṟattālum naṭappaṉa. vēṟṟumi yurupāvaṉa ai, oṭu, ku, iṉ, atu, kaṇ eṉṉum āṟām eṉat tolkāppiyaṉarkku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻiya ṉūlār vakippaṭuttiyuḷḷārkaḷ. vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷ puṇarum nilimikkaṇ peyariṉ piṉṉiṭattē niṟṟaṟkuriyaṉa. urupēṟkum peyarkaḷ uyartiṇip peyar, aḵṟiṇip peyar eṉa iruvakippaṭum. avi vēṟṟumiyurupōṭu poruntuṅkāl peyarkkum vēṟṟumiyurupiṟkumiṭiyē cāriyi mikum. cāriyikaḷāvaṉa iṉ, vaṟṟu, attu, am, oṉ, āṉ, akku, ikku, aṉ eṉavarum ivvoṉpatum ivipōlvaṉa piṟavum ām. iṉ cāriyi ikaraṅkeṭṭu ṉakaramey maṭṭum niṟṟalum, ṉakaraṅ keṭṭu ikaravuyir maṭṭum niṟṟalum, ṉakaram ṟakaramākat tirintu niṟṟalum ākiya mūṉṟu tiripuṭiyateṉṟum, vaṟṟuc cāriyi mutaṟkaṇuḷḷa vakaramey keṭa aṟṟu eṉattiriyumeṉṟum, iṉ, oṉ, āṉ, aṉ eṉṉum iccāriyikaḷiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramāyt tiritaluṇṭeṉṟum, akaravīṟṟuc colmuṉ varum attuccāriyi akaraṅkeṭa ttu eṉa niṟkumeṉṟum, ikara aikāra vīṟṟuc colmuṉvarum ikkuc cāriyi ikaraṅkeṭa kku eṉa niṟkumeṉṟum, valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varuṅkāl akkuc cāriyi iṟutik kuṟṟiyalukaramum ataṉālūrappaṭṭa kakarameyyum akkakara meyyiṉmēl niṉṟa kakarameyyum orucērakkeṭa a eṉat tirintu niṟkumeṉṟum, kacata varu moḻiyāy varuṅkālattu am cāriyiyiṉ makaram muṟiyē ṅa ña na eṉat tiritalum, melleḻuttum iṭiyeḻuttum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattuk keṭutalumākiya nilimit teṉṟum, coṟkaḷ peyarun toḻilumāyp pirintum peyarum peyarumāykkūṭiyum icippa vēṟṟumiyurupu virintu niṟku miṭattum maṟintu niṟkumiṭattum moḻikaḷip pirittuk kāṇuṅkāl peyarkkum vēṟṟumiyurupiṟku miṭiyē vantu niṟṟal cāriyiyiṉ iyalpeṉṟum cāriyiyiṉ ilakkaṇat tiṉi āciriyar teḷivāka variyaṟuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷar.
coṟkaḷi nilimoḻi varumoḻiyākap pirittuk kāṇum muṟiyiṉit coṟcitar maruṅku eṉavum peyar cāriyi urupu mutaliya colluṟuppukkaḷ oṉṟaṉpiṉṉoṉṟāka oṭṭi naṭattaṟkuriya moḻivaḻakkiṉi oṭṭutaṟkoḻukiya vaḻakku eṉavum āciriyar kuṟiyiṭṭu vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār. niṟutta colluṅ kuṟittu varukiḷaviyumākap pirittaṟkēṟṟavāṟu oṭṭi nillātu, nilākkatir nilāmuṟṟam eṉṟāṟ pōla uṭaṅkiyintu niṟkum puṇarmoḻikaḷ oṭṭutaṟkoḻukiya vaḻakkiṉa allaveṉavuṅ ākavē avi cāriyi peṟā veṉavum iḷampūraṇar kūṟiya viḷakkam ivaṇ cintittuṇarat takuvatām.
kāram, karam, kāṉ eṉpaṉa eḻuttiṉ cāriyikaḷām. avaṟṟuḷ karam, kāṉ eṉṉum iru cāriyikaḷiyum neṭṭeḻuttup peṟutalilli. kuṟṟeḻuttu mēṟkūṟiya mūṉṟu cāriyikaḷiyum peṟum. neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷil ai, au eṉṉumiraṇṭum kāṉ cāriyiyum peṟum. eṉa ivviyalil iyipuṭimi karuti eḻuttuk cāriyikaḷum uṭaṉ kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
meyyīṟṟiṉmuṉ uyirmutaṉ moḻi varuṅkāl varumoḻi mutaliluḷḷa uyir taṉittu nillātu; meykaḷukkuriya puḷḷi peṟutalākiya avviyalpiṉik keṭuttu nilimoḻi yīṟṟiluḷḷa ammeyyuṭaṉ kūṭi niṟkum. iṅṅaṉam kūṭiya uyir pirintu nīṅkiyavaḻi nilimoḻiyīṟṟiluḷḷa mey mīṇṭum taṉ paḻiya vaṭivākiya puḷḷiyip peṟum. ivviyalpiṉi ivviyaliṉ 36, 37-ām cūttiraṅkaḷāl  āciriyar kuṟippiṭuvar. kuṟṟiyalukaramum meyyīṟupōlun taṉmiyattu eṉa ivviyaliṉ mūṉṟām cūttirattāl āciriyar māṭṭēṟṟik kūṟiyuḷḷār. immāṭṭēṟu puḷḷi peṟutalum uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭuttalumākiya meyyiṉ taṉmikaḷuḷ puḷḷi peṟutali vilakki uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭuttalākiya avvaḷavukkuc cellutaliṉ immāṭṭēṟu orupuṭiccēṟa leṉavuṇarka eṉa uriyāciriyar viḷakkaṅ kūṟuvar. kuṟṟiyalukaramum aṟṟeṉa moḻipa eṉa muṉṉar māṭṭēṟu kūṟiṉamiyāl meyyīṟṟiṉmuṉ uyir taṉittu nillātavāṟu pōṉṟu kuṟṟiyalukaravīṟṟiṉ muṉṉum uyir taṉittu nillātu akkuṟṟiyalukarattōṭu porunti niṟkum eṉat tol kāppiyaṉār uyttuṇara vittami peṟappaṭum.
uyirīṟṟiṉmuṉ uyirmutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu uyirōṭu uyirkkuk kalantu niṟkum iyalpiṉmiyāl iru moḻikaḷum oṭṭi nillātu viṭṭicippaṉavām. nilimoḻiyīṟṟilum varu moḻi mutalilum niṟkum avviraṇṭuyirkaḷum uṭampaṭutaṟ poruṭṭu avaṟṟiṭiyē yakara vakaraṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu uṭampaṭu meyyāy varum. uṭampaṭāta iraṇṭuyirkaḷum uṭampaṭutaṟ poruṭṭu iṭiyē tōṉṟum mey uṭampaṭumey eṉa vaḻaṅ kappaṭum. uṭaṉpāṭu, uṭampāṭu eṉa maruvi vaḻaṅkiyatu varumuyirkku uṭampāka aṭukkum mey uṭampaṭumey eṉapporuḷ kōṭalumuṇṭu. ellā moḻikkum iṟutiyum mutalum uyir varumvaḻi avaṟṟiṭiyē uṭampaṭumey peṟutali vilakkār eṉat tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟutalāl avar kālattu uyirīṟṟiṉ muṉ uyirmutaṉmoḻi vantu puṇaruṅkāl uṭampaṭumey peṟṟē varavēṇṭumeṉṉum variyaṟiyilli yeṉpatu naṉku pulaṉākum. uṭampaṭumeyyāka varutaṟkuriya eḻuttukkaḷ iviyeṉat tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟātu pōyiṉum avariyaṟṟiya iyaṟṟamiḻ nūlākiya it tolkāppiyattilum itaṟkuppiṉ tōṉṟiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷellāvaṟṟilum yakara vakaraṅkaḷē uṭampaṭumeyyāka āḷappeṟṟuḷḷami kāṇalām. uṭampaṭu meyyē yakāra vakāram, uyir mutaṉ moḻi varūuṅkāliyāṉa eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kāṭṭiya paḻañcūttiram yakara vakaraṅkaḷē uṭampaṭumeyyāy varutaṟkuriyaṉa eṉpatit teḷivāka viḷakkutal kāṇka. uyirkaḷuḷ ikara īkāra aikāra vīṟukaḷ yakara vuṭampaṭumey koḷḷum eṉavum, ēkāravīṟu yakara vakaraṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟi uṭampaṭumeyyākap peṟṟuvarum eṉavum, ēṉiya vuyirīṟukaḷ yāvum vakara vuṭampaṭu meyyē peṟum eṉavum iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum vakipeṟa viḷakkiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
eḻuttāl oṉṟupōlat tōṉṟic collāl vēṟu paṭṭup poruḷ viḷaṅki niṟkum puṇarmoḻikaḷ ōci vēṟu pāṭṭaṟ pirintu puṇarcci vēṟupaṭutal colnaṭiyiṉ nili peṟṟa paṇpākum. ivvāṟu varum puṇarmoḻikaḷ kuṟippiṉāluṇarum poruḷiyuṭiyaṉa. eḻuttu vakiyāl ittaṉmiya eṉat teḷivāka vuṇarum muṟimiyi yuṭiyaṉa alla. eṭuttukkāṭṭākac cempoṉ patiṉṟoṭi eṉṉum puṇar moḻi poṉṉippaṟṟip pēcciṉkaṇ eṭuttāḷap paṭṭāl cempoṉ + patiṉṟoṭi eṉap pirintum, cempik kuṟittu nikaḻum pēccil eṭuttāḷappaṭṭāl cempu + oṉpatiṉṟoṭi eṉap pirintum ōci vēṟupaṭṭu vēṟuvēṟu poruḷuṇartti niṟṟal kāṇalām. ivaṟṟiṉ iyalpiṉi ivviyaliṉ īṟṟiluḷḷa iraṇṭu cūttiraṅkaḷālum āciriyar aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟār. eṉavē avar kālattu iṅṅaṉam nuṇṇitākap poruḷuṇarttum puṇarmoḻikaḷ peruka vaḻaṅkiṉami naṉku pulaṉākum.



5. toki marapu
uyiriyum meyyiyum iṟutiyākavuṭiya coṟkaḷip piṉvarum iyalkaḷil īṟukaḷtōṟum taṉittaṉiyē virittōti muṭikkak karutiya āciriyar, pala vīṟukaḷukkum potuvāṉa vitikaḷi ivviyalil ovvōr cūttiraṅkaḷāl tokuttu muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟār. ataṉāl ivviyal tokimarapu eṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. nilimoḻi varumoḻip puṇarccikaṭpaṭum ilakkaṇaṅkaḷāyt toṉṟutoṭṭu varum ilakkaṇa marapukaḷit tokuttuṇarttutaliṉ tokimara peṉap paṭṭatu eṉiṉumamiyum. nilimoḻiyum varumoḻiyum mēl puṇariyaliṟ kūṟiya karuvikaḷāl tokkuppuṇarum ceyki kūṟuvatu ivviyalātaliṉ itu puṇariyalōṭu iyipuṭit tāyiṟṟu.
ivviyal muppatu cūttiraṅkaḷiyuṭiyatu. itaṉkaṇ alvaḻiyum vēṟṟumiyumākiya iruvaḻiyilum kacatapa eṉṉum valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varuṅkāl mikutaṟkuriya melleḻuttukkaḷ muṟiyē ṅa ña na ma eṉpaṉavām eṉṉum varumoḻik karuviyum, irupattunāṉkīṟṟiṉ muṉṉum vaṉkaṇa moḻinta kaṇaṅkaḷukku iruvaḻiyum varumoḻimuṭipum, ṇakara ṉakara vīṟṟuc coṟkaḷukku iruvaḻiyum nilimoḻi muṭipum, lakara, ṉakara vīṟukaḷiṉ muṉṉum ṇakara ḷakara vīṟukaḷiṉ muṉṉum varum takara nakaraṅkaḷ muṟiyē ṟakara ṉakaraṅkaḷākavum ṭakara ṇakaraṅkaḷākavum tiriyu meṉṉum varumoḻik karuviyum, uyiriyum meyyiyum iṟutiyākavuṭiya muṉṉili viṉic coṟkaḷ valliṉa mutaṉmoḻi varuṅkāl iyalpum uṟaḻcciyu mākiya irunilimiyiyuṭiyaṉa veṉpatum, aukārattiyum ñanamava eṉṉum meykaḷiyum kuṟṟiyalukarattiyum īṟākavuṭiya muṉṉili viṉiccoṟkaḷ muṟkūṟiya muṭipiṟku muṟṟum poruntuvaṉa alla eṉpatum, uyiriyum meyyiyum īṟākavuṭiya uyar tiṇip peyarkaḷ nāṉkukaṇattum iruvaḻiyum iyalpāy muṭiyumeṉpatum, avaṟṟuḷ ikaravīṟṟu uyartiṇip peyar tirintu muṭiyumiṭaṉumuṇ ṭeṉpatum, aḵṟiṇi viravu peyar iyalpāy muṭivaṉavumuḷa veṉpatum, mūṉṟām vēṟṟumip puṇarcciyilum iraṇṭām vēṟṟumip puṇarcciyilum uḷavākun tiripukaḷum, ikara aikāravīṟṟup peyarkaḷukku alvaḻi muṭipum, ikara aikāravīṟṟuḷ ēḻām vēṟṟumiyiṭap poruḷuṇara niṉṟa iṭiccol muṭipum, neṭṭeḻuttiṉ piṉṉiṉṟa meyyīṟu keṭutal kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ piṉṉiṉṟa iṟutimey iraṭṭittal ākiya nilimoḻikkaru viyum, ivaṟṟiṟku urupiyali nōkkiyatōr, varumoḻik karuviyum, ukaramoṭu puṇarum meyyīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ yakaramum uyirum varumoḻiyāy variṉ avvukaram peṟātu iyalpām eṉṉum varumoḻic ceykiyum aḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum eṇṇuppeyarum tammiṟ puṇarumāṟum, aḷavuppeyarkkum niṟippeyarkkum mutalātaṟkuriya veḻuttukkaḷ ka ca ta pa na ma va a u eṉṉumiv voṉpatumē yeṉṉam variyaṟiyum, mēlē kūṟappaṭṭaṉa vaṟṟiṟkup puṟaṉaṭiyum, yāvar eṉpatu yār eṉavum yātu eṉpatu yāvatu eṉavum varum marūu muṭipum ivviyalil uṇarttappaṭṭaṉa.



6. urupiyal
vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷ peyarkaḷōṭu poruntum muṟimi yiṉi yuṇarttuvatu urupiyalākum. itaṉkaṇ muppatu cūttiraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. peyarum ataṉāl ēṟkappaṭum vēṟṟumiyurupum ākiya avviraṇṭiṟkum iṭiyē varum cāriyikaḷ iviyeṉpatum, vēṟṟumiyurupiṉi yēṟkuṅkāl peyarkaḷ peṟum iyalpum tiripumāvaṉa ivi yeṉpatum ivviyalil vakuttu viḷakkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
uyirkaḷuḷ a ā u ū ē au eṉṉum āṟiṉiyum īṟākavuṭiya peyarkaḷum, meykaḷuḷ ñakara nakaravīṟṟup peyarkaḷum, tev eṉṉum vakaravīṟṟup peyarum, makaravīṟṟup peyarkaḷ cilavum, kuṟṟiyalukara vīṟṟup peyarkaḷum urupēṟ kuṅkāl iṭiyē iṉcāriyi peṟuvaṉa.
paṉmip poruḷik karutiṉa akara vīṟṟup peyarkaḷum, yā eṉṉum ākāravīṟṟu viṉāppeyarum, avi, ivi, uvi, yāvi, av, iv, uv eṉpaṉavum vaṟṟuccāriyi peṟṟu urupēṟpaṉa. yāvi eṉṉum viṉāppeyar vaṟṟuc cāriyi peṟuṅkāl appeyariṉ īṟṟil niṉṟa aikāramum ataṉālūrappaṭṭa vakarameyyum keṭṭu muṭiyum. ellām eṉṉum potuppeyar aḵṟiṇikkaṇvariṉ vaṟṟuccāriyiyum, uyar tiṇikkaṇvariṉ nam cāriyiyum peṟuvatuṭaṉ vēṟṟumi yurupiṉ iṟutiyil um cāriyiyum peṟṟumuṭiyum.
atu, itu, utu eṉavarum ukaravīṟṟuc cuṭṭuppeyarum, ēḻ eṉṉum ḻakaravīṟṟu eṇṇuppeyarum, kuṟṟiyalukaravīṟṟuḷ varum ellā eṇṇuppeyarkaḷum, yātu eṉṉum viṉāp peyarum, aḵtu, iḵtu, uḵtu eṉavarum cuṭṭup peyarkaḷum aṉcāriyi peṟṟu urupēṟpaṉavām. aṉcāriyi peṟuṅkāl atu, itu, utu eṉpaṉa iṟutiyukaraṅ keṭṭu muṭivaṉa; aḵtu, iḵtu, uḵtu eṉpaṉa āytaṅkeṭṭu muṭivaṉa. paḵteṉpataṉi yiṟutiyākavuṭiya orupatumutal eṇpatu variyuḷḷa eṇṇuppeyarkaḷ eṭṭum, aṉcāriyiyēyaṉṟi āṉcāriyi peṟutalum uṇṭu. āṉcāriyi peṟuṅkāl nilimoḻiyiluḷḷa paḵteṉpataṉ kaṇ pakarameymaṭṭum niṟka aḵteṉpatu keṭum.
ōkāra vīṟṟuppeyar urupēṟkuṅkāl oṉcāriyipeṟum.
akara ākāra vīṟṟu marappeyarkaḷ ēḻāmurupu peṟumvaḻi attuccāriyipeṟum. makaravīṟṟup peyarkaḷ urupēṟkuṅkāl attuccāriyi peṟuvaṉa. aḻaṉ, puḻaṉ eṉpaṉa attum iṉṉum peṟṟu uṟaḻvaṉa.
nī eṉṉum peyar niṉ eṉattirintu urupēṟkum. makara vīṟṟuḷ num eṉpatum tām, nām, yām eṉpaṉavum attum iṉṉum peṟātu iyalpāy muṭivaṉa. yām eṉṉum peyaril yakarattiṉmēlēṟiya ākāram ekaramākat tiriya aṅku niṉṟa yakaram keṭum. tām, nām eṉpaṉa mutalkuṟuki muṟiyē tam nam eṉa niṟkum. ellārum eṉṉum paṭarkkic colliṭattum ellīrum eṉṉum muṉṉilic colliṭattum iṟutiyil niṉṟa um eṉpatu keṭa, avvirupeyarum muṟiyē tam cāriyiyum nam cāriyiyum peṟṟu urupēṟkum. avipeṟum urupiṉ piṉṉar um cāriyi vantu poruntum
 ṉakara vīṟṟuḷ tāṉ eṉpatu mutalkuṟukit taṉ eṉṟum yāṉ eṉpatu mutaṟkaṇ niṉṟa yakaraṅkeṭṭu ataṉiyūrntu niṉṟa ākāram ekaramāki eṉ eṉṟum tirintu urupēṟkum.
neṭiṟṟoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷuḷ (ṭaṟa) oṟṟi raṭṭikkuñ coṟkaḷ urupēṟkuṅkāl iṉcāriyipeṟātu iyalpāvaṉa.
kuṟṟukaravīṟṟut ticippeyarkaḷ kaṇṇurupiṉi ēṟkuṅ kāl iṉcāriyi peṟātu iyalpātalumuṇṭu. ivvāṟu iyalpāyavaḻi iṟutiyiluḷḷa kuṟṟiyalukaram tāṉ ūrntu niṉṟa meyyōṭu cērakkeṭum.
iṅku eṭutturikkappaṭṭa peyarkaḷallāta ēṉiya peyarkaḷ cāriyi peṟṟē urupēṟṟal vēṇṭumeṉṉum variyaṟiyillātaṉavām. avi cāriyi peṟṟum peṟātum muṭivaṉavām.



7. uyir mayaṅkiyal
uyirīṟu niṉṟu valleḻuttōṭum ciṟupāṉmi ēṉi yeḻuttukkaḷōṭum puṇarumāṟu kūṟuvatu uyir mayaṅkiya lākum. mayaṅkutal - kalattal. uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyākiya uyartiṇip peyarkaḷ alvaḻi vēṟṟumiyākiya iru vaḻiyilum iyalpāmeṉavum, oru cila viṭaṅkaḷil aḵṟiṇi viravup peyar iyalpātalumuṇṭeṉavum toki marapil vitantu kūṟiya āciriyar, aviyoḻinta uyirīṟṟu aḵṟiṇip peyarkaḷiyum ēṉi viṉimuṟṟu viṉiyeccam peyarec caṅkaḷiyum akaravīṟumutal aukāravīṟumuṭiya neṭuṅkaṇakku muṟiyil vittu uṇarttukiṉṟār. ivviyalil 93-cūttiraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuṭ pala māṭṭēṟṟu muṟiyil amintaṉavākum. ivviyaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭa vitikaḷ yāvum valleḻuttu mikuvaṉa, iyalpāvaṉa, melleḻuttu mikuvaṉa, uyirmika varuvaṉa, nīṭavaruvaṉa kuṟukivaruvaṉa, cāriyipeṟuvaṉa, piṟavāṟu tirivaṉa eṉṉum ivvakiyuḷ aṭaṅkuvaṉavākum.
a, i, u. eṉṉum mūṉṟu cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉum uyirum yakaramumvariṉ vakaravoṟṟum kacatapañanamava eṉpaṉavariṉ vanta oṟṟeḻuttukkaḷum mikumeṉpatum ceyyuḷuḷ cuṭṭu nīṇṭu muṭiyumeṉpatum 3-6, 34, 35, 53-ām cūttiraṅkaḷiṟ collap paṭṭaṉa.
ā, ā, ī, u, ū, ē, ō, ōḷa eṉpavaṟṟi īṟākavuṭiya peyarkaḷ alvaḻi vēṟṟumiyākiya īriṭattum valleḻuttu mikappeṟumeṉṟum, ikara aikāra vīṟṟup peyarkaḷ vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ valleḻuttu mikappeṟu meṉṟum 1, 14, 19, 22, 33, 47, 57, 62, 64, 72, 74, 78, 87, 90, 93-ām, cūttiraṅkaḷil āciriyar virittukkūṟiyuḷḷār.
alvaḻikkaṇ akaravīṟṟuḷ aṉṉaveṉṉum uvamavurupum, aṇmicuṭṭiya uyartiṇi viḷippeyarum, ceymmaṉa eṉṉum viṉiccollum, ēvalik karutiya viyaṅkōḷ viṉiyum, ceyta eṉṉum vāypāṭṭup peyareccamum, ceyyiya eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉiyeccamum, amma eṉṉum uriyaciyum, akara vīṟṟup palavaṟicollum, ākāravīṟṟuḷ ā, mā eṉṉum peyarc collum, uyartiṇi viḷippeyarum, yā viṉāvum muṟṟuviṉiyum, miyā eṉṉum acic collum, taṉ toḻilik kuṟittuvarum ākāra viṉāviyuṭiya viṉiccollum, īkāra vīṟṟuḷ nī, mī eṉpaṉavum pakaravīkāramum, ukaravīṟṟuc cuṭṭup peyarkaḷum, tēṟṟap poruḷil varum ekaravīṟum, ciṟappup poruḷil varum okaravīṟum, māṟukōḷeccap poruḷilum viṉāp poruṇmiyilum eṇṇupporuṇmiyilum aiyap poruṇmiyilum varum ēkāra ōkāra vīṟṟiṭic coṟkaḷum valleḻuttu mikātu iyalpāvaṉa.
akaravīṟṟu marappeyarum ā, i, u, ē, ai ākiya īṟukaḷil eṭuttōtiya piṭā, taḷā, mā (maram) ā, mā, (vilaṅku) uti, oṭu, cē, vici, ñemi, nami eṉṉum peyarkaḷum valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻivariṉ melleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉavām. yā, piṭā, taḷā, puḷi (cuvi) eṉpaṉa valleḻuttup peṟutalumuṇṭu.
ummittokikkaṇvarum ākāravīṟṟuppeyarum vēṟṟumikkaṇ kuṟiṟkīḻ niṉṟa ākāravīṟṟuppeyarum ōreḻuttoru moḻiyākiya ākāra vīṟṟuppeyarum akaram mikku muṭiyum. irā eṉṉuñ collukku akaramikutal illi. kuṟiṟkīḻ niṉṟa ūkāravīṟum ōreḻuttuttoru moḻiyākiya ūkāra vīṟum vēṟṟumikkaṇ ukaram mikkumuṭiyum. ē yeṉṉum peyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ ekaram mikku muṭiyum. ōkāravīṟu vēṟṟumik kaṇ okaram mikum. aukāravīṟṟup peyar iruvaḻiyilum ukarammikum.
ceyyuṭkaṇvarum akaraccuṭṭum, amma eṉṉum iṭic colliṟutiyum, pala cila eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷiṉ iṟuti akaramum, ḻakarameyyiyūrnta iṟuti ukaramum nīṇṭu muṭivaṉavām.
ā muṉvarum pakara vīkāram kuṟukum. kuṟiṟkīḻ niṉṟa iṟuti ākāram kuṟuki ukaram peṟum.
maka, āṭūu, makaṭūu cē eṉpaṉa iṉcāriyipeṟum. nilā attuccāriyipeṟum. paṉi (kālam), vaḷi (pūtam), maḻi eṉpaṉa attuccāriyiyum iṉcāriyiyum peṟuvaṉa. puḷi, eru, ceru, paṉi, ari, āviri eṉpaṉa amcāriyipeṟuvaṉa. ikara aikāra vīṟṟu nāṭ peyarmuṉ toḻiṟcolvariṉ iṭiyē āṉcāriyi varum. avviraṇṭīṟṟut tiṅkaṭ peyar muṉ toḻiṟcolvariṉ iṭiyē ikkuccāriyi varum. ūveṉṉum ōreḻuttorumoḻi ṉakaravoṟṟum akkuc cāriyiyum peṟum.
a, ī, u, ai, ō eṉṉum īṟukaḷukku urupiyaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭa cāriyikaḷi ivviyaliluḷḷa 18, 51, 61, 79, 92-ām cūttiraṅkaḷāl avvīṟṟup poruṭ puṇarccikkum māṭṭeṟintu viti kūṟuvar āciriyar.
cāva eṉpataṉ vakaravuyirmeyyum vāḻiya eṉpataṉ yakaravuyirmeyyum keṭṭu muṭiyum. nāḻi eṉpataṉmuṉ uri yeṉṉuñcol vantupuṇariṉ nilimoḻiyīṟṟiṉ ikaram tāṉ ēṟiya ḻakara meyyuṭaṉkeṭa avviṭattu ṭakaramey tōṉṟi muṭiyum. ceru eṉpataṉ muṉvarum amcāriyiyiṉ makaraṅkeṭa varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum. paṉi, āviri eṉpaṉa amcāriyi peṟuṅkāl iṟuti aikāraṅkeṭṭu muṭivaṉa. paṉi eṉṉuñcolmuṉ aṭṭu eṉpatu varumoḻiyāyvariṉ nili moḻiyiṟuti aikāraṅkeṭa avviṭattu ākāram tōṉṟum. pala cila eṉpavi tam muṉṉart tāmvariṉ iṟuti niṉṟa lakara vuyirmey lakaravoṟṟākat tiriyum. iṉṟiyeṉpataṉ iṟutiyikaram ceyyuḷuḷ ukaramākattiriyum. cuṭṭup peyarīṟṟu ukaram aṉṟu eṉpataṉōṭu puṇaruṅkāl ākāramākat tiritalum aiyeṉpataṉōṭu puṇaruṅkāl keṭutalum ceyyuḷiṭat tuṇ ṭākun tiripukaḷām. vēṭki yeṉṉuñcolmuṉ avā eṉpatu vantu puṇariṉ nilimoḻiyīṟṟiluḷḷa aikāram tāṉ ūrntu niṉṟa kakarameyyoṭuṅkeṭa aṅkuḷḷa ṭakaram ṇakara māyt tirital ceyyuṭkaṇvarun tiripākum.



8. puḷḷi mayaṅkiyal
meyyīṟu vaṉkaṇattōṭum piṟakaṇattōṭum puṇarumāṟu kūṟuvatu puḷḷi mayaṅkiyalākum. meyyīṟellām puḷḷiyoṭu niṟkumātaliṉ puḷḷiyeṉṟār. meyyīṟṟuḷ ukarampeṟuvaṉa, iṟuti keṭṭu valleḻuttu mikuvaṉa melleḻuttu mikuvaṉa, iṟutikeṭātu valleḻuttu mikuvaṉa melleḻuttu mikuvaṉa, valleḻuttum melleḻuttum uṟaḻntu muṭivaṉa, iyalpāy varuvaṉa, cāriyi peṟuvaṉa, tirintu muṭivaṉa eṉṉum ivvakiyiṉuḷ ivviyaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭa vitikaḷyāvum aṭaṅku vaṉavām.
ña, na, ṇa, ma, la, ḷa eṉṉum meykaḷi iṟutiyākavuṭiya toḻiṟpeyar muṉṉar valleḻuntu mutaṉmoḻi variṉ iru vaḻiyum varumoḻi valleḻuttu mika nilimoḻiyīṟu ukaram peṟṟu muṭiyum. nakara vīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ ukarampeṟātu akarampeṟṟu muṭiyum, īm, kam, urum, miṉ, piṉ, kaṉ, val, tev, puḷ, vaḷ eṉa eṭuttōtiya peyarkaḷum toḻiṟpeyarpōla iruvaḻiyum ukarampeṟṟu valleḻuttu mikuvaṉavām. avaṟṟuḷ kaṉ eṉṉuñcol vēṟṟumikkaṇ akaram peṟṟu valleḻuttu mikappeṟum. val eṉṉuñ colliṉmuṉ nāy, palaki eṉpaṉa varumoḻiyāyvariṉ avvaḻi ukaramiṉṟi akarampeṟṟu muṭiyum.
verin eṉṟa col, iṟuti nakaravoṟṟum accol peṟṟa akaramumkeṭa valleḻuttu varumvaḻi avvalleḻut tāyiṉum ataṉ kiḷiyeḻuttākiya melleḻuttāyiṉum mikku muṭiyum, makaravīṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ iṟuti makaramkeṭa valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum. iṅṅaṉam makara vīṟukeṭṭa viṭattu varumoḻi valleḻuttōṭu melleḻuttu uṟaḻntu muṭiyum moḻikaḷum cila uḷa. āyiram eṉpataṉmuṉ aḷavuppeyar vantu puṇarumiṭattu vēṟṟumiyiṟpōla iṟuti makaraṅkeṭṭu valleḻuttu mikum. illam eṉṉum marappeyar iṟuti makaraṅkeṭṭu melleḻuttu mikum. aḻaṉ eṉpataṉ iṟutikeṭa varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum.
vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇvarum yaraḻa vīṟṟup peyarkaḷ valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu avval leḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉa. tāy eṉṉuñcolmuṉ makaṉ viṉivariṉ valleḻuttu mikum. ār, vetir, cār, pīr, kumiḻ eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ valleḻuttu varuvaḻi melleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉa. vēṟṟumikkaṇ yakara vīṟṟuṭ cilavum cār, pāḻ eṉpaṉavum valleḻuttōṭu melleḻuttuppeṟṟu uṟaḻvaṉavām. kīḻ eṉṉuñcol valleḻuttuppeṟṟum peṟātum uṟaḻum.
āṇ, peṇ, umaṇ, muraṇ, kuyiṉ, ekiṉ, tāṉ, pēṉ, kōṉ eṉpaṉavum neṭṭaḻuttiṉ piṉvarum lakāra, ḷakāra vīṟṟuppeyarkaḷ cilavum tiriyātu iyalpāvaṉavām. tāy eṉṉum peyarum alvaḻikkaṇvarum yakāra vīṟṟup peyarkaḷum valleḻuttu mikātu iyalpāvaṉa. alvaḻik kaṇvarum ellārum, tām, nām, yām, tāṉ eṉpaṉa kuṟukalun tiritalumiṉṟi iyalpāvaṉa. nūṟāyiram, tāmari, veḷḷam, āmpal eṉpavaṟṟōṭum ēṉi uyir mutaṉmoḻikaḷōṭum puṇarum ēḻ eṉṉum eṇṇuppeyarum, uyir mutaṉ moḻikaḷōṭum yakara vakara mutaṉmoḻikaḷōṭum puṇarum vakara vīṟṟuc cuṭṭuppeyarkaḷum kuṟukalun tiritalumiṉṟi iyalpāvaṉavām.
āṇ, ekiṉ, pīr, pūl, vēl, āl, kumiḻ eṉpaṉa amcāriyiyum, vēṟṟumikkaṇvarum īm, kam eṉpaṉavum kōl eṉpataṉōṭu puṇarum tāḻ eṉṉuñ collum, tamiḻ eṉṉum collum akkuccāriyiyum, veyil, iruḷ eṉpaṉa attuccāriyiyum iṉcāriyiyum, makaravīṟṟu nāṭpeyar ikara vīṟṟu nāṭpeyarpōla āṉcāriyiyum ataṉmēl attuc cāriyiyum, ceyyuḷiṭattuvarum viṇ eṉṉuñcol viṉic col varumoḻiyāka varumiṭattu attuccāriyiyum, taṉittum aṭiyaṭuttum varum āyiram eṉṟa eṇṇuppeyar piṟa eṇkaḷōṭu puṇarumiṭattu attuccāriyiyum peṟuvaṉa eṉpar āciriyar.
ellārum eṉṉum paṭarkkippeyarum, ellīrum eṉṉum muṉṉilippeyarum, neṭumutal kuṟuki muṭiyumiyal puṭiya tām, nām, yām eṉṉum peyarkaḷum, ṉakara vīṟṟuḷ tāṉ, yāṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷum cāriyipeṟuvaṉa īṟukeṭṭu iṭiyilum iṟutiyilum cāriyipeṟṟum, neṭumutal kuṟukuvaṉa neṭumutal kuṟukiyum, urupu puṇarccikkaṇ muṭintavāṟē vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarcciyiṉum muṭiyumeṉa āciriyar māṭṭēṟṟu muṟiyāṉ vitikūṟiyuḷḷār. ellām eṉṉum viravuppeyar vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ iṭiyē vaṟṟuccāriyiyum iṟutiyil umcāriyiyum peṟum. appeyar alvaḻikkaṇ cāriyi peṟātu. uyartiṇikkaṇ iṭiyē nam cāriyiyum iṟutiyil umcāriyiyumpeṟum. vakara vīṟṟuc cuṭṭuppeyar vaṟṟuccāriyipeṟum. ēḻ eṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar aṉcāriyi peṟum.
ṇakara ḷakara vīṟukaḷ valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varuṅkāl ṭakaramākavum, ṉakara lakara vīṟukaḷ ṟakaramākavum tirivaṉa. eḷḷi yuṇarttum eṇ eṉṉum peyariṉ ṇakaram alvaḻiyilum ṭakara mākattiriyum. nel, cel, kol, col eṉpavaṟṟiṉ lakaram alvaḻiyilum ṟakaramāyttiriyum. alvaḻikkaṇvarum ḷakaravīṟu tirintum tiriyātum uṟaḻntu muṭiyum. vēṟṟumiyiṟpōla alvaḻiyilum ṭakaramākattiriyum ḷakara vīṟukaḷ cilavuḷa.
maṉ, ciṉ, āṉ, īṉ, piṉ, muṉ eṉpaṉavum ceyiṉ eṉṉum viṉiyeccamum avvayiṉ, ivvayiṉ, uvvayiṉ evvayiṉ eṉavarum ēḻām vēṟṟumi yiṭapporuḷuṇarttum iṭic coṟkaḷum ākiyavaṟṟiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramākattiriyum. mīṉ eṉpataṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramākattirintum tiriyātum uṟaḻum.
lakara ḷakara vīṟṟuccoṟkaḷ takaramutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu lakara ḷakaraṅkaḷ āytamākattiriyum. melleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varuṅkāl muṟiyē lakaram ṉakaramākavum ḷakaram ṇakaramākavum tirivaṉa.
makaravīṟu alvaḻikkaṇ valivariṉ ēṟṟamelleḻuttākat tiriyum. akam eṉṉuñ colliṉmuṉ ki eṉpatu varumoḻiyāyvariṉ nilimoḻiyiṉ naṭuveḻuttākiya kakaravuyir meykeṭa melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum. num eṉpataṉi alvaḻikkaṇ kūṟuṅkāl nakaravoṟṟiṉmēl niṉṟa ukaram keṭa avvoṟṟiṉmēl īkāram ūrntu nī eṉṟāki ōr ikaram iṭiyilvara iṟuti makaraṅkeṭṭu rakara voṟṟut tōṉṟi nīyir eṉattiriyumeṉpar tolkāppiyar. eṉavē num eṉpatē tiripil colleṉṟum nīyir eṉpatu ataṉ tiripeṉṟuṅ koḷḷutalvēṇṭum.
tēṉ eṉṉuñcol, valivariṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramākat tirintum tiriyātum uṟaḻtalum, iṟuti ṉakaraṅkeṭṭu valimeli mikutalum, melivariṉ iṟutikeṭṭum keṭātum uṟaḻtalum peṟum. iṟāl eṉpatu varumoḻiyākavariṉ tēṉ eṉpataṉ ṉakaram keṭṭut takaram iraṭṭittu muṭiyum.
cāttaṉ, koṟṟaṉ mutaliya iyaṟpeyar muṉṉart tantiyeṉpatu varumoḻiyāyvariṉ, tanti yeṉpataṉ mutaliluḷḷa takaraṅkeṭṭu, ataṉmēlēṟiya akaraṅkeṭātu niṟpa, nilimoḻiyiyaṟpeyariluḷḷa aṉkeṭṭu, aṅku niṉṟa meyyiṉmēl varumoḻi mutaliluḷḷa akaram ēṟimuṭiyum. muṟkūṟiya iyaṟpeyaruḷ ātaṉ, pūtaṉ eṉṉum irupeyarkaḷum varumoḻiyākiya tantiyeṉṉum muṟippeyaroṭu puṇaruṅkāl ivviyaṟ peyarkaḷiṉ iṟutiniṉṟa aṉkeṭa eñciya takaravoṟṟum, tanti eṉpatil muṉkūṟiyapaṭi takaraṅkeṭa eñciya akaravuyirum cērakkeṭṭu, ānti, pūnti eṉa muṭivaṉavām. ippeyarkaḷ ciṟappup paṇpaṭuttuvaruṅkāl aṅṅaṉantiritaliṉṟi iyalpāvaṉa eṉpar. mēṟkūṟiya iyaṟpeyarkaḷ iṉṉāṟku makaṉ iṉṉāṉ eṉṉum muṟiyil oṭṭi niṟkuṅkāl nilimoḻip peyarīṟṟiṉ aṉkeṭṭu amcāriyi peṟṟup puṇartalumuṇṭu.
muṉ eṉṉum colmuṉ il eṉṉuñcol vantu puṇariṉ iṭiyē ṟakaravoṟṟut tōṉṟi muṉṟil eṉamuṭital toṉṟu toṭṭu maruvivaḻaṅkum ilakkaṇa muṭipām. poṉ eṉṉuñcol, īṟṟil ṉakaramkeṭa lakaravuyirmeyyum makara meyyumpeṟṟup polam eṉat tirintuvaḻaṅkum. il eṉṉum col, vīṭṭi yuṇarttātu illāmiyeṉṉum poruḷi yuṇarttuṅkāl, aikāram peṟṟu varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikutalum, mikāmiyum, iyalpātalum, ākārampeṟṟu valleḻuttu mikutalum ākiya nāṉkumuṭipiṉi yuṭiyatākum.



9. kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal
kuṟṟiyalukaravīṟu niṉṟu varumoḻiyōṭu puṇarum iyal piṉi yuṇarttuvatu ivviyalākum. neṭṭeḻuttiṉ piṉṉum toṭarmoḻiyīṟṟum kuṟṟiyalukaram varum eṉa moḻimarapiṟ kūṟiṉār. toṭarmoḻiyīṟṟuk kuṟṟukaratti ayaliluḷḷa eḻuttu vakiyāl uyirttoṭar, iṭittoṭar, āytat toṭar, vaṉṟoṭar, meṉṟoṭar eṉa aintākappakuttu, avaṟṟoṭu neṭṭeḻuttiṉ piṉvaruṅ kuṟṟukarattiyuṅ kūṭṭi aṟuvakippaṭuttu viḷakkukiṉṟār.
yaraḻamuṉṉark kacatapaṅañanama īroṟṟākivaruṅ kuṟṟukaraṅka ḷ oruvāṟṟāṉ iṭiyoṟṟukkaḷōṭu toṭarntuvariṉum kuṟṟiyalukarattiyaṭuttu nērē toṭarntaṉa allavātaliṉ avi iṭittoṭarākak koḷḷappaṭāveṉṟum, āṟīṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramum iruvaḻiyilum keṭātu niṟintē niṟkumeṉṟum, vaṉṟoṭarkkuṟṟiyalukaram valleḻuttu mutaṉ moḻi varumiṭattu muṉkūṟiya (arimāttiriyiṉuṅ kuṟukum) iyalpil niṟṟalumuritteṉṟum, kuṟṟiyalukara vīṟṟiṉmuṉ yakara mutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu īṟṟiṟ kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkeṭa aṅku ōr ikaram vantu kuṟuki niṟkumeṉṟum ivviyaliṉ mutal aintu cūttirattāṉum kuṟṟiyalukarattiyal piṉi āciriyar potuvakiyāṟ kūṟippōntār. 6-mutal 17-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷāl kuṟṟukaravīṟṟu vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarcci kūṟiṉār. 18-mutal kuṟṟukaravīṟṟu alvaḻippuṇarcciyiṉit toṭaṅkik kūṟukiṉṟār.
uṇmit taṉmiyi yuṇarttum uṇṭu eṉṉuñcol valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻivariṉ iṟutiniṉṟa ukaram mey yoṭuṅkeṭa ṇakaram ḷakaramākat tiriyumeṉṟum, kuṟṟukara vīṟṟut ticippeyarkaḷuḷ iraṇṭu perunticiyum tammiṟ puṇarumiṭattu ēyeṉṉuñcāriyi iṭiyē vantumuṭiyu meṉṟum, kuṟṟukaravīṟṟup perunticikaḷōṭu kōṇat ticikaḷ puṇarumiṭattu iṟutiyukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭumeṉṟum avvaḻi teṟku eṉpataṉ ṟakaram ṉakaramākat tiriyumeṉṟum kūṟuvar āciriyar.
kuṟṟukara vīṟṟu eṇṇup peyariyalpiṉi 28-ām cūttira mutalākat toṭaṅkik kūṟukiṉṟār. pattu eṉṉum eṇṇiṉ muṉ oṉṟumutal eṭṭuvariyuḷḷa eṇkaḷum āyirameṉṉum eṇṇum niṟippeyarum aḷavuppeyarum vantu puṇaru muṟiyiṉi 28-mutal 30-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷ uṇarttuvaṉa oṉṟu mutal eṭṭuvariyuḷḷa kuṟṟukara vīṟṟu eṇṇum peyarkaḷiṉmuṉ pattu vantu puṇartali 31-mutal 38-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷ kuṟippiṭuvaṉa. oṉpatu eṉṉum colliṉmuṉ pattu vantu puṇaruṅkāl nilimoḻiyākiya oṉpatil okarattiṟku mēlākat takara meytōṉṟit toṉpatu eṉṟākip patu keṭṭu ṉakaram ṇakaramākat tirintu iraṭṭittut toṇṇ eṉa niṟkumeṉṟum, varumoḻiyākiya paḵteṉpatil pakaravuyirmeyyum āytamumkeṭṭu ūkāram tōṉṟi iṟutiyiluḷḷa tu eṉpataṉ takaram ṟakaramākat tirintu ūṟu eṉṟākit toṇṇuṟu eṉa muṭiyumeṉṟum, ivviyal 39-ām cūttirattāl āciriyar viritturippar. mēṟkūṟiya oṉṟumutal oṉpatu variyuḷḷa eṇṇuppeyarkaḷōṭu aḷavuppeyarum niṟip peyarum vantu puṇaru muṟiyiṉi 40-mutal 53-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷālum, nūṟeṉṉuñcol varumoḻiyāy vantu puṇarvataṉi 54, 55, 56, 57-ām cūttiraṅkaḷālum, āyiram eṉṉum eṇ taṉittum nūṟeṉṉum aṭiyaṭuttum puṇartali 58-mutal 65-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷālum kūṟuvar āciriyar. oṉpatu eṉṉum eṇṇiṉmuṉ nūṟu eṉṉum eṇ vantupuṇariṉ muṟkūṟiyavāṟu nilimoḻiyāy niṉṟa oṉpatu eṉṉum colliṉ okarattiṉ muṉṉē takaramey oṉṟu tōṉṟit toṉpatu eṉṟākip patukeṭṭu ṉakaramey ḷakara meyyāka iraṭṭittut toḷḷ eṉa niṟkumeṉṟum, varumoḻiyākiya nūṟeṉpataṉ mutalil niṉṟa nakaramey keṭa ammeyyiṉ mēlēṟiya ūkāram ākāramākattirintu ataṉi yaṭuttu ikaravuyirum rakaravuyir meyyum iṭiyētōṉṟi īṟṟiluḷḷa ṟakara vukaraṅkeṭṭu makarameytōṉṟi āyiram eṉṟākit toḷḷāyiram eṉa muṭiyumeṉṟum 57-ām cūttirattāl tolkāppiyaṉār virittukkūṟiyuḷḷār. toṇṇuṟu, toḷḷāyiram eṉṉum puṇarmoḻikaḷukku āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟum puṇarcci muṟikaḷ valintu kūṟuvaṉavākavē amintuḷḷaṉa veṉṟum oṉpatu eṉṉum poruḷpaṭat tolkāppiyarē toṇṭu eṉṉum eṇṇuppeyarit tam nūlil āṇṭiruttalāl toṇṭu+pattu = toṇṇuṟu eṉṟum, toṇṭu+āyiram = toḷḷāyiram eṉṟum nilimoḻi varumoḻi ceytu puṇarcciviti kūṟuvatē moḻiyiyalpukku ottatākumeṉṟum paritimāṟkaliñar kūṟum koḷki iṅku cintikkat takkatākum.
nūṟu eṉpatu nilimoḻiyāka ataṉ muṉ oṉṟu mutal oṉpatum aviyūr pattum aḷavum niṟiyum vantu puṇarum muṟiyiṉi 66-mutal 68-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷ viḷakku vaṉavām. ōṉṟu mutal eṭṭiṉiyūrnta paḵteṉṉum eṇṇup peyaruṭaṉ oṉṟu mutal oṉpateṇkaḷum āyiramum aḷavum niṟiyum puṇaru muṟiyiṉi 69, 70, 71-ām cūttiraṅkaḷ kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu poruṭ peyarkaḷ puṇarumiyalpiṉi 72, 73-ām cūttiraṅkaḷālum iraṇṭu mutal oṉpatu variyuḷḷa eṇkaḷiṉmuṉ aḷavu, niṟi, eṇ eṉpavaṟṟukkuriya mā eṉṉuñcol puṇartali 74-ām cūttirattālum āciriyar kuṟippiṭuvar. eṇṇuppeyar kaḷuḷ mikka eṇṇōṭu kuṟinta eṇ vantu puṇartali ummittokiyākavum, kuṟinta eṇṇōṭu mikka eṇ varutalip paṇput tokiyākavumkoṇṭu āciriyar viti kūṟiyuḷḷami ivaṇ karutaṟkuriyatākum. lakāra ṉakāra vīṟṟuc ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟuvatu 75-ām cūttiram. iv vatikārattu nilimoḻi varumoḻiceytu puṇarkkappaṭā moḻikaḷ iviyeṉat tokutturippatu 76-ām cūttiramākum. ivviyaliṉ iṟutiyiluḷḷa 77-ām cūttiram eḻuttatikārattiṉ puṟaṉaṭiyākum.


collatikāram
eḻuttuñ collum poruḷum nāṭi eṉa mēṟpāyirattuḷ niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē eḻuttilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiya āciriyar ippaṭalattāl collilakkaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟār. ataṉāl itu collatikārameṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. collāvatu muṟkūṭiya eḻuttiṉāṉ ākkappaṭṭu irutiṇip poruṭṭaṉmiyiyum oruvar uṇarntu koḷḷutaṟkuk karuviyākiya ōciyām. kiḷavi, col, moḻi eṉpaṉa eḻuttiṉālākiya ōciyiyē kuṟippaṉa. kaṭaloli, caṅkoli, iṭiyoli mutaliyaṉa eḻuttiyal taḻuvā ōcikaḷām ivaṟṟi aravam, ōci, ici eṉṟa coṟkaḷāl vaḻaṅkutal marapu. colleṉpatu eḻuttiṉālakkappaṭṭu irutiṇip poruḷkaḷiyum aṟivikkum ōci yeṉṟum, taṉṉiyuṇara niṉṟavaḻi eḻutteṉavum, piṟaporuḷi yuṇarttiyavaḻic colleṉavum kūṟappaṭumeṉṟum uriyāciriyar kūṟuvar. oruvar poruḷi uṇartaṟkum uṇarttaṟkum karuviyāy niṟpatu col. tāṉē oru poruḷik karutiyuṇarttum uṇarvu collukku illi. poruḷiyuṇarttuvāṉoruvaṉ colliṉ tuṇikoṇṭaṉṟip poruḷi yaṟivuṟuttalā kāmiyiṉ avaṉatu toḻilic collākiya karuvimēlēṟṟic col uṇarttumeṉak karuvik karuttāvākak kūṟuvar tolkāppiyar.
coṟkaḷip pākupaṭuttu viḷakkak karutiya āciriyar irutiṇi, aimpāl, eḻuvaki vaḻu, eṭṭu vēṟṟumi, aṟu vakit toki, mūṉṟiṭam, mūṉṟu kālam, iruvaki vaḻakku eṉṉum ivveṭṭu vakiyāṉ ārāyntuṇarttiṉāreṉpar iḷampūraṇar. ivveṭṭiṉōṭu col nāṉkuvakiya eṉṟalum, avaṟṟiyē palavākap pakuttalum, vikāravakiyum, poruḷ kōḷ vakiyum, ceyyuṭkuriya col nāṉkeṉṟalum, eṉa iviyuṅkūṭṭi eṭṭiṟanta palavakiyāṉ ārāyn tuṇarttiṉā reṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
col taṉimoḻi, toṭarmoḻi eṉa iru vakippaṭum. moḻikaḷ yāṅkaṇun taṉittu nillāvēṉum ipporuṭku iccol eṉa aṟivuṭiyōr variyaṟuttukkūṟiya paṭippuk kālattum, toṭarmoḻic coṟkaḷuḷ oṉṟu niṟpa maṟṟiya eñciya vaḻiyum taṉittuniṟṟaluṇṭu. ataṉāl taṉimoḻiyeṉṟa pirivum koḷḷappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. taṉimoḻi poruṇmi māttiram uṇarttuvatallatu kēṭṭārkku oru payaṉpaṭa niṟpataṉṟām. kēṭṭārkkup poruḷiṉitu viḷakkip payaṉpaṭa niṟpaṉa toṭarmoḻikaḷēyām. ākavē toṭarmoḻikaḷiṉ iyalpiṉi muṉṉuṇartti avaṟṟukkuk karuviyākiya taṉimoḻi yilakkaṇattiṉip piṉṉuṇarttutalē muṟiyākum. im muṟiyiṉi yuḷattuṭkoṇṭu ippaṭalattuḷ alvaḻi vēṟṟumiyākiya toṭarmoḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇatti muṉṉuṇartti attoṭarmoḻikaḷip pakuttukkāṇum muṟiyāl peyarc col, viṉiccol, iṭiccol, uriccol ākiya taṉimoḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷip piṉṉar uṇarttuvar āciriyar.
coṟkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu toṭaruṅkāl payaṉili vakiyāṉum, tokinili vakiyāṉum, eṇṇunili vaki yāṉum toṭarumeṉpatu tamiḻilakkaṇa marapākum. cāttaṉ vantāṉ eṉṟāṟpōla eḻuvāyum payaṉiliyumākat toṭarntu niṟpatu payaṉilivaki yeṉappaṭum. vēṟṟumi yurupum uvama vurupum eṇṇummiyākiya iṭiccollum viṉiccollīṟum paṇpuṇarttum īṟum iviyallāta piṟitōr collum maṟintu niṟkattoṭarum colliṉatu toṭarcci toki nilivaki, yeṉappaṭum. poruḷkaḷi oṉṟōṭoṉṟu cērttu eṇṇum muṟiyil aminta coṟkaḷatu toṭarcci eṇṇunilivaki yeṉappaṭum. ivvāṟu mūvakiyāl toṭarum toṭarmoḻikaḷellā vaṟṟiyum poruḷnilimi nōkki alvaḻit toṭareṉṟum vēṟṟumit toṭareṉṟum iru vakiyākap pakutturippar tollāciriyar.
poruḷiyiṭamākakkoṇṭu nikaḻvatu collākum collilakkaṇaṅkūṟak karutiya āciriyar accol nikaḻccikku nilikkaḷaṉākiya poruḷkaḷellāvaṟṟiyum uyartiṇi yeṉavum aḵṟiṇiyeṉavum irutiṟaṉāka variyaṟuttu, apporuḷ vakipaṟṟi nikaḻuñ coṟkaḷiyum uyartiṇic colleṉṟum aḵṟiṇic colleṉṟum iru vakiyākap pakutturittār. tiṇiyeṉṉum collukku oḻukkam eṉpatu poruḷākum. makkaḷatu nallaṟiviṉ payaṉāyamivatu oḻukkam. vilaṅku mutaliya ciṟṟuyirkaḷiṉiṉṟum makkaṭ kulattāri uyartiṇiyeṉac ciṟappittuuyarttuvatu maṉavuṇarviṉ pāṟpaṭṭa nalloḻukkamēyām. ulaka vāḻvil mēṉmēl uyarcci yaṭitaṟkuk kāraṇamākiya ivvoḻukka vuṇarvu makkaṭ kulattāriṭamē ciṟappāka amintu vaḷartal karuti avarkaḷi uyartiṇiyeṉat taṉic ciṟappuṭiya tokutiyākavum, naṉṟuntītum pakuttuṇarntoḻukum nallaṟivu vāykkappeṟāta maṟṟiya vuyirkaḷiyum uyiral poruḷ kaḷiyum oḻukka vuṇarccikkuriyavallāta aḵṟiṇi yeṉac ciṟappiltokutiyākavum muṉṉit tamiḻāciriyar pakuttuḷḷārkaḷ. iṅṅaṉam ulakap poruḷkaḷellā vaṟṟiyum uyartiṇi, aḵṟiṇi eṉa iraṇṭāka aṭakki avaṟṟi āṇpāl, peṇpāl, palarpāl, oṉṟaṉpāl, palaviṉpāl eṉa aintu pālkaḷākap pakuttu ipporuḷ vēṟupāṭṭiṉi viḷaṅka aṟintu koḷḷutaṟkuriya collamip piṉiyuṭiyatāka nam muṉṉōr tam tāymoḻiyākiya tamiḻmoḻiyi uruvākki vaḷarttārkaḷ. ivvāṟu coṟkaḷiṉ vāyilākat tiṇipālkaḷi viḷaṅka aṟivikkum muṟi tamiḻilaṉṟi vēṟem moḻiyiluṅ kāṇappaṭāta ciṟappiyalpākum.



collilakkaṇa vaki
muntu nūlkaṇṭu muṟippaṭa eṇṇiya tolkāppiyaṉār, coṟkaḷip poruḷ nilimi nōkkit toṭarmoḻi, taṉi moḻiyeṉa iruvakippaṭuttu, attoṭarmoḻiyi al vaḻittoṭar, vēṟṟumittoṭar eṉa iru vakippaṭuttu, avviruvakittoṭarum ceppum viṉāvumāka nikaḻtalāl avaṟṟi vaḻuvāmaṟ kūṟutaṟkāka muṟpaṭac coṉṉilimiyāṟ poruḷi uyartiṇi, aḵṟiṇiyeṉa iru vakippaṭuttār. avaṟṟuḷ uyartiṇiyuṇarttuñ coṟkaḷi oruvaṉi yaṟiyuñ col, oruttiyi yaṟiyuñcol, palari yaṟiyuñcol eṉa mūvakippaṭuttu, aḵṟiṇi yuṇarttuñ coṟkaḷi oṉṟaṉi yaṟiyuñcol, palavaṟṟiyaṟiyuñcol eṉa iruvakippaṭuttu, amikkavēṇṭuñ coṟkaḷi yeṭut tōtiṉār. ataṉpiṉ vēṟṟumittoṭar kūṟuvār mayaṅkā marapiṉavāki varuvaṉa eḻuvaki vēṟṟumiyuṇartti, ataṉpiṉ avvēṟṟumikkaṇ mayaṅkumāṟuṇartti, ataṉpiṉ eṭṭāvatākiya viḷivēṟṟumi yuṇartti, ataṉpiṉ taṉimoḻip pakutiyākiya peyarccol, viṉiccol, iṭiccol, uriccol eṉpavaṟṟiṉ pākupāṭum uṇartti, ataṉpiṉ coṟkaḷ vikārappaṭumāṟum oṭṭumāṟum eñcumāṟum piṟavum uṇarttukiṉṟār. eḻuttatikārattuḷ eḻuvāy vēṟṟumiyiyum ataṉ tiripākiya viḷivēṟṟumi yiyum alvaḻikkaṇṇē muṭitta āciriyar ivvatikārattē vēṟṟumikaḷuṭaṉ iyittu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟār.
iccollatikāram oṉpatu iyalkaḷāl iyaṉṟatākum. kiḷaviyākkattuḷ alvaḻit toṭarum, vēṟṟumiyiyal, vēṟṟumi mayaṅkiyal, viḷimarapu eṉṉum mūṉṟiyalkaḷilum. vēṟṟumit toṭarum, peyariyalil peyarilakkaṇamum, viṉiyiyalil viṉiyilakkaṇamum, iṭiyiyalil iṭiccol lilakkaṇamum, uriyiyalil uriccollilakkaṇamum, eccaviyaluḷ eñciyaṉa piṟavum uṇarttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivvakiyiṉāl ivvatikārattiṉ iyalkaḷum oṉpatāyiṉa.



kiḷaviyākkam
kiḷavi-col ākkam-ātal. coṟkaḷ poruḷ mēl āmāṟuṇarttiṉamiyiṉ kiḷaviyākkameṉṉum peyart tāyiṟṟu eṉa iḷampūraṇarum, vaḻukkaḷintu coṟkaḷi amittuk koṇṭamiyāl kiḷaviyākkamāyiṟṟu eṉac cēṉāvariyarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum, coṟkaḷ oṉṟō ṭoṉṟu toṭarntu poruḷmēl ākum nilimiyik kūṟuvatu ivviyalātaliṉ kiḷaviyākkam eṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu eṉat teyvacciliyārum ivviyalukkup peyarkkāraṇaṅ kūṟiṉar. kiḷaviyatu ākkattikkūṟuvatu kiḷaviyākkam eṉa vēṟṟumit tokip puṟattup piṟanta aṉmoḻit tokiyākakkoṇṭār teyvacciliyār. ivviyaliṉ cūttiraṅkaḷ aṟupattiraṇṭeṉa iḷampūraṇar nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum, aṟupattoṉṟeṉac cēṉāvariyarum, aimpattoṉpateṉat teyvacciliyārum pakuttu uri kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
col uyartiṇiccol, aḵṟiṇiccol eṉa iraṇṭu vakippaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ uyartiṇiccol āṭūu vaṟicol, makaṭūu vaṟicol, pallōraṟicol eṉa mūvakippaṭum. immūṉṟiṉiyum muṟiyē āṇpāṟcol, peṇpāṟcol, palarpāṟcol eṉap piṟkālattār vaḻaṅkuvar. aḵṟiṇic col oṉṟaṟicol, palavaṟicol eṉa iru vakippaṭum. ivaṟṟi muṟiyē oṉṟaṉpāṟcol, palaviṉpāṟcol eṉap piṟkālattār vaḻaṅkuvar. aṟivārkkuk karuviyākiya col aṟicollāyiṟṟu.
uyartiṇiyeṉpatu maṟṟuḷḷa poruḷellāvaṟṟiṉum uyarvākiyaporuḷ eṉa vicēṭittu niṉṟamiyiṉ paṇput tokiyāmeṉṟum, uyarnta makkaḷ uyarāniṉṟa makkaḷ uyarum makkaḷ eṉa mūṉṟu kālamuṅ koḷvārkku viṉit tokiyumāmeṉṟum, makkaḷāvār orutaṉmiyaraṉṟi āṇ, peṇ, aliyeṉṉum vaṭivu vēṟṟumiyuṭiyarākaliṉ avarellāriṭattum potuvāka amintuḷḷa makkaṭṭaṉ miyik kuṟittu makkaḷ ivar eṉṉum potup poruṇmi uyartiṇiyāmeṉpataṟivittaṟku makkaḷeṉṉātu makkaṭ cuṭṭeṉṟāreṉṟum, makkaḷallāta uyiruṭiyaṉavum uyiril laṉavum, aḵṟiṇiyāmeṉpataṟivittaṟku avarala piṟa eṉṟareṉṟum kūṟuvar teyvacciliyār. uyartiṇiyallāta tiṇi aḵṟiṇiyātaliṉ altiṇi aḵṟiṇi yeṉṟāyiṟṟu.
uyirporuḷ, uyirilporuḷ eṉṉum aḵṟiṇip poruḷvaki yiraṇṭaṉuḷ uyirpporuḷ vakiyuḷ āṇ peṇ vēṟupāṭu kāṇappaṭumēṉum avvēṟupāṭu uyiruḷḷa vaṟṟuḷ cilavaṟṟiṟkum uyirillāta poruḷkaḷukkum iyiyā miyāl uyiruḷḷaṉa illaṉavākiya ellāvaṟṟiṟkum porunta oṉṟaṟicol, palavaṟicol eṉṉum iruvakic coṉ muṭipukaḷē vakukkap peṟuvaṉavāyiṉa.
makkaṭ piṟappilē tōṉṟip peṇtaṉmi mikuntum āṇ taṉmi kuṟintum āṇ peṇ eṉṉum iruvakiyuṅ kalantu niṟkum pēṭṭiṉikkuṟitta peyarccollum teyvattik kuṟitta peyarccollum irutiṇi aimpālkaḷuḷ iṉṉapāl eṉatterintu koḷḷutaṟkuk karuviyākiya īṟṟeḻuttiṉi (vikutiyiṉi) uṭiyaṉa alla. avitām uyartiṇip peyarāy niṉṟu āṇpāṟcol mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ vikutiyiṉiyē tam viṉikkīṟākappeṟṟu iṉṉapāl eṉa viḷaṅki niṟpaṉavām. ivaṟṟiṉ iyalpiṉi ivviyal ām cūttirattāl āciriyar teḷivupaṭuttukiṉṟār.
ṉakaramākiya meyyeḻutti iṟutiyākavuṭiyatu āṇ pāṟ collām. ḷakarameyyi iṟutiyākavuṭiyatu peṇ pāṟ collām. rakarameyyum pakaravuyirmeyyum mār eṉṉum collum ākiya immūṉṟaṉuḷ oṉṟi yiṟutiyākavuṭiya col palarpāṟ collām. tu, ṟu, ṭu eṉavarum mūṉṟeḻut tukkaḷuḷ oṉṟiyiṟutiyākappeṟṟacol oṉṟaṉpāṟ collām. a, ā, va eṉpavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟi yiṟutiyākappeṟṟatu palaviṉ pāṟ collām. ivvāṟu irutiṇikkaṇṇum aintupālum viḷaṅka iṟutiyil niṉṟolikkum ippatiṉō reḻuttum viṉic colliṭattētāṉ teḷivākap pulappaṭuvaṉa. ivi peyaroṭu varuvaḻit tiripiṉṟi aimpāli viḷakkum āṟṟaluṭiyaṉa alla. eṉavē irutiṇimaruṅkiṉ aimpālaṟiya īṟṟilniṉṟu icikkum patiṉōreḻuttum tōṟṟantāmē viṉiyoṭu varumē eṉṟār tolkāppiyaṉār. itaṉāl irutiṇi aimpālkaḷiyum oruvaṉ collakattu aṟiyumāṟu ivvāṟeṉa āciriyar viḷakkiṉami kāṇka.
irutiṇiyuḷ orutiṇiccol ēṉit tiṇic colloṭu muṭivatu tiṇivaḻu, orutiṇiyuḷ orupāṟcol attiṇiyiluḷḷa ēṉip pāṟcolloṭu muṭivatu pālvaḻu. taṉmi, muṉṉili, paṭarkkiyākiya mūviṭac coṟkaḷuḷ ōriṭaccol piṟaviṭac colloṭu muṭivatu iṭavaḻu iṟappu, nikaḻvu, etirvu eṉṉum mūṉṟu kālaṅkaḷuḷ oru kālattiṉik kuṟitta col ēṉik kālaccolloṭu muṭivatu kālavaḻu. viṉāvukku ēṟṟa viṭiyākātatu ceppuvaḻu. viṉāvutaṟkuriyatallāta poruḷippaṟṟi varuvatu viṉāvaḻu. oruporuṭkuriya vaḻakkuc col maṟṟoru poruḷmēṟ ceṉṟatu marapuvaḻu. iṅṅaṉam tiṇi, pāl, iṭam, kālam, ceppu, viṉā, marapu eṉṉum ivvēḻu vakiyālum coṟkaḷ vaḻuvāmaṟ kāttalē vaḻukkāttaleṉappaṭum. vaḻuvaṟkaveṉṟalum, vaḻuvamittalum eṉa vaḻukkāttal iruvakippaṭum. kuṟitta poruḷi ataṟkuriya collāṟ collukaveṉṟal vaḻuvaṟka veṉṟalām. kuṟitta poruḷukkuriya collaṉṟāyiṉum oruvāṟṟāl apporuḷ tarutaliṉ amittukkoḷka eṉa amitikūṟutal vaḻuvamittalākum. ivviyaliṉ kaka-ām cūttira mutalākavuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷ mēṟkūṟiya iruvakiyāṉum vaḻukkāppaṉavām.



vēṟṟumiyiyal
vēṟṟumi yilakkaṇam uṇarttiṉamiyāl itu vēṟṟumi yiyaleṉṉum peyaruyaṭiyatāyiṟṟu. kiḷaviyāk kattuḷ peyar, viṉi, iṭi, uriyeṉṉum nāṉku coṟkum potuvilakkaṇamuṇarttiṉār. appotuvilakkaṇattiṉit toṭarntu avaṟṟatu ciṟappilakkaṇaṅkūṟutal muṟi. āyiṉum vēṟṟumiyeṉpaṉa orucār peyarum iṭiccollumā taliṉ avaṟṟiṉ ilakkaṇamum potuvilakkaṇamātal karutik kiḷaviyākkattiṟkum peyariyalukkumiṭiyē vēṟṟumi yilakkaṇam uṇartta eṭuttukkoṇṭār. vēṟṟumi yilakkaṇameṉpatu oṉṟāyiṉum ciṟappuṭiya eḻuvaki vēṟṟumikaḷum avaṟṟatu mayakkamum eṭṭāvatākiya viḷivēṟṟumiyum taṉittaṉi yiyalkaḷāl uṇarttutaṟkuriya poruḷvēṟupāṭuṭimiyiṉ avaṟṟi muṟiyē vēṟṟumi yiyal, vēṟṟumi mayaṅkiyal viḷimarapu eṉa mūṉṟiyalkaḷāl uṇarttiṉār.
vēṟṟumiyiyaṟ pakutiyip patiṉēḻu cūttiraṅkaḷāka iḷampūraṇarum, irupattiraṇṭu cūttiraṅkaḷākac cēṉāvari yarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum, irupattoru cūttiraṅkaḷākat teyvacciliyārum pakuttu urikūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
peyarpporuḷi vēṟupaṭuttum urupukaḷ vēṟṟumi yeṉappaṭṭaṉa. ceyappaṭuporuḷ mutaliyaṉavākap peyarp poruḷi vēṟupaṭuttuṇarttaliṉ vēṟṟumiyāyiṉa eṉavum ceyappaṭuporuḷ mutalāyiṉavaṟṟiṉ vēṟupaṭuttup poruḷ māttira muṇarttaliṉ eḻuvāyum vēṟṟumiyāyiṟṟu eṉavum kūṟuvar cēṉāvariyar. poruṇmi cuṭṭal mutaliya āṟu poruḷiyuṅ kuṟittu avaṟṟāl tāṉ vēṟupaṭa niṟṟalāṉum muṭikkuñcollit tāṉ vicēṭittu niṟṟalāṉum eḻuvāyum vēṟṟumiyāyiṟṟu eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. oru poruḷi orukāl viṉimutalākkiyum orukāṟ ceyappaṭu poruḷākkiyum orukāl ēṟpatu ākkiyum orukāl nīṅka niṟpatu ākkiyum orukāl uṭiyatu ākkiyum orukāl iṭamākkiyum ivvāṟu tammiyēṟṟa peyarpporuḷi vēṟupaṭuttiṉamiyāl vēṟṟumiyeṉappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟum, mēl kiḷaviyākkattāl alvaḻittoṭar kūṟi iṉi vēṟṟumittoṭar kūṟukiṉṟā reṉṟum kūṟuvar teyvacciliyār.
peyar, ai, oṭu, ku, iṉ, atu, kaṇ, viḷieṉa vēṟṟumi yeṭṭeṉṟār tolkāppiyaṉār. vēṟṟumi ēḻeṉak koḷvōr viḷivēṟṟumiyi eḻuvāyuḷ aṭakkuvar. piṟitōr iṭiccolliyēlātu iyalpākiyum tāṉē tirintum niṟkum peyariṉ iṟuti viḷiyeṉappaṭum. paṭarkkic colliyum poruḷiyum muṉṉilic collum poruḷumāka vēṟṟumi ceyvatu viḷi vēṟṟumiyātaliṉ itaṉi eḻuvāyuḷ aṭakkātu vēṟākak koḷvatē tamatu tuṇipeṉpār,
vēṟṟumitāmē ēḻeṉamoḻipa
viḷikoḷvataṉkaṇ viḷiyōṭeṭṭē
eṉṟār tolkāppiyaṉār.
ettakiya toḻilum karuttā illāmal naṭipeṟātu. ātaliṉ ataṉicceytu muṭikkum viṉi mutaṟporuḷit taruvatāyt tiripillātu niṉṟapeyar eḻuvāy vēṟṟumiyeṉa mutaṟkaṇ vikkappaṭṭatu. viṉimutal orutoḻilic ceyyuṅkāl acceyalāṟ ṟōṉṟiya poruḷ ceyappaṭu poruḷeṉappaṭum. ittakiya ceyappaṭuporuḷākap peyarpporuḷi vēṟupaṭuttuvatu aiyurupātaliṉ aḵtu iraṇṭām vēṟṟumiyeṉappaṭṭatu. viṉimutal oru kāriyatticceytu muṭittaṟku iṉṟiyamiyātu vēṇṭap paṭuvatu karuvi. ittakiya karuvipporuḷākap peyarpporuḷi vēṟupaṭuttuvatu oṭuvurupātaliṉ atu mūṉṟām vēṟṟumi yeṉappaṭṭatu. oruvaṉ oru kāriyattik karuviyāṟ ceyvatu taṉakkum piṟarkkum utavutaṟ poruṭṭēyām. ivvāṟu tarappaṭum evvakip poruḷiyum ēṟṟukkoḷvatākap peyarpporuḷi vēṟupaṭuttuvatu kuvvurupātaliṉ atu nāṉkām vēṟṟumi yeṉappaṭṭatu. oruvaṉ oru poruḷip piṟarkkuk koṭukkuṅkāl apporuḷ avaṉi viṭṭu nīṅkutalik kāṇkiṉṟōm. ivvāṟu nīṅka niṟkum poruḷākap peyarpporuḷi vēṟupaṭuttuvatu iṉṉuru pātaliṉ aḵtu aintām vēṟṟumiyāyiṟṟu. oruvaṉiṭattu nīṅkiya poruḷi yēṟṟukkoṇṭavaṉ apporuḷit taṉ ṉuṭiyatu eṉak kiḻami (urimi) pārāṭṭak kāṇkiṟōm, ittakiya kiḻamip poruḷākap peyarp poruḷi vēṟu paṭuttuvatu atu vurupātaliṉ aḵtu āṟām vēṟṟumiyāyiṟṟu. mēṟkūṟiya ellā nikaḻccikkum iṭam iṉṟiyamiyātatu. taṉṉiyēṟṟa peyarpporuḷi iṭapporuḷāka vēṟupaṭuttuvatu kaṇṇurupātaliṉ aḵtu ēḻām vēṟṟumi yāyiṟṟu. ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟākap peyarpporuḷi etir mukamākkuvatu viḷi vēṟṟumiyātaliṉ aḵtu eṭṭām vēṟṟumiyeṉa iṟutik kaṇ vikkappaṭṭatu.
mēṟkūṟiya eṭṭu vēṟṟumikaḷiyum muṟiyē peyar vēṟṟumi, aikāra vēṟṟumi, oṭu vēṟṟumi, kuvvēṟṟumi, iṉ vēṟṟumi, atu vēṟṟumi, kaṇ vēṟṟumi, viḷi vēṟṟumi eṉap peyar tantu vaḻaṅkutalum, ivaṟṟuḷ aikāra vēṟṟumi mutal kaṇ vēṟṟumi īṟākavuḷḷa āṟiyum muṟiyē iraṇṭāvatu, mūṉṟāvatu, nāṉkāvatu, aintāvatu, āṟāvatu, ēḻāvatu eṉa eṇ muṟiyāṟ peyariyiṭṭu vaḻaṅku talum āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār kālattum avarkku muṉṉum vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻiyaṟ kuṟiyīṭukaḷākum. ivvuṇmi ivvēṟṟumiyiyaṟ cūttiraṅkaḷāl naṉku viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. ivviyalil eḻuvāy vēṟṟumi mutalāka ēḻām vēṟṟumiyīṟākavuḷḷa ēḻu vēṟṟamikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ viritturikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ēḻuvēṟṟumiyiṉ urupum urupuniṟkum iṭamum ataṉ poruḷum apporuḷiṉ vakikaḷum ākiyavaṟṟi ivviyalil āciriyar muṟiyē viḷakkic celkiṉṟār. iṅṅaṉam ēḻu vēṟṟumikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟumukamākap payaṉili kōṭalum, urupēṟṟalum, kālantōṉṟāmiyum ākiya immūṉṟum peyarkkuriya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ eṉpataṉi āciriyar uyttuṇara vittuḷḷār.
mēṟkūṟiya vēṟṟumikaḷiṉ poruḷvakiyi virit turikkuṅkāl kāppiṉ oppiṉ ūrtiyiṉ iḻiyiṉ eṉpaṉa mutalāka ivviyaliṟkūṟiya poruḷkaḷēyaṉṟi ipporuḷōṭu poruntittōṉṟum ellāc coṟkaḷum koḷḷutaṟkuriyaṉa eṉpati ivviyaliṉ puṟaṉaṭic cūttirattāl āciriyar teḷivupaṭuttukiṉṟār.



vēṟṟumi mayaṅkiyal
oru vēṟṟumikkuriya urupu maṟṟoru vēṟṟumiyōṭu mayaṅkuvatu urupu mayakkam, oru vēṟṟumikkuriya poruḷ maṟṟoru vēṟṟumiyiṟ ceṉṟu mayaṅkuvatu poruḷ mayakkam. ivviruvaki mayakkattiṉiyuṅ kūṟuvatu ivviyal. ataṉāl itu vēṟṟumi mayaṅkiyaleṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. vēṟṟumikkuc colliya ilakkaṇattiṟ piṟaḻntu vaḻuvāy amittukkoḷḷappaṭuvaṉavum piṟavumāka vēṟṟumiyoṭu toṭarpuṭiya vitikaḷ cila īṇṭukkūṟappaṭutaliṉ vēṟṟumi mayaṅkiyaleṉṉum ippeyar paṉmi nōkkic ceṉṟa kuṟi yeṉṟum itaṉkaṇ yātaṉurupiṟ kūṟiṟṟāyiṉum eṉṟa cūttirattāl urupu mayakkamuṇartti ēṉic cūttiraṅkaḷāṟ poruḷ mayakkamuṇarttiṉāreṉṟuṅ kūṟuvar cēṉāvariyar.
ivviyaliluḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷi 35-āka iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum, 34-ākac cēṉāvariyarum, 33-ākat teyvacciliyārum pakuttu uri kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. ivviyaliṉ toṭakkattē karumamallāc cārpeṉ kiḷavi (1) eṉpatu mutal accak kiḷavikku (17)eṉṉum cūttira muṭiya vēṟṟumip poruḷ mayakkam uṇartti aṉṉapiṟavum (18) eṉpataṉāl ataṟkup puṟaṉaṭiyuṅkūṟi muṭittār. poruḷ mayakkamāvatu ovvoru vēṟṟumikkum uriyaṉavāka vēṟṟumiyiyaliṟ collappaṭṭa kāttal ottal mutaliya avvavvēṟṟumiyiṉ poruṭ pakutikaḷ tamakkuriya vēṟṟumip poruḷiviṭṭu nīṅkātu piṟitoru vēṟṟumiyiṉ poruḷiṉ kaṇṇē ceṉṟu mayaṅkutalām. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka oṉṟi nōkkuvōm. iraṇṭām vēṟṟumikkuc collappaṭṭa kāppiṉ oppiṉ eṉavarum poruṭ pakutikaḷuḷ cārpu poruṇmiyum oṉṟākum. atu karumac cārpum karumamallāc cārpum eṉa iraṇṭu vakippaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ karumac cārpāvatu tūṇiccārntāṉ eṉṟāṟpōla oṉṟiyoṉṟu meyyuṟṟuc cārtalākum. karumamallāc cārpeṉpatu aracaric cārntāṉ eṉṟāṟpōla oṉṟiyoṉṟu meyyuṟutaliṉṟi varuvatākum. ivaṟṟuḷ karumamallāta cārpuporuṇmi taṉakkuriya iraṇṭām vēṟṟumiyākiya ceyappaṭu poruḷil nīṅkātu aracarkaṭ cārntāṉ eṉa ēḻām vēṟṟumikkuriya iṭap poruḷilum mayaṅkiṉami kāṇalām. ivvāṟē tol kāppiyaṉār kūṟiya ēṉiya vēṟṟumip poruḷ mayakkaṅkaḷiyum pakuttuṇarntu koḷḷutal payilvōr kaṭaṉākum.
iṉi urupu mayakkamāvatu oru vēṟṟumikkuriya urupu taṉakkuriya vēṟṟumip poruḷi viṭṭup piṟitoru vēṟṟumip poruḷiṟ ceṉṟu mayaṅkutal nāṇaṟ kiḻaṅku maṇaṟku īṉṟa muḷi eṉpuḻi nāṇaṟ kiḻaṅku maṇaliṭattē tōṟṟuvitta muḷiyeṉpatu poruḷātalāl, maṇaṟkaṇ eṉak kaṇṇurupu niṟka vēṇṭiya ēḻām vēṟṟumip poruḷiṭattē maṇaṟku eṉa nāṉkām vēṟṟumi yurupu mayaṅkiyateṉak koḷḷutal vēṇṭum. ivvāṟu oru toṭar enta vēṟṟumi yurupiṉāṟ collappaṭṭālum avvurupiṟ kēṟpap poruḷi māṟṟāmal poruḷukkēṟpa urupiṉi māṟṟik koḷḷutal vēṇṭumeṉpār,
		yātaṉurupiṟ kūṟiṟṟāyiṉum
poruḷ celmaruṅkiṉ vēṟṟumicārum
eṉṟār āciriyar, vēṟṟumi yurupukaḷ oṉṟum palavumākat toṭarntu aṭukki, muṭikkuñ colloṉṟāl muṭitalum, avvurupukaḷ oru toṭariṉ iṭiyilē yaṉṟi iṟutiyilum niṟṟalum, iṅṅaṉam iṟutiyilum iṭiyilum virintu niṉṟa ellā vurupukaḷum muṭikkuñ colloṉṟiṉāl muṭitalēyaṉṟit taṉittaṉi muṭikkuñ collip peṟṟu muṭitalum, virintu niṟpatākiya tokānilit toṭariṉkaṇṇē niṉṟa avvurupukaḷ maṟintu niṟṟalum uḷaveṉpatum, toṭariṟutiyilē maṟintu niṟṟaṟkuriya urupukaḷ aiyurupum kaṇṇurupumēyaṉṟi ēṉiya allaveṉpatum, ku, ai, āṉ eṉṉum ivvurupukaḷ akaram peṟṟut tirivaṉaveṉpatum, oru vēṟṟumiyiṉ poruḷ citiyāmal ataṉkaṇ piṟitoru vēṟṟumiyiṉ urupu mayaṅki niṟṟal kūṭumeṉpatum, avvāṟu nāṉkām vēṟṟumi yurupu piṟavēṟṟumikaḷiṉ poruḷ citiyāmal mayaṅki niṟkum iṭaṅkaḷ ivi yeṉpatum, iṅṅaṉamē ēṉiyurupukaḷum vaḻakku naṭiyi yoṭṭi mayaṅki varutalāl avi kuṟṟamuṭiyaṉa allaveṉpatum ākiya urupu mayakkampaṟṟiya vitikaḷi ivviyalil 19 mutal 28 variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷāl āciriyar teḷivupaṭuttukiṉṟār.
ivvēṟṟumikaḷi muṭikkuñ collāyum ēṟkuñ collāyum varuvaṉa viṉiyum peyarumātaliṉ viṉic collāl aṟiyappaṭun toḻiṟkāraṇaṅkaḷiyum peyarccollāl aṟiyappaṭum poruḷ vēṟupāṭṭiṉiyum āciriyar ivviyaliṟ kūṟukiṉṟār. viṉi, ceyvatu, ceyappaṭuporuḷ, nilam, kālam, karuvi eṉṉum āṟuṭaṉē iṉṉataṟku, itu payaṉ eṉavarum iraṇṭiṉiyuṅ kūṭṭat toḻilukkuriya kāraṇaṅkaḷ eṭṭāmeṉṟum, ellāt toḻiṟkum ivveṭṭum varumeṉṉum iṉṟiyamiyāmi yilli; ivaṟṟuḷ cila toḻiṟkaṇ oṉṟiraṇṭu kuṟiyattakkaṉa vaḻakkiṉkaṇ kuṟintuvaru meṉṟum viṉikkuriya mutaṉili kaḷik kuṟittu āciriyar viḷakkiyuḷḷār.
oru poruḷiṉ iyaṟpeyar maṟṟoru poruḷukku āki varuvatu ākupeyarām. mutalukkuriya iyaṟpeyarāl ciṉip poruḷum, ciṉikkuriya peyarāl mutaṟporuḷum, iṭattiṉ peyarāl avviṭattu nikaḻ poruḷum, paṇpiṉ peyarāl paṇpu koḷporuḷum, kāraṇap peyarāl akkāraṇattāl iyaṉṟa kāriyap poruḷum, iraṇṭupeyartokku orucol nīrmippaṭṭu niṟṟalāl maṟṟoru poruḷum, ceyappaṭu poruḷi yuṇarttum peyarāl viṉimutalākiya poruḷum viḷaṅka niṟpaṉa ākupeyarkaḷām. aḷavuppeyarum niṟip peyarum aḷakkappaṭuvatum niṟukkappaṭuvatumākiya poruḷi yuṇarttiṉ aviyum ākupeyarēyām. eṉavē oru poruḷiṉ iyaṟpeyar apporuḷōṭu toṭarpuṭiya piṟitoru poruḷiṉ mēl ākivaruṅkāl ākupeyareṉappaṭumeṉpatu naṉku pulaṉām. iv vākupeyarkaḷ iyaṟpeyarāy niṉṟa kālattut tamakkuriya poruḷiṉ nīṅkātu niṉṟu tam poruḷiviṭṭup piriyāta toṭarpuṭiya poruḷi yuṇarttutalum, avvāṟu neruṅkiya toṭarpiṉṟi accoṟporuḷōṭu oruvāṟṟāṉ toṭarpuṭiyavēṟo ru poruḷiyuṇarttutalum eṉa ivviraṇṭiyalpiṉiyuṭiyaṉa eṉpar tolkāppiyar. eṉavē ivvākupeyarkaḷellām niṉṟāṅku niṉṟu tam iyaṟpeyarp poruḷiyum vēṟuṇartti niṟkum āṟṟaluṭiyaṉa veṉpatu peṟappaṭum. ivvāṟu iyaṟpeyarkaḷ tam poruḷōṭu toṭarpuṭiya vēṟoru poruḷmēl ākivaruṅkāl aṅṅaṉam ātaṟkuriya poruṭṭoṭarpu ai mutaliya aṟuvaki vēṟṟumikaḷiṉ iṭamāka niṉṟu tōṟṟumiyalpiṉatākum. ivvāṟu ākupeyarkaḷellām vēṟṟumip poruḷkaḷuṭaṉ neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiyaṉa eṉpati ārāyntaṟital vēṇṭu meṉpār vēṟṟumi maruṅkiṟ pōṟṟal vēṇṭum eṉṟār tolkāppiyaṉār. iṅṅaṉam āciriyar kūṟiyataṉiyuḷaṅ koṇṭu ivvākupeyarkaḷ eḻuvāy vēṟṟumi mayakkameṉṟuṇarka eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum, mutaliṟ kūṟuñ ciṉiyaṟi kiḷaviyum ciṉiyiṟkūṟum mutalaṟikiḷaviyum paṇpukoḷ peyarum irupeyaroṭṭum āṟām vēṟṟumip poruḷ mayakkam. piṟantavaḻik kūṟal ēḻām vēṟṟumipporuḷ mayakkam. iyaṉṟatu moḻitalum viṉimutalurikkuṅ kiḷaviyum mūṉṟām vēṟṟumipporuḷ mayakkam eṉat teyvacciliyārum kūṟiyavi ivaṇ karutat takkaṉavām. ākavē vēṟṟumip poruṇmayakkamākiya oppumikaruti ākupeyarilakkaṇam ivviyaliṉ iṟutikkaṇ kūṟappaṭṭa teṉpatu paḻiya uriyāciriyarkaḷiṉ karuttātal naṉku peṟappaṭum.
ākupeyarc cūttirattiṉkaṇvarum irupeyaroṭ ṭeṉpataṟkup poṟṟoṭi yeṉa utāraṇaṅkāṭṭiṉar iḷampūraṇar. ataṉiyuṇarnta cēṉāvariyar irupeyaroṭ ṭeṉpataṟku aṉmoḻit tokimēlvarum irupeyaroṭṭu eṉapporuḷ kūṟiyatōṭu tokiyāta luṭimiyāl eccaviyaluḷuṇarttappaṭum aṉmoḻit toki iyaṟkip peyar ākupeyar eṉṉum iruvakip peyaruḷ ākupeyareṉa oṉṟāyaṭaṅkutal paṟṟi īṇṭuk kūṟappaṭṭatu eṉa viḷakkamuṅ kūṟiyuḷḷār. poṟṟoṭi yeṉpatu aṉmoḻit tokiyāvataṉṟi ākupeyarākāteṉakkaṇṭuṇarnta nacciṉārkkiṉiyar irupeyaroṭṭeṉpataṟku aṉmoḻip poruḷmēl nillāta iru peyaroṭṭu eṉap poruḷkūṟi makkaṭcuṭṭu, eṉa ataṟku utāraṇamuṅ kāṭṭiṉār. makkaḷ+cuṭṭu eṉṉum irupeyarum oṭṭi niṉṟu makkaḷākiya cuṭṭappaṭum poruḷ eṉṉum poruḷittantaṉa. itaṉkaṇ cuṭṭu eṉṉum peyar cuṭṭappaṭum poruḷiyuṇartti ākupeyarāy niṟpa, makkaḷ eṉṉum mutaṉmoḻi av vākupeyarp poruḷi vicēṭittu niṟka iṅṅaṉam irupeyarum oṭṭiniṉṟaṉavātaliṉ irupeyaroṭṭeṉṟār āciriyar. itaṉkaṇ piṉṉuḷḷa moḻiyē ākupeyarāy niṉṟatātaliṉ itaṉip piṉmoḻi yākupeyareṉpārumuḷar. iṉi poṟṟoṭi eṉṉum toṭariṉkaṇ poṉ eṉṉum mutalmoḻi ivvāṟu aṉmoḻit tokipporuḷi vicēṭittu nillātu toṭi yeṉṉum iyaṟpeyarp poruḷiyē vicēṭittu niṟka avviru coṟkaḷiṉ tokiyāṟṟalāl avviraṇṭumallāta maṟṟoru moḻiyiṉ poruḷ tōṉṟak kāṇkiṉṟōm. eṉavē makkaṭcuṭṭu eṉa ākupeyarāyvarum irupeyaroṭṭum poṟṟoṭiyeṉa aṉmoḻit tokimēl varum irupeyaroṭṭum tammuḷ vēṟeṉavē uṇartal vēṇṭum. irupeyaroṭṭeṉpatu, iraṇṭu peyar tokku oru col nīrmippaṭṭu maṟṟoru poruḷ taru peyarāki varuvatu eṉakkūṟit tuṭiyiṭi yeṉpatu tuṭipōṉṟa iṭiyiṉi yuṭiyāḷi yuṇartti ākupeyarāyiṟṟu eṉa utāraṇaṅkāṭṭi viḷakkiya teyvacciliyār, ākupeyarāvatu oṭṭuppaṭṭa peyarōṭu oṟṟumippaṭṭuvarum eṉṟum aṉmoḻit tokiyāvatu apporuḷiṉ vēṟupaṭṭu varumeṉṟum attaṉmi yuṭiyatātal aṉmoḻi eṉṟa collālēyē viḷaṅku meṉṟum avviraṇṭiṟkum vēṟupāṭu kāṭṭiṉār. eṉiṉum avar irupeyaroṭṭāku peyarkkukkāṭṭiya tuṭiyiṭi yeṉpatum cēṉāvariyar kāṭṭiya poṟṟoṭi yeṉpatupōla iraṇṭu peyaruntokka tokiyāṟṟalāl ataṉiyuṭiyāḷi yuṇarttiya aṉmoḻi tokku niṉṟateṉak koḷḷutaṟkum iṭamuṇṭātaliṉ ataṉi orutaliyāka ākupeyareṉat tuṇitaṟkilli. ākupeya reṉṟum aṉmoḻittokiyeṉṟum vēṟuvēṟu ilakkaṇamuṭiyaṉavāka āciriyar kūṟutalāl ākupeyarum aṉmoḻit tokiyum tam poruḷuṇarttātu piṟitu poruḷuṇarttalāṉ okkumāyiṉuṅ ākupeyar oṉṟaṉ peyarāṉ ataṉōṭu toṭarpuṭiya piṟitu poruḷiyuṇartti orumoḻik kaṇṇatāy varumeṉṟum aṉmoḻittoki attakiya toṭarpetuvum vēṇṭātu irumoḻiyun tokka tokiyāṟṟalāl piṟitu poruḷuṇartti irumoḻikkaṇ varumeṉṟum iviyē iraṇṭiṟkum vēṟupāṭeṉṟum civañāṉamuṉivar kūṟuṅ koḷkiyē āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉārkku uṭaṉpāṭātal peṟṟām.


viḷimarapu
viḷi vēṟṟumiyatu ilakkaṇam uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ viḷimarapeṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. itaṉkaṇ uḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷ 37. teyvacciliyār 36-ākap pakuttu uri kūṟiyuḷḷār.
viḷi vēṟṟumiyāvatu paṭarkkip peyarpporuḷi etir mukamākkutalip poruḷākavuṭiyatākum. īṟutirital, īṟṟayal nīṭal, piṟitu vantaṭital, iyalpātal eṉpaṉa viḷi vēṟṟumiyiṉ urupukaḷākak koḷḷattakkaṉa. viḷi koḷḷum peyarkaḷ iviyeṉavum viḷi koḷḷāp peyarkaḷiviyeṉavum āciriyar ivviyalil uṇarttukiṉṟār.
i, u, ai, ō, ṉa, ra, la, ḷa eṉpavaṟṟi yiṟutiyākavuṭiya uyartiṇip peyarkaḷ viḷi koḷḷum peyarkaḷām. ēṉip peyarkaḷ viḷiyēlā. tāṉ, yāṉ, nīyir eṉpaṉavum cuṭṭuviṉāp peyarkaḷum ta, na, nu, e eṉpavaṟṟi mutalākavuṭiya kiḷip peyarkaḷum ivipōlvaṉa piṟavum viḷivēṟṟumiyōṭu poruntāta peyarkaḷām.
īṟutirital:
ikaravīṟu īkāramākavum aikāravīṟu āy eṉavum muṟip peyarīṟṟu aikāram ākāramākavum aṇmi viḷiyāyiṉ akara mākavum tiriyumeṉṟum, toḻiṟ peyar paṇpukoḷpeyar eṉpavaṟṟiṉ iṟutiyiluḷḷa āṉ vikutiyum āḷ vikutiyum āy vikutiyākat tiriyumeṉṟum, ar, ār eṉpaṉa īr eṉat tiriyumeṉṟum kūṟuvar āciriyar.
īṟṟayal nīṭal:
lakara ḷakara vīṟṟu uyartiṇip peyarkaḷ īṟṟayal nīṇṭu viḷiyēṟpaṉa.
piṟitu vantaṭital:
ōkāravīṟṟup peyarum kuṟṟiyalukaravīṟṟup peyarum lakara ḷakaravīṟṟu muṟip peyarum rakaravīṟṟut toḻiṟ peyarum paṇpukoḷ peyarum iṟutiyil ēkāram peṟṟu viḷippaṉa.
iyalpātal:
ikaravīṟṟu aḷapeṭip peyarum aṇmiyiluḷḷāri yaḻikkum peyarum āṉīṟṟup peyarum ṉa, ra, la, ḷa eṉpavaṟṟi yiṟutiyākavuṭiya aḷapeṭip peyarkaḷum iyalpāy niṉṟu viḷiyēṟpaṉavām.
i, u, ai, ō, ṉa, ra, la, ḷa eṉṉum eḻuttukkaḷi yiṟutiyāka vuṭiya aḵṟiṇi viravup peyarkaḷ mēl uyartiṇip peyarkkuc colliya muṟiyāl viḷiyēṟpaṉa veṉṟum, aḵṟiṇikkaṇ varum ellā vīṟṟup peyarkaḷum ēkāram peṟṟu viḷiyēṟpaṉa veṉṟum iṅkukkūṟappaṭṭa irutiṇip peyarkaḷum cēymi viḷikkaṇ varuṅkāl tattam māttiriyil nīṇṭolippaṉa veṉṟum kūṟuvar āciriyar.



peyariyal
itukāṟum alvaḻi vēṟṟumiyākiya toṭarmoḻi yilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiya āciriyar, iṉi attoṭarmoḻikku uṟup pākiya taṉimoḻi yilakkaṇaṅ kūṟat toṭaṅki mutaṟkaṇ peyarilakkaṇa muṇarttukiṉṟār. ataṉāl ita peyariya leṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. itaṉkaṇ 43-cūttiraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟi 41-cūttiraṅkaḷāka aṭakkuvar teyvac ciliyār.
ellāc coṟkaḷum poruḷ kuṟittu varuvaṉavē. collāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭa poruḷit terintukoḷḷutaṟkum collit terintukoḷḷutaṟkum accollē karuviyākum. col poruḷuṇarttum muṟi veḷippaṭunili, kuṟippunili yeṉa iruvakippaṭum. colleṉac ciṟappitturikkattakkaṉa peyarum viṉiyum eṉa iraṇṭēyām. iṭic collum uriccollum peyar viṉikaḷic cārntu tōṉṟuvaṉa eṉpar āciriyar.
