kantaḻiyeṉpatu tī; vaḷḷiyeṉpatu tiṅkaḷ; immuttī vaḻipāṭu kaṭavuḷvāḻttoṭu porunti varumeṉak kūṟuvārum, kantaḻiyeṉṉuñcol ittolkāppiyattil koṭinili vaḷḷiyeṉpavaṟṟiyaṭuttu mutalil kūṟappaṭāmiyālum iccol vēṟu paḻiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷil yāṇṭum kuṟikkappaṭāmiyālum, veṟiyaṟi ciṟappiṉ vevvāy vēlaṉ veṟiyāṭṭayarnta kāntaḷ eṉa āciriyar muṉṉar veṭcittiṇiyiṟ kuṟitta kāntaḷ eṉṉum collē ēṭeḻutu vōrāl kantaḻiyeṉat tavaṟākat tiritteḻutappaṭṭateṉ pārum eṉap pala tiṟattar urikoḷvōr. iṅkeṭuttuk kāṭṭiya viḷakkaṅkaḷ avaravaratu aṟiviṉ tiṟattāl nalintum valintum tirittuṅ kūṟappaṭṭaṉavātaliṉ ivi tol kāppiyaṉār karutiya poruḷi viḷakkuvaṉaveṉak koḷḷu taṟkilli.
kuṉṟāc ciṟappiṟ koṟṟavaḷḷi yeṉa muṉ vañcittiṇik kuriyatākac collappaṭṭa koṟṟavaḷḷi yeṉṉun tuṟiyum orōvaḻik kaṭavuḷ vāḻttoṭu poruntip pāṭāṇpāṭṭāy varutaluṇṭu.
koṭuppōrēttik koṭārppaḻittal mutalāka vēli nōkkiya viḷakkunili yīṟākac collappaṭṭaṉavum, vāyuṟi vāḻttu, ceviyaṟivuṟūu, puṟanili vāḻttu kikkiḷivaki eṉpaṉavum tuyileṭi nili mutalākap paricilviṭiyīṟākac collappaṭṭaṉavum, nāḷum puḷḷum nimittamum ōmpaṭiyum uṭpaṭa ulaka vaḻakkiṉkaṇṇē mūṉṟukālamum paṟṟi varuvaṉavum pāṭāṇtiṇikkuriya tuṟikaḷāmeṉa ivviyal 29, 30-ām cūttiraṅkaḷil tokutturittār āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār.
iṅṅaṉam akattiṇiyēḻaṟkum puṟamāy nikaḻum puṟattiṇikaḷ veṭci mutal pāṭāṇ iṟutiyāka ēḻeṉak kūṟiyirukkavum, paṉṉirupaṭalam puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāli mutaliya nūlkaḷiyiyaṟṟiya piṟkālattu āciriyarkaḷ pakivaruṭiya pacukkaḷik kavartal veṭci, aṅṅaṉam kavarappaṭṭa pacukkaḷi mīṭṭal karanti, pakivar nāṭṭiṉmēṟ paṭiyeṭuttuc cellutal vañci, aṅṅaṉam tamnāṭṭi nōkkivarum paṭiyi etir ceṉṟu taṭuttu niṟuttutal kāñci, tammuṭiya matilik kāttal nocci, pakivaruṭiya matilivaḷittal uḻiñi, pakivaroṭu porutal tumpi, pakivari vellutal vāki, oruvaṉuṭiya pukaḻ, koṭi, taṇṇaḷi mutaliyavaṟṟip pukaḻntu pāṭutal pāṭāṇ, mēṟkūṟiya puṟattiṇik kellām potuvāyuḷḷa ceyal muṟikaḷ potuviyal, orutalik kāmam kikkiḷi, ovvākkāmam peruntiṇi eṉap paṉṉiraṇṭu tiṇikaḷākap pakuttum, ivaṟṟuḷ mutalaṉa ēḻum puṟam eṉavum iṟutiyiluḷḷa kikkiḷi peruntiṇi akappuṟa meṉavum iṭiyiluḷḷa mūṉṟum puṟappuṟameṉavum vakippaṭuttiyum, ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. ippakuppu muṟiyiṉit tolkāppiya vuriyāciriyarkaḷākiya iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum puṟattiṇiyiyalurip pakutikaḷil āṅkāṅkē maṟuttuḷḷārkaḷ. eṉiṉum ittakiya vēṟupāṭukaḷi muṉṉōr nūliṉ muṭipu oruṅku ottalum tiripu vēṟuṭittātalum eṉa iruvēṟu vakippaṭa nikaḻum vaḻinūl cārpu nūlkaḷil marapu nili tiriyātu viravum poruḷkaḷāka amittukkoḷḷutalē muṟiyākum.
akam puṟam eṉap pakuttavaṟṟit tammuḷ vēṟupāṭu nōkki akam akappuṟam puṟam puṟappuṟam eṉa nāṉkākap pakuttalum, veṭcittiṇi, uḻiñittiṇikaḷiṉ maṟutali viṉiyi vīṟṟu viṉiyātalum vēṟṟuppūc cūṭutalumākiya vēṟupāṭupaṟṟi vēṟutiṇiyāka vitteṇṇutalum iṉṉōraṉṉavi piṟavum tiripuṭiyavāyiṉum marapunili tiriyātaṉa eṉavum ivvuṇmiyuṇarātār paṉṉirupaṭala mutaliya nūlkaḷi vaḻīi

 vēṇṭiyavāṟē kūṟupa 
eṉavum varum civañāṉamuṉivar kūṟṟu ikkaruttiṉip pulappaṭuttuvatākum.
āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār kikkiḷi mutalākap peruntiṇi yiṟutiyākavuḷḷa ēḻutiṇikaḷiyum akameṉak koṇṭārāyiṉum avaṟṟuḷ akameṉac ciṟappittaṟkuriyaṉa naṭuvē eṇṇappaṭṭa aintiṇikaḷumē eṉavum, avvintiṉ muṉṉum piṉṉumāka aṭutteṇṇappaṭṭa kikkiḷi peruntiṇiyeṉpaṉa oruvāṟṟāṉ avaṟṟiṉ puṟattavākak koḷḷatakkaṉa eṉavuṅ karutiṉāreṉpataṟku, makkaḷ nutaliya akaṉintiṇiyuñ, cuṭṭiyoruvar peyar koḷappeṟār, puṟattiṇi maruṅkiṟ poruntiṉallatu, akattiṇi maruṅkiṉ aḷavutalilavē eṉavarum akattiṇiyiyaṟ cūttiraṅkaḷē cāṉṟātaliṉ avvirutiṇikaḷiyum akappuṟameṉap piṉṉuḷḷōr pakuttami tollāciriyar karuttukku muraṇākāmi kāṇka.



kaḷaviyal
kaḷavoḻukkam uṇarttiṉamiyāl kaḷaviyaleṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. kaḷavāvatu aṉpu, aruḷ, aṟivu, aḻaku, kuṭippiṟappu mutaliyavaṟṟāl ottu viḷaṅkun talivaṉum taliviyum nallūḻiṉ ceyalāl tāmē etirppaṭṭu, ulakattāraṟiyātu maṟintoḻukutal. aimperum pātakaṅkaḷu ḷoṉṟākap pēcappaṭuṅ kaḷaveṉpatu piṟarkkuriya poruḷi vañcaṉiyāṟ kavarntukoḷḷutalākiya kuṟṟamākum. iḵtu attaṉmiyataṉṟi otta aṉpuṭiya kaṉṉiyari avartam icivaṟintu cuṟṟattāraṟiyātu kātalāl uḷaṅkalantu paḻakum peruṅkēṇmi yātalāl ciṟappuṭiya aṟameṉavē koḷḷappaṭum. otta aṉpuṭiya oruvaṉum oruttiyumākiya iruvarum tamatu neñcak kalappiṉip piṟaraṟiyātapaṭi ulakar muṉ muṟin toḻukiṉarākaliṉ, karantavuḷḷattarākiya avviruvaratu oḻukalāṟu, kaḷaveṉṟa collāl vaḻaṅkappaṭu vatāyiṟṟu. ikkaḷaviṉi maṟintavoḻukkam, maṟi, arumaṟi yeṉṟa coṟkaḷāl vaḻaṅkuvar āciriyar.
mēl kikkiḷimutaṟ peruntiṇi iṟuvāyāka eḻu tiṇiyōti avaṟṟiṉ puṟattu nikaḻum tiṇikaḷum ōtippōn tār. avveḻutiṇiyiṉum orutalivēṭkiyākiya kikkiḷiyum oppil kūṭṭamākiya peruntiṇiyum oḻittu, iruvar aṉpum otta nilimiyākiya naṭuvaṇintiṇik kaṇṇum puṇarppum, piritalum iruttalum, iraṅkalum, ūṭalumākiya uripporuḷ, kaḷavu kaṟpu eṉṉum iruvakik kikōḷiṉum nikaḻumātaliṉ avviruvakik kikkōḷiṉuṅ kaḷavākiya kikōḷ ivvōttiṉuḷ uṇarttutalāṉ avaṟṟiṉpiṉ kūṟappaṭṭatu eṉa muṉṉuḷḷa iyalkaḷōṭu ivviyalukkuḷḷa toṭarpiṉi viḷakkiṉār iḷampūraṇar.
iṉpattiṟkup potuvilakkaṇam akattiṇiyiyaluṭ kūṟi, ataṟkiṉamākiya poruḷum aṟaṉum kūṟum puṟattiṇiyi ataṉ puṟattu nikaḻtaliṟ puṟattiṇiyiyaluṭkūṟi, īṇṭu avviṉpattiṉi virittuc ciṟappilakkaṇam kūṟutaliṉ, iḵtu akattiṇiyiyalōṭu iyipuṭittāyiṟṟu eṉṟār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. ivviyaṟ cūttiraṅkaḷi 51-āka iḷampūraṇarum 50-āka nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum pakuttu urikūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
aṉpiṉintiṇiyākiya ik kaḷavoḻukkattiṉik kāmappuṇarcci, iṭantalippāṭu pāṅkoṭutaḻāal, tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭam eṉa nālvakiyākap pakutturippar. aṉpuṭiyār iruvar muṟpiṟappiṉ nalviṉiyāl etirppaṭṭu oruvari yoruvar iṉṟiyamiyātavarāy aṉpiṉāl uḷamottalākiya neñcak kalappē kāmap puṇarcciyākum. iruvaratu uḷḷak kuṟippum muyaṟciyumiṉṟi nalviṉip payaṉākat taṉṉiyalpil nikaḻum ivvuṟaviṉi iyaṟkip puṇarcci, teyvappuṇarcciyeṉṟa peyarkaḷāl vaḻaṅkuvar muṉṉiyōr. iyaṟkip puṇarcci puṇarnta talimakaṉ piṟṟināḷum avviṭattiṟ ceṉṟu etirppaṭutal iṭantalippāṭākum. taliviyōṭu taṉakkuḷḷa uṟaviṉit talivaṉ taṉ uyirtōḻaṉākiya pāṅkaṉukkuc colli, nī eṉakkuttuṇiyāka vēṇṭumeṉa vēṇṭa, avaṉ kuṟivaḻic ceṉṟu talimakaḷ niṉṟa niliyi yuṇarntu vantuṇarttiyapiṉ ceṉṟu kūṭutal pāṅkaṟkūṭṭa meṉappaṭum. ikkaḷavoḻukkam nīṇṭa nāḷikkut toṭarntu nikaḻavēṇṭumeṉa virumpiya talivaṉ. talivikkuc ciṟantāḷākiya tōḻiyi irantu piṉṉiṉṟu avaḷ vāyilākat taliviyik kūṭal tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭamākum. ivi nāṉkum immuṟiyē nikaḻum.
iṉi, immuṟi nikaḻātu iṭiyīṭupaṭṭu varutalum uṇṭu. talimakaḷi yetirppaṭṭa kālattu aṉpuṭiyā rellārkkum iyaṟkippuṇarcci taṭiyiṉṟi nikaḻumeṉpataṟ killi. talimakaḷi yātāṉumōriṭattu etirppaṭṭa talimakaṉ, avaḷ kātaṟ kuṟippuṇarntu kūṭṭattiṟku iṭiyīṭu uṇṭāyavaḻi aṅkē ceṉṟa vēṭki taṇiyātu vantu, nēṟṟuk kaṇṭāṟpōl pōla iṉṟuṅ kāṇalākumō eṉa eṇṇi aṅkē maṟunāḷuñ cellutalum, talimakaḷum avvāṟē vēṭkiyāl aṭarppuṇṭu aṅkē varutalum ākiyavaḻip puṇarcci nikaḻum. aṅkē āyattārālō piṟarālō iṭiyīṭu paṭṭaviṭattut talivaṉ taṉ varuttattiṉip pāṅkaṉukku uṇartti, avaṉ talimakaḷ  niṉṟa niliyaṟintuvantukūṟa aṅkē ceṉṟum kūṭuvaṉ. av viṭattum iṭiyīṭupaṭiṉ tōḻivāyilāka muyaṉṟeytuvaṉ. iṉi oru kūṭṭamum nikaḻātu aṅkuṇṭākiya vēṭki iruvarkkun taṇiyātu niṉṟu maṇañceyta piṉṉark kūṭutalum uriyaṉ. ivvakiyiṉāl ikkaḷavoḻukkam mūvakip paṭumeṉṟār iyampūraṇar.
ellāvuyirkkum uriya iṉpavuṇarvum, avviṉpattiṟkuk kāraṇamākiya poruḷum, apporuḷiṉi yīṭṭutaṟkuriya varampākiya aṟamum eṉac collappaṭṭu aṉpiṉāl nikaḻum aintiṇikkaṇ nikaḻum kāmak kūṭṭattiṉi ārāyuṅkāl, maṟiyōriṭattu ōtappaṭṭa eṇvaki maṇattuḷḷum tuṟiyami nalyāḻt tuṇimiyōrākiya kantaruvaratu oḻukalaṟṟiṉi yokkum eṉpar āciriyar. kantaruvarāvār nalla yāḻamittu ici pāṭutalil vallavareṉṟum avartām ekkālattum āṇum peṇṇumāka iṇintē cellum iyalpiṉareṉṟum kūṟupavātaliṉ, avarkaḷit tuṟiyami nalyāḻttuṇimi yōr eṉṟār tol kāppiyaṉār. avarkālattē vaṭavar nākarikam tamiḻakattil mella mellap paravat talippaṭṭamiyāl maṟiyōrtē ettu maṇamuṟikkum teṉṟamiḻnāṭṭu maṇamuṟikkumuḷḷa oṟṟumi vēṟṟumikaḷi viḷaṅkavuṇarttal avaratu kaṭaṉāyiṟṟu. oruvaṉ oruttiyeṉṉum iruvariṭiyē yuṇṭākum kūṭṭuṟavi orutalik kēṇmiyākiya kikkiḷi. otta kēṇmiyākiya aṉpiṉintiṇi ovvākkēṇmiyākiya peruntiṇiyeṉa mūvakiyākap pakutturittāl tamiḻ marapu. piramam, piracāpattiyam, āriṭam, teyvam, kāntaruvam, acuram, irākkatam, picācam eṉa eṭṭuvakiyākap pakuttal vaṭanūl marapākum nāṟpatteṭṭāṇṭu piramacāriyaṅ kātta iḷiñaṉukkup paṉṉiraṇṭu vayatuṭiya kaṉṉiyi aṇikala ṉaṇintu koṭuppatu piramam. mittuṉa kōttirattāṉ makaḷ vēṇṭic ceṉṟāl maṟukkātu koṭuppatu piracāpattiyam. takutiyuṭiyāṉoruvaṉukkup poṉṉiṉāṟ pacuvum erutuñ ceytu avaṟṟiṭiyē peṇṇi niṟutti aṇikalaṉ aṇintu nīṅkaḷum ivipōṟ polintu vāḻmiṉ eṉa nīrvārttuk koṭuppatu āriṭam. vēḷvi ceyta āciriyaṉukku vēḷvit tīmuṉ kaṉṉiyit takkiṇiyākak koṭuppatu teyvam. kantaruva kumārarum kaṉṉiyarum tammuḷ etirppaṭṭuk kūṭiṉāṟpōṉṟu oruvaṉum oruttiyum tāmē yetirppaṭṭuk kūṭuvatu kantaruvam. kollumiyalpuṭiya erutiṉi aṭakkiyavaṉ ippeṇṇi maṇattaṟkuriyaṉ, villēṟṟiṉāṉ ivaḷi maṇattaṟ kuriyavaṉ, iṉṉatoru poruḷ tantāṉ ivaḷi maṇattaṟkuriyaṉ eṉa ivvāṟu colli, colliyavaṇṇañ ceytāṟkup peṇṇik koṭuppatu acurameṉappaṭum. taṉṉāl virumpappaṭṭa peṇṇi, avaḷ viruppattiṟkum cuṟṟattār viruppattiṟkum māṟāka valitiṟ kavarntu celvatu irākkatam eṉappaṭum. mūttāḷ, tuyiṉṟāḷ kaḷittāḷ ākiya makaḷirik kūṭutal picācam eṉappaṭum. maṟiyōrkkuriya nūliṟ collappaṭṭa ivveṭṭu maṇaṅkaḷiyum muṟiyē aṟanili, oppu, poruḷkōḷ, teyvam, yāḻōr kūṭṭam, arumporuḷ viṉinili, irākkatam, pēynili eṉa moḻipeyarttu vaḻaṅkuvar tamiḻar. iviyeṭṭum vaṭamoḻiyāḷarkkē yuriyaṉa veṉpār maṟiyōrtēettu maṉṟal eṭṭu eṉṟār tolkāppiyaṉār. eṉavē ivveṇvaki maṇamuṟikaḷum tamiḻarkkuriyaṉa alla eṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
maṟiyōrkkuriyavākac collappaṭṭa eṇvaki maṇattiṉuḷḷum acuram, irākkatam, picācam ākiya mūṉṟum, tamiḻar kūṟiya kikkiḷippāṟpaṭuvaṉa veṉṟum, piramam, piracāpattayam, āriṭam, teyvam eṉṟa nāṉkum peruntiṇiyāyaṭaṅkumeṉṟum, kantaruvameṉṟa oṉṟum aintiṇiyiṉpāṟpaṭumeṉṟum iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārk kiṉiyarum vakutturittuḷḷārkaḷ. aṉpoṭu puṇarnta aintiṇi maruṅkiṟ kāmakkūṭṭam, maṟiyōrtēettu maṉṟaleṭṭaṉuḷ tuṟiyami nalyāḻt tuṇimiyōrākiya kantaruvaratu iyalpiṉi yuṭiyateṉavē, kantaruva kumārarum kaṉṉiyarum tammuḷ etirppaṭṭuk kaṇṭu iyintāṟpōlat talivaṉum taliviyum tammuḷ etirppaṭṭup puṇarvatu ikkaḷavoḻukkamākumeṉak kantaruvattiṟkum kaḷavoḻuk kattiṟkumiṭiyē yaminta oṟṟumiyiṉi nacciṉārkkiṉiyar naṉku viḷakkiyuḷḷār.
oruvaṉum oruttiyumāka etirppaṭṭār iruvar, puṉalōṭum vaḻip puṟcāyntāṟpōlat tam nāṇamum niṟiyum iḻantu meyyuṟu puṇarcciyiṟ kūṭi makiḻum iyalpē kantaruva maṇamākum. iṅṅaṉam kūṭiṉōr tam vāḻnāḷ muḻutuṅ kūṭi vāḻvareṉṉum niyatiyilli. etirppaṭṭa aḷavil vēṭki mikutiyāṟ kūṭip piṉ aṉpiṉṟip pirintu māṟum varampaṟṟa niliyum ik kantaruvattiṟku uṇṭu. tamiḻar kūṟum kaḷavoḻukkamō iruvaruḷḷattum uḷniṉṟu curanta aṉpiṉ perukkiṉāl, tāṉ avaḷ eṉṉum vēṟṟumiyiṉṟi iruvarum oruvarāyoḻukum uḷḷap puṇarcciyēyākum. ivvuḷḷap puṇarcci nikaḻnta piṉṉar oruvariyoruvar iṉṟiyamiyā toḻukum uyirōraṉṉa ceyirtīr naṭpē cāntuṇiyum nilipeṟṟu vaḷarvatākum. uḷḷap puṇarcci nikaḻntapiṉ oruvariyoruvar piriviṉṟi yoḻukum aṉpiṉ tūṇṭutalāl ulakaṟiya maṇantu vāḻum kaṟpeṉṉun tiṇmi, tamiḻar oḻukalāṟākiya kaḷavoḻukkattiṉ muṭinta payaṉākum. ulakiyalil uḷavākum palavaki iṭiyūṟukaḷāl oruvari yoruvar maṇantukoḷḷa iyalāmal uḷḷappuṇarcci yaḷavē kūṭi vāḻntu piṉṉar iṟanta kātalarum ittamiḻakattu iruntaṉar. maṇimēkaliyiṟ kūṟappaṭum tarumatattaṉ vicāki yeṉṉum iruvarum yāḻōr maṇamākiya kantaruva muṟiyiṟ poruntiyavarkaḷ eṉat tammi nōkki ūrār kūṟiya paḻimoḻiyi vilakkit tam vāḻnāḷ muḻuvatum meyyuṟu puṇarcciyi virumpātu uḷḷap puṇarcciyaḷavil niṉṟu uyir tuṟanta varalāṟu iṅku niṉittaṟkuriyatākum.
kantaruva vaḻakkil meyyaṟu puṇarcci mutaṟkaṇ tōṟṟuvatu. ataṉ payiṟciyāl uḷḷap puṇarcci nilipeṟṟuc cākumaḷavum kūṭi vāḻtalum uṇṭu; uḷḷap puṇarcci tōṉṟātu tammetirppaṭṭārik kūṭi māṟutalum uṇṭu, eṉṟum piriyā niliyil niṉaṟa kaṭavātu aṉpiṉāṟ kūṭum uḷḷap puṇarcciyē kaḷavoḻukkattiṉ ciṟappiyalpākum. ituvē tamiḻiyal vaḻakkamākiya kaḷavukkum vaṭanūl maṇamākiya kantaruvattukkumuḷḷa uyir niliyāya vēṟupāṭākum. aṉpoṭu puṇarnta aintiṇi maruṅkiṟ kāmakkūṭṭam eṉat tolkāppiyaṉārum aṉpuṭi neñcan tāṅkalantaṉavē eṉap piṟacāṉṟōrum uḷḷap puṇarcci yoṉṟiyē kaḷavukkuriya ciṟappiyalpāka viritturittuḷḷārkaḷ. āriya maṇamākiya kantaruvattiṟkum tamiḻar oḻukalāṟākiya kaḷavoḻukkattiṟkum uḷḷa vēṟṟumiyiṉiyum tamiḻiyal vaḻakkameṉac ciṟappitturikkappaṭum kaḷavoḻukkattiṉ tūymiyiṉiyum āriya aracaṉ pirakattuṉukku aṟivikkum nōkkattuṭaṉ kapilar pāṭiya kuṟiñcippāṭṭu, kantaruvattiṟkum kaḷaviṟkumuḷḷa vēṟṟumiyiṉi iṉitu viḷakkuvatākum. innuṭpattiṉi naṉkuṇarntē kantaruvarkkuk kaṟpiṉṟi yamiyavum peṟum, īṇṭuk kaṟpiṉṟik kaḷavē amiyātu eṉa ivviraṇṭaṟku muḷḷa vēṟṟumiyiṉi viḷakkiṉār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
makaḷiri aḵṟiṇip poruḷākiya uṭimipōlak karutip piṟarpāṟ kēṭṭup peṟutalum koṭarāyiṉ cuṟṟat tārkkut terintō teriyāmalō vaṉmiyiṉāṟ kavarntu cēṟalumākiya ceyal muṟikaḷi maṇameṉak kūṟum vaḻakkam tamiḻarkkilli. ākavē ittakiya poruntā maṇa muṟi kaḷukku vaṭanūlkaḷiṟpōlat tamiḻ nūlkaḷil ilakkiyaṅ kāṇutalaritu. aṉpoṭu puṇarnta aintiṇi yeṉavē ataṉ muṉṉumpiṉṉuṅ kūṟappaṭṭa orutalik kāmamākiya kikkiḷi yilum ovvāk kāmamākiya peruntiṇiyilum iruvarpālum otta aṉpiṉik kāṇutalariteṉpatu peṟappaṭum. ittakiya poruttamillāta kūṭṭuṟavukaḷ ennāṭṭilum ekkālattum kāṇappaṭuvaṉavēyām. poruntāta ceyalkaḷik kuṟippākac cuṭṭi vilakkiyum poruttamuṭiya naṟceyalkaḷi veḷippaṭiyāka eṭutturittu viḷakkiyum makkaḷi nalvaḻippaṭuttuvatē ciṟappuṭiya nūliṉ marapākum. immarapiṉi yuḷattuṭkoṇṭu kikkiḷi peruntiṇikaḷik kuṟippākavum aṉpiṉi ntiṇiyi virivākavum kūṟuvar āciriyar.
oruvaṉum oruttiyum kūṭi vāḻum maṉiyaṟattiṉa payaṉāka avviruriyum piṟapputtōṟum cērttu vippatum pirittu vilakkuvatumākiya iruvaki ūḻiṉum, iruvaruḷḷamum eṉkālattum oṉṟi vāḻtaṟkēṟṟa nallūḻiṉ āṇiyāl, otta aṉpuṭiya talivaṉum taliviyum tammuḷ etirppaṭṭuk kāṇpar. aṉpu mutaliyavaṟṟāl talivaṉ mikkavaṉāyiṉuṅ kuṟṟamilli.  icceyti,
		oṉṟē vēṟē yeṉṟiru pālvayiṉ
oṉṟi yuyarnta pāla tāṇiyiṉ
otta kiḻavaṉuṅ kiḻantiyuṅ kāṇpa
mikkō ṉāyiṉuṅ kaṭivari yiṉṟē
eṉa varum kaḷaviyaṟ cūttirattāluṇarappaṭum. iṅṅaṉam oruvaṉum oruttiyum etirppaṭum mutaṟ kāṭcikku nallūḻiṉ āṇiyē kāraṇameṉpār uyarnta pāla tāṇiyiṉ eṉṟum, avvūḻiṉ āṇikkuk kāraṇamāvatu avviruvarum paṇṭip piṟappukkaḷiṟ payiliyatu keḻīiya naṭpeṉpār oṉṟiyuyarnta pālatāṇi yeṉṟum, pala piṟavikaḷilum paḻakiya aṉpiṉ toṭarcciyē oruvariyoruvar iṉṟiyamiyātavarākakkāṇutaṟkuriya kātaṟ kiḻamiyi vaḻaṅkiyateṉpār, ottakiḻavaṉuṅ kiḻattiyuṅkāṇpa veṉṟum kūṟiṉār tolkāppiyaṉār. talivaṉukkum talivikkum aminta otta paṇpukaḷāvaṉa: piṟappu, kuṭimi, āṇmi, āṇṭu, aḻaku, aṉpu, niṟi, aruḷ, uṇarvu, tiru eṉṉum ippattumākum. iyaṟkip puṇarccikkaṇ oruvari yoruvar etirppaṭṭaaḷavē vēṭkiyittūṇṭi niṟṟaṟkum avviruvarum paṇṭip piṟappiṟ payiliyatu keḻīiya naṭpaṉṟip piṟitu kāraṇamilliyeṉpatē tollāciriyar karuttākum. ōtta paruvattār oruvariyoruvar kaṇṭuḻi yellām puṇarcci vēṭki tōṟṟāmiyiṉ oṉṟiyuyarnta pālatāṇiyiṟ kāṇpa eṉṟār āciriyar. īṇṭukkāṇutal eṉṟatu taṉakkuc ciṟantārākak karututali.
talivi otta nalaṅkaḷāṟ ciṟantu tōṉṟiyavaḻi ivaḷ makkaḷuḷḷāḷkollō teyvamō eṉat talivaṉuḷḷattē aiyam tōṉṟum oppumiyiṟ kuṟivuṭiyaḷāyiṉ avviḻipē avaḷi iṉṉāḷeṉat teḷivikkumātaliṉ, anniliyil, aiyantōṉṟutaṟkiṭamilli. talivi taṉṉiṉum uyarnta talivaṉi nōkki ivaṉ teyvamō makaṉō eṉa aiyuṟṟāl avaḷuḷḷattil accantōṉṟumēyaṉṟik kāmavuṇarvu tōṉṟātu. ākavē iṅṅaṉam aiyappaṭuvāṉ talimakaṉē yeṉpar. talimakaḷi nōkki ivaḷ teyvamakaḷō eṉat talivaṉ aiyuṟṟa kālattu, avaḷ kūntalilaṇinta māliyiṭattē moytta vaṇṭukaḷum avaḷaṇinta aṇikalaṉkaḷum naṟumalarum avaḷ pāl tōṉṟum taṭumāṟṟamum kaṇṇimippum accamum attaṉmip piṟavum aiyattiṉik kaḷitaṟkuriya karuviyākum. iṅkeṭuttuk kāṭṭiya aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷik koṇṭu talimakaḷi iṉṉāḷeṉat tuṇinta talimakaṉ, avaḷatu karuttaṟiyātu avaḷi yaṇukuvāṉāyiṉ, acceyal poruntā voḻukkamākiya peruntiṇiyāy muṭiyum otta aṉpiṉāl nikaḻtaṟkuriya ikkaḷavoḻukkattiṟkut talimakaḷatu uḷak karuttit talimakaṉ uṇarntu koḷḷutalē mutaṟkaṇ vēṇṭappaṭuvatākum. aṅkē oruvarōṭoruvar uriyāṭutalum muṟiyaṉṟām. oruvar vēṭkipōla maṟṟavarkkum vēṭkiyuḷatākuṅ kollō eṉa aiyuṟṟu niṉṟa iruvaratu aṟiviṉiyum oruppaṭuttaṟku avviruvar kaṇkaḷum vēṭkiyiṉāl oruvar oruvarkkurikkuṅ kāmakkuṟippuriyām, kaṇṭa aḷavilēyē vēṭkitōṉṟi oruvaratu uḷḷak kuṟippiṉi maṟṟavar ēṟṟukkoṇṭa niliyilētāṉ kaṇṇiṉāl varum ikkuṟippu nikaḻum.
taṉṉittāṉ koṇṭoḻukum perumiyum, ceṉṟa viṭaṅkaḷil maṉattic cellaviṭātu tīmiyiṉ nīkki naṉṟiṉ pāluykkum nallaṟivum āṭavaratu ciṟappiyalpākum. taṉatu niṟi kāvalukku iṭiyūṟu nērumō veṉṉum accamum, peṇṇiyalpākiya nāṇamum, tāṉ mēṟkoṇṭa koḷkiyi nekiḻaviṭāmiyākiya maṭaṉum makaḷiratu ciṟappiyalpākum. ivviru tiṟattārum tammik kāvātu vēṭki mītūrnta niliyiṟpuṉa lōṭum vaḻip puṟcāyntāṟpōlat tamakkuriya ikkuṇaṅkaḷi nekiḻaviṭutal kūṭāmiyiṉ, tām etirppaṭṭa mutaṟkāṭcilēyē meyyuṟu puṇarccikku uṭaṉpaṭātu uḷḷap puṇarcci yaḷavē yoḻuki maṇantukoṇṭa piṉṉarē kūṭutal muṟiyākum.
oruvariyoruvar peṟal vēṇṭumeṉṉum uḷḷa nikaḻcciyum iṭiviṭātu niṉittalum uṇṇāmiyāluḷa tām uṭal melivum, taṉakku ākkamāvaṉa iviyeṉat taṉak kuḷḷē collik koḷḷutalum, nāṇattiṉ elli kaṭatta lum, kāṇpaṉavellām aṉpuṭiyār uruvākavē tōṟṟu talum, tammi maṟattalum, maṉa mayakkamuṟutalum, uyir nīṅki ṉāṟpōṉṟu uyirp paṭaṅkutalum ākiya ivi yoṉpatum uyirōraṉṉa ceyirtīr naṭpākiya kāmavuṇarviṉic ciṟappip paṉavātaliṉ, ivaṟṟik kaḷavoḻukkattiṟkuc ciṟantaṉavākac kūṟuvar muṉṉiyōr. vēṭki mutal cākkāṭīṟāka iṅkuc collappaṭṭavi nikaḻnta piṉṉarē meyyuṟu puṇarcci nikaḻumeṉpar.
taṉiyiṭattē talimakaḷi yetirppaṭṭa talimakaṉ, taṉatu perumiyum uriṉum nīṅka vēṭki mītūrtalāl meyyuṟu puṇarcciyi virumpiṉāṉāyiṉum talimakaḷpāl tōṉṟum accamum nāṇum maṭaṉum ataṟkut taṭiyāy niṟpaṉavām. attaṭi nīṅkutaṟ poruṭṭut talimakaḷi muṉṉilippaṭuttic cila kūṟutalum, tāṉ collum colliṉvaḻi avaḷ niṟkum paṭi cila kūṟutalum, avaḷatu, nalattiṉi yeṭutturittalum, atu kēṭṭatalimakaḷpāl muṟuvaṟ kuṟipput tōṉṟi niṟṟaliyaṟitalum, taṉ akattē nikaḻum nōyāl puṟattu nikaḻum taḷarviṉik kuṟippāl viḷakkutalum, taṉṉuḷḷattu vēṭki mītūrtali nilippaṭac collutalum, talimakaḷ uḷḷap paṇpiṉit tāṉ aṟinta teḷiviṉit taṉ maṉattakattē tērntu veḷippaṭuttalum ākiya ivvuriyāṭalkaḷ talivaṉpāl nikaḻvaṉavām.
perumiyum uraṉumuṭiya talivaṉ, kātal veḷḷam puraṇṭōṭat taṉakkuc ciṟantāḷeṉat teḷinta taliviyiṉ meyyit tīṇṭip paḻakutalum, viḷiyāṭṭiṉkaṇ avaḷ nalattip pārāṭṭi yurittalum, avaḷ niṉṟa iṭatti neruṅki niṉṟu aṉpoṭu taḻīiya coṟkaḷic collutalum, talivaṉatu ūṟṟuṇarvu eṉṟum payilāta taṉ melliyal meyyiṟ paṭa nāṇamuṭiyaḷākiya talivi, aṅkuḷḷa kompum koṭiyumākiyavaṟṟit taṉakkuc cārpākak koṇṭu maṟintu olki niṟka, atukaṇṭa talivaṉ, ivvūṟṟiṉpattiṟku iṭiyūṟāy niṉ maṉattu nikaḻntavi yāviyeṉa viṉavi niṟṟalum, avaḷi meyyuṟutaṟkiyalum kālam vāykkāmiyi yeṇṇi varuntutalum, piṉṉar avaḷi meyyuṟutalum, mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuṭaṉ iṉpan tiḷittaliyum virivākap peṟṟaviṭattu, niṉṉip piriyēṉ eṉat talivikkut teḷivuri pakartalum ākiya eṭṭuvakik kūṟṟum, muṉkūṭṭam peṟṟa iṭattiṉiyē mīṇṭun talippaṭṭuk kūṭi makiḻtalum, talimakaḷip pirinta vaḻik kalakkamuṟutalum, eṉṟum nili niṟpatākiya illaṟatti mēṟkoḷḷa niṉintu mēl nikaḻvaṉavaṟṟi yeṭutturikku miṭattum taṉpāl cōrvu mikutiyālum kātal mikutiyālum nērvuṟṟa paḻipāvaṅkaḷi yeṭuttukkāṭṭi iṭitturikkum uyirt tōḻaṉākiya pāṅkaṉ taṉatu kaḷavoḻukkattiyēṟṟu uṭaṉpaṭṭaviṭattum talimakaḷāl virumpappaṭṭa uyirttōḻiyi vāyilākap peṟṟu avaḷi irantu piṉṉiṉṟu avaḷ kūṭṭak kūṭuvēṉ eṉak karuti avvirattali mēṟkoḷḷumiṭattum ūrum pērum tāṉ iḻantaṉa piṟavum ākiyavaṟṟi viṉavu mukattāl taṉ maṉak karuttup pulaṉākat tōḻiyik kuṟiyirantu niṟkum pakutiyum, tōḻi ivaṉ kūṟukiṉṟa kuṟi taliviyik kuṟittatāka viruntateṉṟu avaḷmēl cērtteṇṇum niliyiṟ cila kūṟalum, palakāluñ ceṉṟu irattalum maṟṟiyavaḻiyum talivaṉ varuntik kūṟukiṉṟa colliṉit taliviyoṭu cārttik kūṟutaliṉ muṉṉuṟu puṇarcci muṟiyēyaṭika veṉavum talivi pētit taṉmiyaḷ eṉavum ivvāṟu oḻukutalāṟ kēṭuḷatām eṉavum ivvoḻukkam niṉ perumikku ēlātu eṉavum kūṟit tōḻi talivaṉi avviṭattiṉiṉṟum añci nīkkutalāluḷatāya varutta nilimiyum nōkki maṭalēṟuvēṉ eṉak kūṟutalum ākiya kūṟṟukkaḷ talivaṉpāl nikaḻvaṉavām.
talimakaḷatu iḷamip paṇpiṉit tōḻi eṭuttuk kūṟit talivaṉ avviṭattiruntu peyartta vaḻiyum, varuttattatiṉāl melikiṉṟami kūṟiyaviṭattum, talimakaṉ kuṟiyi maṟukkun tōḻi aṉputōṉṟa nakitta niliyilum, avaḷatu uṭampāṭṭiṉippeṟṟu makiḻumiṭattum, tāṉ cellum vaḻiyiṭi iṭiyūṟuṇṭāyaviṭattum eṉattōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattiṭattē talivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻtal iyalpākum.
aṉpuṟṟāriruvar tuṇiyāyk kūṭutaṟku nimittamākavaṉa paṉṉiraṇṭām. aviyāvaṉa. kāṭci, aiyam, tuṇivu eṉa muṉṉark kūṟiya mūṉṟum, kuṟippaṟitaliṉ payaṉāyt tōṉṟum vēṭki mutal cākkāṭīṟākac collappaṭṭa oṉpatumākum. ivi paṉṉiraṇṭumē aṉpoṭu puṇarnta aintiṇimaruṅkiṟ kāmak kūṭṭattiṟku nimittamāvaṉavām. ivaṟṟuḷ muṟkūṟiya kāṭci, aiyam, tuṇivu eṉpaṉa mūṉṟum aṉpiṉintiṇikkuriya tātalēyaṉṟi orutalik kāmamākiya kikkiḷikkuriya kuṟippukkaḷākavum amiyum. nōkkuvavellām aviyē pōṟal, maṟattal, mayakkam, cākkāṭu eṉap piṉṉarkkūṟiya nāṉku nilikaḷum otta aṉpāl nikaḻum vaḻi aintiṇi yātalēyaṉṟi ovvāk kāmattāl nikaḻumvaḻip peruntiṇik kuriya poruntā nilikaḷākavum karutappeṟum. mutal, karu, uriyeṉṉun tiṇik kūṟupāṭṭuṭaṉ poruntiya yāḻōr neṟiyiṉiyotta kāmakkūṭṭam vēṭki, orutaliyuḷḷutal, melital, ākkañceppal, nāṇuvariyiṟattal eṉṉum aintu nilikaḷiyun taṉakkuriya ciṟappu nilikkaḷaṉākak koḷḷum eṉpar āciriyar.
tōḻiyiṉ uṭampāṭupeṟṟut talimakaḷik kūṭiya talimakaṉ avaḷi maṇantukoḷḷumvariyum kūṟum poruḷkaḷit tokuttuk kūṟuvatu iruvakik kuṟipiḻippākiyaviṭattum eṉat toṭaṅkum ivviyaṟ cūttiramākum. itaṉit talivi kūṟṟākakkoṇṭu poruḷurippar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. taliviyiṉpāl nilipeṟṟuḷḷa nāṇamum maṭaṉum peṇmikku aṅka mātalāl kāmavoḻukkattiṉkaṇ kuṟippiṉālum iṭattiṉalu mallatu avaḷpāl vēṭki pulappaṭutalilli. vēṭki yuriyāta kaṇkaḷ yāṇṭumiṉmiyāl vēṭki kāraṇamāka accam nīṅkiṉālum nāṇamum maṭaṉumākiya iraṇṭum taliviyiṭattu eṉṟum nīṅkātuḷavām. talivaṉ puṇarcci karutik kūṟuñcolliṉ etirē icivillātārippōṉṟu kūṟutalē talivikku iyalpākum. iṉṉiṉṉa iṭaṅkaḷil talivi uriyāṭutaṟkuriyaḷ eṉpati ivviyal 21-ām cūttirattāl āciriyar virittuk kūṟiyuḷḷār.
iyaṟkip puṇarcci puṇarntu nīṅkiya taviṉ iṉṉa nāḷil niṉṉi maṇantukoḷvēṉ eṉac colliviṭṭut tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattiṟku muyalātu nīṅkiya niliyil, taṉṉik kaṇṭu tōḻi aiyuṟutaṟkēṟṟa kuṟippu taṉkaṇ tōṉṟātapaṭi maṟittoḻukiya talivi, talivaṉ varumaḷavum āṟṟātu varutta muṟṟa niliyilum, kaḷavoḻukkattil vantoḻukun talivaṉ cevilittāy mutaliyōri etirppaṭṭapoḻutum, ivvoḻukkattiṉi niṉ tōḻikkuc colluka eṉa avaṉ taṉakkuc colliya niliyilum tāṉē tōḻikkuk kūṟutaluṇṭu. uyirikkāṭṭilum nāṇam ciṟantatu; attakiya nāṇattiṉuṅ kaṟpuc ciṟappuṭiyatu eṉṉum muṉṉōr moḻiyi yuḷattuṭkoṇṭu, talivaṉ uḷḷaviṭattiyaṭiya niṉittalum ivvāṟu kuṟṟantīrnta naṟcoṟkaḷik kūṟutalumākiya ivi, talivi tāṉēkūṟuṅ kūṟṟiṉuḷ amitaṟkuriya poruḷvakiyeṉpar āciriyar.
kaḷavoḻukkattiṉkaṇ talivikku iṉṟiyamiyāta vaḷākiya tōḻi uriyāṭutaṟkuriya poruḷvaki yellā vaṟṟiyun tokuttuk kūṟuvatu, nāṟṟamum tōṟṟamum (kaḷavu-24) eṉat toṭaṅkuñ cūttiramākum. itaṉkaṇ tōḻi kūṟutaṟkuriyaṉavāka muppattiraṇṭu poruṇmikaḷ virit turikkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. iṅkē tōḻi kūṟuvaṉavāka āciriyar eṭuttōtip poruṭpakutikaḷi uṟṟunōkkuṅkāl, makkaḷ maṉa niliyi avartam tōṟṟam oḻukkamutaliyavaṟṟāl uyt tuṇarum maṉappayiṟciyum otta aṉpiṉarākiya talivaṉiyum taliviyiyum ulakiyal kūṟit tītorīi naṉṟiṉpā luykkum nallaṟivum, uḷḷak karuttaṟitalarumiyum, taṉ aṟiviṉ tiṟattip piṟaraṟiyātu oḻukum aṭakkamum, maṟipulappaṭāmal niṟuttum nalluḷḷamum, mācaṟṟa aṟavuṇarcciyum, ceyyat takuvaṉa aṟintu kūṟalum ākiya peṇmikkuṇaṅkaḷ muḻuvatum tōḻiyiṉiṭattu oruṅkamittiruttal pulaṉām. ivvāṟu poṟuttaṟkariya peruṅ kuṇaṅkaḷ yāvum oruṅku vāykkap peṟṟavaḷē tōḻiyeṉap pārāṭṭutaṟkuriyava ḷeṉpār, tāṅkaruñciṟappiṉ tōḻi eṉṟār tol kāppiyaṉār.
talivaṉatu kaḷavoḻukkam puṟattārkkup pulaṉākiya niliyil ūrār alar kūṟiya kālattum, taliviyiṉ vēṭki aḷaviṟanta niliyilum, avaḷatu vaṉappumikkut tōṉṟiya kālattum, talivaṉoṭu taliviyi orucērak kaṇṭa kālattum taliviyiṉ melivukkuriya kāraṇaṅkaḷik kaṭṭuvippittum kaḻaṅku pārttum kaṇṭaṟintu avaḷatu nōytīra vēlaṉiyaḻittu veṟiyāṭiya poḻutum, veṟiyāṭutalit tōḻi taṭuttu niṟuttiya niliyilum, kātal mikutiyāl talivaṉi niṉintu talivi kaṉavil araṟṟiya poḻutum cevilittāy tōḻiyi viṉavutaluṇṭu. iṉṉavāṟu nikaḻntateṉat tōḻi kūṟiyavaḻi ivviruvaratu oḻukalāṟṟiṉāl kuṭikkup paḻi viḷitalākāteṉat teyvatti vēṇṭutalum talivi talivaṉuṭaṉ pōyiṉāḷeṉṟaṟinta niliyil tōḻiyoṭu ārāyntu avviruvariyum maṉiyaṟattiṉkaṇ niṟuttaṟku muyalutalum avaḷ ceyal. talivaṉ maṇantukoḷḷātu talimakaḷip pirinta kālattu avaḷ kaṟpuvaḻippaṭṭu maṉikkaṇ amitiyāka aṭaṅkiyirukkum niliyiṉi yeṇṇiya niliyilum talivaṉatu kuṭippiṟappu tam kuṭippiṟappiṉōṭu okkum eṉa ārāytaṟkaṇṇum ivipōṉṟa piṟaviṭaṅkaḷilum cevili uriyāṭutaṟkuriyaḷ. ceviliyuṇarvuṭaṉ otta karuttuṭiyaḷāyiṉ naṟṟāykkum mēṟcollappaṭṭa kūṟṟukkaḷ uriyaṉavām.
taliviyiṉ meliviṉik kaṇṭu varuntiya ceviliyum naṟṟāyum, kuṟṟamaṟṟa ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya uyarntōrākiya aṟivarip paṇintu niṉṟu ivaḷatu melivu etaṉālāyiṟṟu eṉa viṉavi niṟpar. atu kēṭṭa periyōr, mukkāla nikaḻcciyiṉiyum tām oruṅkuṇarum nuṇṇuṇarvuṭi yōrātaliṉ, taliviyoṭu talivaṉukkuṇṭākiya toṭarpiṉi veḷippaṭac collutal marapaṉmiyāṉum nikaḻntati maṟittal vāymikku māṟātalāṉum nummakaḷ talimakaṉ aṟiyā aṟiviṉiyuṭiyāḷ eṉa aiyak kiḷaviyāl maṟumoḻi kūṟuvar. etirkālattil taṉṉi maṇattaṟkuriya kaṇavaṉālum aṟiyappaṭāta pēraṟiviṉiyuṭiyāḷ nummakaḷ eṉa oru poruḷum, taṉ kaṇavaṉākiyoḻukum talivaṉālum aṟiyappaṭāta aṟivurimipūṇṭu mayaṅkukiṉṟāḷ. ivaḷ taṉṉaṟiviṟ ciṟitum mayakkamilaḷ, avaṉ poruṭṭu mayaṅkukiṉṟāḷ eṉa maṟṟoru poruḷum tarum niliyil iṅṅaṉam aiyuṟakkūṟiya aṟivaratu coṟporuḷi yuyttuṇarntu talivaṉ taliviyākiya iruvariṭiyē yaminta toṭarpiṉic ceviliyum naṟṟāyum aṟintukoḷḷutalum uṇṭu.
vēṟu vēṟākat tammuḷ kātal ceytoḻukum aṟivillā tārippōlat taṉatu mikka vēṭkiyit talivaṉ muṉpu colluñ col, taliviyiṭattu nikaḻtalilli. aṅṅaṉam col nikaḻātoḻiyiṉum taliviyatu vēṭki putukkalattup peytanīr puṟattē pocintu kāṭṭumāṟupōlum uṇarviṉi yuṭitteṉṟu kūṟuvar. iyaṟkip puṇarcciyākiya kaḷavu, kūṭṭivippār piṟariṉṟit taṉimiyiṟ polivatu. ātaliṉ talivaṉ talivi yiruvarum tattamatu uḷakkaruttip pulappaṭuttun tūtuvarākat tāmē niṉṟu kūṭutalum uṇṭu annili meyppāṭṭiyaluḷ pukumukam purital mutaliya meyppāṭu paṉṉiraṇṭāṉum naṉkaṟiyappaṭum. eṉavē talivaṉ pāṅkaṉatu utavipeṟṟuk kūṭutalum tōḻiyiṉ uṭaṉpāṭu peṟṟuk kūṭutalumākiya ivi yāvar māṭṭum nikaḻavēṇṭumeṉṉum variyaṟiyilli yeṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṉ piṉṉart talivaṉatu colliṉ elliyik kaṭattal talimakaḷukku aṟaṉaṉṟākalāṉum tāṉ cellutaṟkuriya iṭattit tāṉē yuṇarvaḷātalāṉum tāṅkaḷ mīṇṭum kaṇṭu aḷavaḷāvutaṟkuriya ōriṭatti yaṟivikkum poṟuppu talimakaḷic cārntatākum. tōḻiyāl aṟivikkappaṭṭup poruntumiṭamum uṇṭu.
kaḷaviṟpuṇarcci tōḻiyiṉ tuṇiyiṉṟi mūṉṟu nāḷikkumēl nikaḻtalilli. ammūṉṟu nāḷikkuḷḷum tōḻiyiṉtuṇi vilakkappaṭutalilliyeṉpar āciriyar. eṉavē iyaṟkip puṇarcci, iṭantalippāṭu, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam eṉṉum mūṉṟunāḷelliyaḷavum talivaṉ tōḻiyiṉ utaviyiṉṟi avaḷaṟiyātu talimakaḷikkaṇṭu aḷavaḷāvutal kūṭumeṉpatum, ammūṉṟu nāḷikkumēlāyiṉ tōḻiyiṉ utaviyiṉṟit talivi etirppaṭutaṟku ariyaḷeṉpatum, ammūṉṟu nāḷikkuḷḷēyē tōḻiyiṉ uṭaṉpāṭṭip peṟutalumuṇṭeṉpatum āciriyar karuttātal uyttuṇarap paṭum.
palavakiyāṉum talimakaḷpāluḷavām naṉmikaḷi nāṭuvār pakkattiṉi ārāyum ārāycci talivaṉukku vēṇṭu mātalāṉum, tāṅkaḷ mēṟkoṇṭa aṉpiṉ vaḻippaṭṭa kaḷavoḻukkam oṉṟiya aṉpuṭiya tuṇiyākiya tōḻiyāl puṟattāraṟiyātu nilipeṟṟu nikaḻtal vēṇṭumātalāṉum, taṉakkut tuṇiyāvāḷ iṉṉāḷeṉat talivaṉukkuc cuṭṭik kūṟuñ col talivikkuriyatākum eṉpar āciriyar.
kaḷavoḻukkattil naṇukiyārāyntaṟitaṟkariya maṟip poruḷellāvaṟṟiyum kēṭṭaṟkum collutaṟkum uriya tāyākac ciṟappitturikkappaṭupavaḷ ceviliyēyām. taṉakku iṉṟiyamiyāta uyirttōḻiyākat taliviyāl virumpappaṭṭa tōḻiyeṉpāḷ mēṟkūṟiya ciṟappuṭiya ceviliyiṉ makaḷāvāḷ. taliviyiṉ kaḷavoḻukkampaṟṟit taṉ maṉattuḷḷē ārāytalum taliviyiṉ cūḻccikkut tāṉ ucāttuṇiyāki niṟṟalumākiya peruṅkēṇmiyuṭiyāḷ it tōḻiyēyeṉpar.
talivaṉ taṉpālvantu kuṟiyuṟṟu niṟka avaṉuḷḷak karuttiṉi yuṇartalum, taliviyiṉ uḷḷak kuṟippiṉik kaṇṭuṇartalum, taliviyum tāṉum oruṅkirunta niliyil talivaṉvara anniliyil avviruvaratu uḷḷakkaruttiṉi yuṇartalum eṉat tōḻi, taṉ karuttuṭaṉ avviruvaratu karuttiṉiyum vittu oṉṟupaṭuttuṇarum uṇarcci mūvakippaṭum. ivvāṟu mūvar matiyiṉiyum oṉṟupaṭut tuṇartaliṉ itu matiyuṭampaṭuttaleṉṟu peyarāyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam talivaṉ taliviyeṉṉum iruvarpālum otta aṉpuṭimiyuṇarntapiṉallatu talivaṉ taṉṉi irantu piṉṉiṟkum muyaṟcikkut tōḻi iṭantaramāṭṭāḷ. taṉṉi irantu piṉṉiṟkun talivaṉatu niṉiviṉkaṭpaṭum mācaṟṟa aṉpiṉ tiṟattiyuṇarntu avaṉit taliviyōṭu kūṭṭuvittalum attōḻiyiṉ ceyalēyām.
talivaṉ talivi yiruvarum piṟaraṟiyātu pakalilum iravilum aḷavaḷāvutaṟkeṉak kuṟikkappaṭṭa iṭamē kuṟiyeṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭum. maṉiyuṉuṭpukātu aṅkuḷḷōr kūṟuñcoṟkaḷ kēṭkum aṇimikkaṇ amivatu iravukkuṟiyākum. ūriṉ matiṟpuṟamāyt talimakaḷ aṟintu cērtaṟkut takutiyuṭiyatākiya iṭam pakaṟkuṟiyākum. talivaṉ ceyta aṭiyāḷameṉa mayaṅtaṟkuriyaṉa iyaṟkiyāka nikaḻiṉ kuṟiyallātavaṟṟik kuṟiyeṉak koḷḷutalum talimakaḷukkuriya iyalpākum. kaṟpuṭi makaḷirkkuriya ciṟappiṟ kuṉṟāvāṟu mēṟkūṟiya iruvakik kuṟiyiṭaṅkaḷilum piṟaraṟiyātapaṭi talivaṉoṭukūṭi yoḻukum oḻukkamum talimakaḷukku uṇṭu.
iṅṅaṉam kaḷavoḻukkattil vantoḻukutal kāraṇamāka eṉṟum taṉ tōḻarkaḷuṭaṉ kalantukoḷḷutaṟ kuriya viḷiyāṭṭiṉiyum tiruviḻāc ceyalmuṟikaḷiyum vilakiyoḻukum oḻukkam talivaṉukkilli. yāvarālum virumpi nōkkappaṭun talivaṉ kaḷavoḻukkam kāraṇamāka ivaṟṟi vilakiyoḻuku vāṉāyiṉ avaṉ vāramipaṟṟi avaṉip palarum viṉava, atu kāraṇamāka avaṉatu kaḷavoḻukkam puṟattārkkup pulaṉāmātaliṉ, ivaṟṟi nīṅkiyoḻukutal kūṭāteṉa vilakkuvar āciriyar. ivvāṟē vaḻiyarumiyum neñcaḻitalum añcutalum iṭiyūṟum ākiya ivi talivaṉpāl nikaḻtalkūṭāteṉpar.
taliviyiṉ tantiyum tamiyaṉmārum ikkaḷavoḻuk kattik kuṟippiṉāl uṇarvar. naṟṟāy, ceviliyuṇarum muṟimiyāl aṟintukoḷvāḷ. iraviṉum pakaliṉum avvaḻi vantu cellun talimakaṉiyaṟintu avaṉatu varuki kāraṇamāka makaḷir cilarum palarum tammukak kuṟippiṉāṟ pulappaṭuttum ampalum, iṉṉāṉōṭu iṉṉāḷiṭiyatu naṭpu eṉac collāl viritturippatāya alarum tōṉṟiya piṉṉarallatu immaṟi veḷippaṭātātaliṉ, ikkaḷavu veḷippaṭutaṟkut talivaṉē kāraṇamāvaṉ, kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭa piṉṉart taliviyi maṇantukoḷḷutalum atu veḷippaṭutaṟku muṉṉarē maṇam ceytukoḷḷutalum eṉa maṇantukoḷḷum muṟi irutiṟappaṭum.
kaḷavu veḷippāṭē kaṟpiyal vāḻkkiyākak karutap paṭumāyiṉum ulakattāraṟiyat taliviyi maṇantukoḷḷāta niliyil ōtal, paki, tūtu eṉpaṉa kāraṇamākat talivaṉ neṭṭiṭip pirintu cēṟal kūṭāteṉpar āciriyar.



kaṟpiyal
kaṟpiṉatu ilakkaṇam uṇarttiṉamiyāl itu kaṟpiyaleṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. aṉpurimi pūṇṭa talimakaṉ taṉpāl aṉpuṭiya taliviyip peṟṟōr koṭuppap palaraṟiya maṇantu vāḻum maṉivāḻkkiyē kaṟpeṉac ciṟappitturikkap peṟuvatākum. muṉ kaḷaviyaliṟ kūṟiyavāṟu otta aṉpuṭiya oruvaṉum oruttiyum nallūḻiṉ āṇiyāl etirppaṭṭu uḷḷap puṇarcciyiṟ kūṭiyoḻukiṉa rāyiṉum talimakaṉuṭiya peṟṟōr uṭaṉpāṭiṉṟi avviruvarum maṉivāḻkkiyi mēṟkoḷḷutal iyalātu. ākavē oruvariyoruvar piriyātu vāḻtaṟkuriya uḷḷat tuṟutiyi ulakattāraṟiya veḷippaṭuttum niyatiyākiyavatuvic caṭaṅkuṭaṉ talivaṉ talimakaḷi maṇantukoḷḷutal maṉivāḻkkikku iṉṟiyamiyāta ciṟappuṭiya nikaḻcciyāyiṟṟu. uḷḷap puṇarcciyaḷavil urimi pūṇṭoḻukiya talivaṉum taliviyum ulakattāraṟiya maṉiyaṟam nikaḻttutaṟku urimi ceytaḷikkuñ ceyal muṟiyē paṇṭit tamiḻar koṇṭoḻukiya tirumaṇac caṭaṅkākum. itaṉik karaṇam eṉṟa collāl vaḻaṅkuvar tolkāppiyaṉār.
kaṇavaṉiṟ ciṟanta teyvam illiyeṉavum avaṉi iṉṉavāṟu vaḻipaṭutal vēṇṭumeṉavum talimakaḷukkup peṟṟōr kaṟpittalāṉum, antaṇar cāṉṟōr aruntavat tōr viruntiṉar mutaliyōrpāl iṉṉavāṟu naṭantukoḷḷutal vēṇṭumeṉat talimakaṉ talimakaḷukkuk kaṟpittalāṉum, niṉ maṉiviyi ivvāṟu pātukāppapāyāka eṉat talivaṉukkum niṉ kaṇavaṉukku ivvāṟu paṇi ceytoḻuku vāyāka eṉat talivikkum cāṉṟōr kaṟpittalāṉum ivvatuvic caṭaṅkākiya karaṇamum kaṟpeṉappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. talivaṉ talivi yiruvarum oruvari yoruvar iṉṟiyamiyātavarāy maṇantu vāḻuṅkāl tali makaḷatu maṉattiṉkaṇ aminta kalaṅkā nilimiyākiya tiṇmiyē kaṟpeṉappaṭum. kaṟpeṉṉuntiṇmi eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar. ittakiya maṉavuṟutiyi ulakattāraṟiyap pulappaṭuttuvatu tirumaṇac caṭaṅkākiya karaṇamēyākum. kātalar iruvarum piriviṉṟiyiyinta naṭpuṭiyār eṉpataṉi valiyuṟuttuvatu vatuvic caṭaṅkākiya ikkaraṇamē yātaliṉ inniyati piḻipaṭumēl avviruvaratu vāḻkkiyil cātaliyotta perun tuṉpam nērumeṉpatu tiṇṇam. karaṇam piḻikkil maraṇam payakkum eṉa nam nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkum paḻamoḻi itaṉi valiyuṟuttal kāṇalām.
talivaṉ, talimakaḷip peṟṟōraṟiyātu uṭaṉpōkkil aḻittuc ceṉṟavaḻi, avaḷuṭiya peṟṟōratu uṭaṉpāṭiṉṟiyum karaṇam nikaḻtaluṇṭeṉpar āciriyar. eṉavē makaṭkoṭikkuriya peṟṟōratu icivillātu pōyiṉum kātalariruvaratu uḷḷattuṟutiyip pulappaṭuttu vatākiya tirumaṇac caṭaṅku ulakattāraṟiya nikaḻtal iṉṟiyamiyātateṉpatu naṉku pulaṉām. ittirumaṇac caṭaṅku mikappaḻiya kālattil nāṭṭil ellā makkaḷukkum vitikkappaṭṭilatu. paṭippuk kālantoṭṭu nilipeṟṟu varum mūvēntar kuṭumpattiṟkē mutaṉ mutal vakukkappaṭṭiruntatu. araciyalāṭciyil paṭṭattaraci mutaṉmi peṟutal kāraṇamākavē ivvariyaṟi vitikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. vaṇpukaḻ mūvarākiya mēlōr mūvarkkum vakutta karaṇam avarkīḻ vāḻum kuṭimakkaḷukkum uriyatāyiṟṟu. icceyti, mēlōr mūvarkkum puṇartta karaṇam, kīḻōrkkākiya kālamum uṇṭē eṉavarum ivviyaṟ cūttirattāl naṉku viḷaṅkum.
oruvaṉ, oruttiyi aṉpiṉāṟ kūṭiyoḻukip piṉṉar avaḷi yaṟiyēṉ eṉap poy kūṟutalum, niṉṉip piriyēṉ eṉat teyvattiṉ muṉṉiliyil uṟutikūṟip piṉ ataṉi vaḻuvik kaṭippiṭiyiṉṟi yoḻukutalumākiya tīya vaḻakkaṅkaḷ innāṭṭil tōṉṟiya piṉṉarē cāṉṟōrākiya kuṭumpat talivarkaḷ, kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyum piriviṉṟi vāḻtaṟkuriya maṇac caṭaṅkākiya karaṇatti vakuttamittārkaḷ. ic ceyti,
		poyyum vaḻuvun tōṉṟiya piṉṉar
aiyar yāttaṉar karaṇam eṉpa
eṉavarum tolkāppiyac cūttirattāl iṉitu viḷaṅkum. poyyāvatu ceytataṉi maṟittal. vaḻuvāvatu ceytataṉ kaṇ muṭiya nillātu tappi yoḻukutal, karaṇattoṭu muṭinta kāliyil aviyiraṇṭum nikaḻāvā mātalāṟ karaṇam vēṇṭuvatāyiṟṟu eṉpar iḷampūraṇar.
aiyar eṉṉuñ col talimic ciṟappuṭiya periyōrik kuṟittu vaḻaṅkun taṉittamiḻc collākum. accol īṇṭu tamiḻkkula mutalvarākiya muṉṉōrik kuṟittu niṉṟatu. itaṉi ārya eṉṉum vaṭacolliṉ tiripākap piṟaḻavuṇarntu ittolkāppiyac cūttirattiṟku māṟupaṭap poruḷ kūṟiṉārumuḷar. eṉṉimuṉ ṉillaṉmiṉ tevvir palareṉṉi, muṉṉiṉṟu kaṉṉiṉ ṟavar (tirukkuṟaḷ-771) eṉavum, eṉṉipuṟki yuṇṭum peruntōḷaṉṉē (puṟam-84) eṉavum varum toṭarkaḷiṉ eṉ-ai eṉpatu eṉ talivaṉ eṉṟa poruḷil vaḻaṅkak kāṇkiṉṟōm. ai eṉpataṉ aṭiyākap piṟantatatē aiyar eṉṉun tamiḻc collākum. ai viyappākum (uri-88) eṉpatu tolkāppiyam. tīneṟi vilakki naṉṉeṟic celuttum pēraṟi vuṭimiyāṟ palarum viyantu pārāṭṭat takka talimic ciṟappuṭiyāri aiyar eṉa vaḻaṅkum marapuṇmi itaṉāṟ pulaṉākum.
		putalvaṟ payanta puṉiṟutīr poḻutiṉ
neyyaṇi mayakkam purintōḷ nōkki
aiyar pāṅkiṉum amararc cuṭṭiyum
ceyperuñ ciṟappoṭu cērtaṟ kaṇṇum	(kaṟ-5)
eṉpuḻittalivaṉ, putalvaṉip payantu vālāmi nīṅkiya taliviyik karuti, aṟaṉāṟṟi mūtta aṟivuṭiyōrkaḷākiya taṉ kula mutalvarit tuṇiyākak koṇṭum amarakattañcā maṟavarkaḷāyt tuṟakkam pukka vīrarkaḷi eṇṇiyuñ ciṟappuc ceytaluṇṭeṉpataṉi āciriyar viḷakkiyuḷḷār. ācaṟu kāṭciyiyar (kuṟiñcip-17) eṉak kapilarum, viṇcelaṉ marapiṉ aiyar (tirumaru-107) eṉa nakkīrarum muṟṟattuṟanta tavaccelvarkaḷi aiyar eṉa vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷārkaḷ. camaṇaril illaṟattārākiya ulaka nōṉpikaḷip perum peyar aiyar eṉpar iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ. tamiyaṉmārkaḷi aiyar eṉa vaḻaṅkutalum uṇṭu. aṟattoṭu niṉṟēṉik kaṇṭu tiṟaṟpaṭa, eṉ aiyarkkuytturittāḷ yāy eṉpatu kuṟiñcikkali. iḷamā veyiṟṟi ivikāṇ niṉiyar, talināḷi vēṭṭattut tanta nal āṉirikaḷ eṉpatu cilappatikāram. tirunāḷippōvār, tirunīlakaṇṭayāḻppāṇar ākiya talimip paṇpuṭiya periyōrkaḷi aiyar eṉṟa collāṟ ciṟappu muṟiyiṟ cēkkiḻāraṭikaḷ vaḻaṅkutalāl iccol ikkālattiṟpōla muṟkālattiṟ cātippeyarāka vaḻaṅkiyatilliyeṉpatu naṉku tuṇiyappaṭum. palanūṟāṇṭu kaṭku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻ nūlkaḷilum kalveṭṭukkaḷilum aiyareṉṉum iccol cātippeyarāka vaḻaṅkappeṟṟilatu. aṅṅaṉamākavum mikap paḻiya tamiḻ nūlāciya tol kāppiyattil vaḻaṅkiya aiyareṉṉuñ collukku ik kālac cātippeyar vaḻakkatti yuḷattuṭ koṇṭu āriyamēlōr eṉap poruḷ kūṟutal varalāṟṟu muṟikku ovvāta piḻiyuriyātal tiṇṇam.
poyyum vaḻuvum tōṉṟiya piṉṉar aiyar karaṇam yāttaṉa reṉavē, avi tōṉṟāta kālam mika muntiya teṉpatum akkālattil ittakiya vatuvic caṭaṅkukku iṉṟiyamiyāmi nērntatilliyeṉpatum, aiyar yāttaṉar karaṇam eṉpa eṉat tolkāppiyaṉār tamakku muṉṉōr kūṟṟāka vitturittalāl ikkaraṇa variyaṟi avar kālattukku muṉṉarē tamiḻ muṉṉōrkaḷāl vitikkappaṭṭa teṉpatum naṉku tuṇiyappaṭum. muṉ poyyum vaḻuvun tōṉṟāta kaḷavu maṇattil piṉ avi tōṉṟiyataṟkut tamiḻaroṭu toṭarpillāta vēṟṟiṉattāratu varukiyē kāraṇamātal vēṇṭum.
kaḷavoḻukkam oḻukā niṉṟa talimakaṉ, oruvarum aṟiyāta paṭi talimakaḷi uṭaṉpōkkil aḻittuc celluṅkāl, avaḷuṭiya cuṟṟattār iṭiccurattiṭiyeyti avviru variyun taṭuttu niṟutta muyaluvar. avaratu varukiyik kaṇṭu añciya talimakaḷ, taṉṉuyiriṉuñ ciṟanta talivaṉip piritaṟku maṉamiṉṟi niṟpaḷ. inniliyiṉi iṭiccura maruṅkiṉ avaḷ tamareytik kaṭikkoṇṭu peyarttaliṟ kalaṅkañar eytik kaṟpoṭu puṇarnta keḷavi eṉpar āciriyar. taliviyiṉ kaṟpu niliyik kaṇṭa cuṟṟattār avviruvarum maṇantu vāḻum neṟimuṟiyiṉi vakuttamippar.
maṟintoḻukum oḻukalāṟākiya kaḷavu palaraṟiya veḷip paṭutalum piṉṉar taliviyiṉcuṟṟattār koṭuppat tali makaḷit talivaṉ maṇantu koḷḷutalumākiya ivi mutalākiya vaḻakku neṟiyil māṟupaṭātu, malivu, pulavi, ūṭal, uṇarvu, pirivu eṉṉum ivvin tiyalkaḷōṭuṅkūṭi nikaḻvatu kaṟpeṉṉum oḻukalāṟākum eṉpar āciriyar. ilvāḻkkiyum puṇarcciyum mutaliyavaṟṟāl talivaṉ talivi yeṉṉum iruvaruḷḷattun tōṉṟum makiḻcciyē maliveṉappaṭum. puṇarcciyāluṇṭākiya makiḻcci kuṟipaṭāmal kālaṅ karutiyirukkum uḷḷa nikaḻacciyē pulaviyākum. aṅṅaṉam uḷḷattu nikaḻntataṉik kuṟippiṉālaṉṟic collāl veḷippaṭuttum nili ūṭal eṉappaṭum. avvāṟu talivikku ūṭal nikaḻntavaḻi ataṟkētuvākiya ceyal eṉpāl nikaḻavilliyēyeṉat talivaṉum avaṉcārpāka vāyilkaḷum talimakaḷukku uṇarttutalē uṇarveṉappaṭum. poruḷīṭṭutalum pōrmēṟ cēṟalum kali payilutalum mutaliya iṉṟiyamiyāta ulakiyaṟ kaṭami karutit talivaṉ taṉ maṉiyāḷip pirintucēṟal paṟṟiya nikaḻcci piriveṉappaṭum.
tamiḻar maṇamuṟiyil nikaḻtaṟkuriya karaṇaṅkaḷ ivi yeṉpatu tam kālattil vāḻnta ellōrkkum naṉku teriyumātalāl avaṟṟit tolkāppiyaṉār tam nūlil virittuk kūṟavilli. toṉṟu toṭṭut tamiḻmakkaḷ koṇṭoḻukiya tirumaṇac caṭaṅkukaḷ cilavaṟṟi akanāṉūṟṟil -66, 86-ām pāṭalkaḷāl oruvāṟu uṇarntu koḷḷalām. vēḷviyācāṉ kāṭṭiyamuṟiyē aṅkicāṉṟāka nikaḻum caṭaṅkumuṟi tolkāppiyattilum caṅka ilakkiyaṅ kaḷilum kūṟappaṭāmiyāl āriyarmēṟkoṇṭa avvivāka muṟikkum tamiḻar koṇṭoḻukiya tirumaṇac caṭaṅkumuṟik kum peritum vēṟupāṭuṇṭeṉat teḷiyalām. kalit tokiyil ōttuṭiyantaṇaṉ erivalaṅkoḷvāṉpōl eṉavarum toṭaril purinūlantaṇarākiya vētiyaratu vēḷvic caṭaṅku uvamiyāka eṭutturikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. ittoṭarp poruḷi ūṉṟinōkkuṅkāl iṅṅaṉam erivalam varutal ōttuṭiyantaṇarālaṉṟi ēṉiya tamiḻ makkaḷāl mēṟ koḷḷappaṭāta caṭaṅkeṉpatu naṉku pulaṉām.
iṉi, ikkaṟpiyaliṟ kūṟappaṭum ēṉiya poruḷkaḷi nōkkuvōm. talivaṉ talivi ākiya iruvarum ulakat tāraṟiya maṇam purintu vāḻum kaṟpiyal vāḻvilē talivaṉ, talivi, tōḻi, kāmakkiḻattiyar, akampukal marapiṉ vāyilkaḷ, cevili, aṟivar, pāṇar, kūttar, iḷiyōr, pārppār eṉpōr uriyāṭutaṟkuriya iṭaṅkaḷiyum poruḷvaki yiṉiyum avartam ceyal muṟikaḷiyum ivviyal 5-mutal 14-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷālum, 24-mutal 30-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷālum, 36-m cūttirattālum āciriyar virittuk kūṟiyuḷḷār.
talivaṉ tēṟṟat teḷiyum elliyiṉit talivi kaṭan taṉaḷāyiṉum kaḷaviṉkaṇ talivi ceyta kuṟiyit tappiṉālum talivaṉ, maṉam ciṟitu vēṟupaṭṭup pulattalum avvēṟupāṭu nilittu niṟpa ūṭutalum uṇṭu. aṅṅaṉam talivaṉ pulantu ūṭiya niliyil avaṉuḷḷam amitiyaṭitaṟkuriya paṇinta moḻikaḷit tōḻi kūṟuvāḷ. parattiyari virumpiyoḻukum talivaṉatu puṟattoḻukkatti nīkkutal karutiyum talivi maṭaṉeṉṉuṅ kuṇattāl aṭaṅkiyoḻukum eḷimiyuṭi yaḷātal karutiyum talivaṉi nōkki aṉpili koṭiyi yeṉat tōḻi iṭitturittaṟkum uriyaḷ. talivaṉatu uḷḷak kuṟippi yaṟital vēṇṭiyum taṉmaṉattu ūṭal nīṅkumiṭattum talivaṉoṭu uṟavallātāḷpōṉṟu talivi vēṟupaṭap pēcutaṟku uriyaḷ.
kaṇavaṉi eññāṉṟum vaḻipaṭṭokutal maṉivikku iyalpākalāl. kāmavuṇarvu mikkut tōṉṟiya niliyil taliviyit talivaṉ paṇintu kūṟutal tavaṟākātu. piṟar tuṉpaṅkaṇṭu uḷamiraṅkum aruḷuṇarvit tōṟṟuvitta aṉpu potinta coṟkaḷi meyyē kūṟutal talivikkuriya iyalpākum.
muṟkūṟiya kaḷavoḻukkattiṉum atu veḷippaṭṭa kaṟpiyal vāḻviṉum alar tōṉṟum. avvalar moḻiyāl talivaṉ talivi yiruvaruḷḷattuṅ kāmavuṇarvu mikkut tōṉṟum. ivvāṟē talivaṉatu viḷiyāṭṭum kāmavuṇarvi mikutippaṭuttum.
kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyum ciṟitu maṉam vēṟupaṭṭu ūṭiya kālattu avartam piṇakkattit tīrttu vittaṟkuriyavarkaḷ vāyilkaḷ eṉappaṭuvar. pāṇar, kūttar mutaliyōr tali makaṉi ekkālum akalātu niṉṟu taliviyiṉatu piṇakkattit tīrkkum vāyilkaḷāka maṉikkaṇ palakālum vantu paḻakum iyalpiṉarākaliṉ, ivarkaḷi akampukal marapiṉ vāyilkaḷ eṉpar āciriyar. ivvāyilkaḷ muṉṉilip puṟamoḻiyākap pēcutalum uṇṭu.
piṉmuṟi vatuviyāka maṟṟoruttiyi maṇanta kālattum taṉ putalvaṉi vāyilākak koṇṭu celliṉum tāṉ parattami ceytu oḻukiyati niṉintu talivaṉ nilikulintu kalaṅkutalum uriyaṉ.
talivaṉik kūṭimakiḻuṅ kālattil tāyippōṉṟu avaṉi iṭitturittut tirutti avaṉatu maṉak kavaliyi māṟṟutalum maṉivikkuriya kaṭamiyākum. talivaṉatu oḻukkattiṟ cōrvu piṟavāmaṟ kāttal taliviyiṉ kaṭamiyāka nūlkaḷiṟ collappaṭutalāl, makaṉip peṟṟa tāyākiya talivi iṅṅaṉam iṭitturikkum uyarvuṭiyaḷātalum talivaṉatu uyarvākavē karutappaṭum. ellāc celvaṅkaḷukkum uriya talivaṉ ivvāṟu aṉpuṭiyārkaṇ paṇintoḻukutal iyalpēyākum.
pōr ceytu pakivari vellutaṟkuriya vaḻi tuṟikaḷi ārāytaṟku iṭamākiya pācaṟiyiṉkaṇ makaḷiroṭu uṭaṉuṟital kūṭātu. pōrttoḻilutaviyiṟ paḻakip puṇpaṭṭa vīrari yupacarittalum, icipāṭi makiḻvittalum mutaliya puṟattoḻil puriyum puṟappeṇṭirāyiṉ pācaṟiyil iruttal poruntuvatākum.
maṉivāḻkkikkaṇ kaṇavaṉ maṉivi ākiya iruvarkku miṭiyē paḻakum ellā vāyilkaḷum avviruvarpālum amitaṟkuriya makiḻcci niliyip poruḷākak koṇṭē uriyāṭutaṟkuriyar, avviruvariṭattum aṉpu nīṅkiya kaṭuñ coṟkaḷik kūṟavēṇṭiya cevvi nērntāl nēr niṉṟu kūṟātu ciṟippuṟamāka otuṅki niṉṟu kūṟutal vēṇṭum.
talivaṉ parattiyiṟ pirintu vantavaḻi irattalum teḷittalum eṉṉum iraṇṭiṭamumallāta ēṉiyiṭaṅkaḷil talivaṉ muṉṉart talivi taṉṉip pukaḻtal kūṭātu. talivaṉ viṉivayiṟ piriyuṅkāl talivi muṉṉark taṉṉip pukaḻnturittal poruntum.
talivaṉ kūṟṟiṉi etirttuk kūṟum urimi pāṅkaṉukku uṇṭu. iṅṅaṉam etirttukkūṟum col ellāk kālattilum nikaḻvatilli; arukiyē nikaḻum.
tuṉpakkālattum taliviyi vaṟpuṟuttiyallatu talivaṉ pirintu cellutal illi. viṉimēṟcelluṅ kālattut talivi taṉ piriviṉip poṟuttirukkamāṭṭāḷ eṉṟa niliyil taṉatu payaṇatti nīṅkutal illi; avaḷi vaṟpuṟuttal karutic ciṟitupoḻutu tāmatittuc celvaṉ. talivaṉ viṉi mēṟkoṇṭu ceṉṟa iṭattil avaṉpāṟ ceṉṟu taliviyiṉ āṟṟāttaṉmiyi yāvarum colvatilli. talivaṉ tāṉ mēṟkoṇṭa viṉiyil veṟṟipeṟṟa niliyilētāṉ taliviyippaṟṟiya niṉivu avaṉuḷḷattē viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum.
talivikkup pūpputtōṉṟi mūṉṟunāḷ kaḻitta piṉpu paṉṉiraṇṭu nāḷum karuttōṉṟuṅ kālamātaliṉ, talivaṉ parattiyiṟ pirinta kālattup pūppiṉatu puṟampu paṉṉiraṇṭu nāḷum taliviṉip pirintuṟital kūṭāteṉpar āciriyar.
yāvarālum virumpattakka kalvi karutip piriyuṅkālam mūṉṟaṇṭukaḷukku mēṟpaṭātu. paki taṇiviṉiyākiya vēntaṟkuṟṟuḻip piriyum pirivum, ataṉiccārnta tūtu, kāval eṉpaṉavum, oḻinta poruḷvayiṟ pirivum ōrāṇṭiṟkuṭpaṭṭa kālavelliyiṉiyuṭiyaṉa.
yāṟu kuḷaṅkaḷilum cōlikaḷilum viḷiyāṭi uṟipatiyik kaṭantupōy nukarcci yeytutal talivaṉ talivi yiruvarkkum uriyatākum. iṅṅaṉam kāmanukarcci yellām nukarntaminta piṟkālattē, pātukāvalaminta piḷḷikaḷuṭaṉē neruṅki, aṟattiṉi virumpum cuṟṟat tāruṭaṉē talivaṉum taliviyum, vīṭupēṟākiya ciṟappiṉi yaruḷum muḻumutaṟporuḷi iṭiviṭātu eṇṇip pōṟṟum naṉṉeṟiyiṟ paḻakutal, mēṟkūṟiya maṉivāḻkkiyiṉ muṭinta payaṉākum.
tōḻi, tāy, pārppāṉ, pāṅkaṉ, pāṇaṉ, pāṭiṉi, iḷiyar, viruntiṉar, kūttar, viṟaliyar, aṟivar, kaṇṭōr ākiya ip paṉṉiruvarum maṉivāḻkkiyiṉkaṇṇē kaṇavaṉ maṉivi yeṉpōriṭiyēyuḷatām piṇakkattiṉit tīrttu vikkum niṟinta ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya vāyilkaḷāvar.
viṉikarutip pirinta talimakaṉ, taṉ uḷḷampōṉṟu uṟṟuḻiyutavum paṟaviyiṉ vēkattiyuṭiya kutiriyiyuṭiya ṉātaliṉ, tāṉ viṉimuṟṟi mīḷuṅ kālattu iṭivaḻiyiṟ ṟaṅkātu virintu varutaliyuṭiyaṉāvaṉ eṉa avaṉ talivi yiṉpāl vitta peruviruppiṉip pulappaṭuttuvar āciriyar.



poruḷiyal
mēṟcollappaṭṭa iyalkaḷilum iṉic collum iyalkaḷilum varum poruḷiṉatu taṉmiyiṉi yuṇarttutaliṉ itu poruḷiyaleṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. akapporuḷ, puṟapporuḷ eṉpaṉa iraṇṭu poruṇmiyiṉum eñci niṉṟaṉa kūṟiṉamiyiṉ itaṉi oḻipiyaleṉṉiṉuṅ kuṟṟamilli yeṉpar iḷampūraṇar.
collatikārattiṟ kūṟiya coṟkaḷi marapiyaliṉ iru tiṇi aimpāl iyal neṟi vaḻāmit tiripil col eṉpa rātaliṉ avi īṇṭut tamporuḷi vēṟupaṭṭicippiṉum poruḷāmeṉavum, ipporuḷatikārattu muṉṉark kūṟiya poruḷiṟ piṟaḻnticippaṉavum poruḷāmeṉavum amittuc, colluṇarttum poruḷum toṭarmoḻi yuṇarttum poruḷum oruṅkē kūṟaliṟ poruḷiyaleṉṟār eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. ivviyaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭaṉa yāvum coṟ poruḷiṉ vaḻuvamitiyum poruḷiṉ vaḻuvamitiyum eṉa iru pakutippaṭumeṉpatum, puṟattiṇiyiyaluṭ puṟattiṇivaḻuk kūṟi akapporuṭkuriya vaḻuvē īṇṭuk kūṟukiṉṟateṉpatum avar karuttu.
akapporuḷ oḻukalāṟṟil talivaṉ, talivi, tōḻi mutaliyōr uriyāṭutaṟkuriya collamitiyiṉiyum avar tam kūṟṟil amittaṟkuriya poruḷ vakiyiṉiyum ciṟappu muṟiyiṟ kūṟuvatu ipporuḷiyalākum. ivviyaliluḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷi 52-āka iḷampūraṇarum, 54-āka nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum pakuttu uri kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
colloṭucol toṭarvupaṭum vāypāṭṭāl toṭarntu nillātu piṟitōr vāypāṭṭāl toṭarntu niṟpiṉum, collak karutiya poruḷ iyipu peṟap pulappaṭum. avvaḻic coṟkaḷukku aṅkamākiya acic coṟkaḷ māṟi niṟṟalilli.
ulakiyalil ellōrkkum uriyatāyp payiṉṟatākātu oru tiṟattārkkē yuriyatāyp payilum colvakiyiṉip pāṟkiḷavi eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar āciriyar. pāl-pakkam. pāṟkiḷavi-oru tiṟattārkkē uriyatāyp payilum col. tuṉpamum iṉpamum ākiya iruvaki niliyiṉiyumuṭiya kāmattik karutiya iyalpu pulaṉāka eṇvaki meypāṭum viḷaṅkap poruntiya uṟuppuṭiyatupōlavum, uṇarvuṭiyatupōlavum, taṉatu kūṟṟiṉi maṟutturippatu pōlavum, neñcoṭu cērttuk kūṟiyum, pēcum āṟṟalillāta paṟavi, vilaṅku mutaliyavaṟṟōṭu porunti avi ceyyātaṉavaṟṟic ceytaṉavākak kūṟiyum, piṟaruṟṟa piṇiyit tāmuṟṟa piṇipōlac cērttiyum, aṟiviyum aṟitaṟku vāyilākiya poṟi pulaṉkaḷiyum vēṟupaṭa niṟutti uvamip peyarum, uvamikkum peyarum, toḻilum paṇpum payaṉumākiya mūvakippaṭṭa poruṭkum uriyavāka uvamam poruntumiṭattu uvamaṅ kūṟutalum, talivaṉ talivi ākiya iruvarkkum uriya oru kūṟṟuc collām.
kāmam iṭiyīṭupaṭṭa kālattu kaṉākkaṇṭu kūṟutalum avviruvarkkum uriyatākum. avviruvarum uṭaṉpōkiya kālattut tāy kaṉākkaṇṭu kūṟutalum uṇṭu. tammuḷ aṉpuṭiyarāyp paḻakum iṉpa niliyillāta ēṉit tuṉpak kālattut talivi, tōḻi, cevili, naṟṟāy ākiya nālvarkkum mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu aṟivum pulaṉum vēṟupaṭak kūṟum oru kūṟṟuc col uriyatākum. aṉporumiyum uyiriṉuñ ciṟanta nāṇamum maṭaṉum, kuṟṟamaṟṟa ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya peṇpālār nālvarkkum uriyavākum. eṉavē ivar nālvarum ikkuṇaṅkaḷukku iḻukku nērāvaṇṇam uriyāṭutal vēṇṭumeṉpatu naṉku peṟappaṭum.
talimakaḷ, taṉatu uṭal vaṉappu vēṟupaṭṭut taṉimi yuṟuṅkāl talimakaṉatu pirivit taṉ avayaṅkaḷ muṉṉamē uṇarntaṉapōlap poruntiya vakiyāṟ kūṟutalum uṇṭu. j‹ cl«ò« cÆU« bkÈªj ÃiyÆY« ‘ïit v‹d tU¤j K‰wd? eṉat taṉakku varuttamillatupōlak kūṟuvatallatu, talivaṉit tāṉē ceṉṟu cērtal talimakaḷiṉ iyalpaṉṟām. taṉ neñcuṭaṉ taṉittu ārāyuṅ kālattut talimakaṉ irukkumiṭatti yaṭital vēṇṭumeṉat talivi kūṟutalum uṇṭu. talivaṉ puṟattoḻukkatti maṟikkumiṭaṉum tāṉ avaṉpāl peru viruppuṟṟa niliyum allāta ēṉiyiṭaṅkaḷilellām maṭaṉ nīṅkātapaṭi niṟṟal taliviyiṉ kaṭaṉākum.
talivaṉ talivi eṉṉum iruvarum iyaṟkip puṇarcci yākiya mutaṟ kāṭciyil oruvariyoruvar cantitta muṟimi tūya aṉpoṭu poruntiya aṟattiṉ vaḻippaṭṭa naṟceyalē yeṉpataṉi aṟivuṟuttutal kaḷavu veḷippaṭuttaliṉ karut tākum. ātalāl aṅṅaṉam veḷippaṭuttum muṟiyiṉi aṟattoṭu niṟṟal eṉpar āciriyar. talivi talivaṉoṭu taṉakkuṇṭākiya toṭarpiṉit tam peṟṟōrkku aṟivittal vēṇṭumeṉṉuṅ karuttiṉaḷākiya kālattaṉṟit tōḻi tāṉē aṟattoṭu niṟkum muṟimiyilaḷ. aṅṅaṉam aṟattoṭu niṟ kuṅkāl taliviyiṉ kuṭippiṟappiṟkum ceviliyiṉ aṟiviṟkum taliviyiṉ nāṇam, kaṟpu mutaliya perumikkum taṉatu kāvalukkum talivaṉukkuriya aṟivu, niṟi, ōrppu, kaṭippiṭiyeṉṉum peruntaṉmikkum tavaṟu nērātapaṭi ikkaḷavoḻukkam nikaḻnta muṟiyiṉi muraṇpāṭillā moḻikaḷāl tōḻi cevilikku aṟivuṟuttal marapākum eṉpar kaḷaviyaluriyāciriyar.
cevilikkuttōḻi aṟattoṭu niṟkuṅkāl, taṉṉikaraṟṟa talivaṉ iḷiyōrākiya eṅkaḷpāl eḷiyaṉāka naṭantu koṇṭāṉ eṉṟu kūṟutalum, ittakiya aruḷum ciṟappu muṭiyāṉ attōṉṟal eṉat talivaṉi uyarttup pukaḻtalum, talivaṉ taliviyeṉṉum iruvarum aṉpiṉāl nirampiya vēṭkiyuṭiyāreṉak kūṟutalum, attakiya talivaṉukkē nam taliviyi maṇañceytu koṭuttal vēṇṭumeṉṉum kuṟipput tōṉṟakkūṟutalum, taliviyiṉatu nōyiṉit taṇittal vēṇṭi vēlaṉ mutaliyōrik koṇṭu veṟiyāṭal mutaliyaṉa nikaḻttiyapoḻutu avi taliviyiṉ nōyit taṇittaṟkuriyaṉa alla eṉat terivittut taṭukkumukamāka avarkaḷpāl cilavaṟṟi viṉāvutalum, talivaṉ yātēṉum oru kāraṇatti muṉṉiṭṭu varuṅkāl taṉ karuttiṉṟit talimakaḷi etirppaṭṭāṉeṉak kūṟutalum, talimakaṉum talimakaḷum oruvari yoruvar cantittapoḻutu nikaḻnta nikaḻcciyiṉi nikaḻnta muṟiyē uṇmiyāka eṭutturittalum eṉa ivvēḻu vakiyālum kaḷavoḻukkatti veḷippaṭuttal muṟiyeṉpar tolkāppiyar.
kāmavuṇarvu mikkuttōṉṟiya kālattallatu col nikaḻcci yillāmiyāl talimakaḷatu vēṭkiyi avaḷatu tōṟṟa mutaliyavaṟṟikkoṇṭu cevili mutaliyōr kuṟip piṉāl uṇarvar.
aṭakkam, amiti, nērmi, uṇmiyiṉi vaṟpuṟuttum colvaṉmi, tītorīi naṉṟiṉpāluykkum nallaṟivu, piṟarpāṟ kāṇutaṟkariya arumi eṉpaṉa peṇṭirkkuriya ciṟappiyalpukaḷākum. ākavē mēṟcolliya aṟattoṭu nilivakiyum iṉikkūṟum varivukaṭātaṟ pakutiyum ākiyavaṟṟi uṇmivakiyāṉum puṉinturi vakiyāṉum kūṟum āṟṟal tōḻi mutaliya peṇpālārkkuṇmi iṉitu pulaṉām.
talimakaṉ varum kālamum vaḻiyum ūriṭiyuḷatāṅ kālalum ākiyavaṟṟit tappiyoḻukutalāl uḷavām tīmikaḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭalum, tāṉ neñcaḻintu kūṟutalum, talimakaṉukkuḷavām iṭiyūṟu kūṟutalum, avaṉip pakaṟkuṟi vilakki iravil varukaveṉṟalum, iravum pakalum iṅku vārātoḻika veṉak kūṟutalum, naṉmiyākavum tīmiyākavum piṟaporuḷiyeṭuttuk kāṭṭalum, piṟavumāka iṅṅaṉam talivaṉatu uyarcci keṭat tōḻi kūṟuñ coṟkaḷyāvum talimakaḷukkut talimakaṉpāl viruppamiṉmi yāṟ kūṟappaṭṭaṉa alla; talimakaḷi avaṉ virivil maṇantukoḷḷutal vēṇṭumeṉṉum vēṭkiyiṉip poruḷākavuṭiya coṟkaḷām. talivaṉ kaḷavoḻukkattu nīṭṭittoḻukiya niliyil ivvāṟu kuṟippākac collātu veḷippaṭiyāka maṟutturittalum uṇṭu.
kaḷavoḻukkattil talivaṉ taṉiyē vantu pōvataṉṟit tērum yāṉiyum kutiriyum piṟavum ūrntu cellutalum iyalpēyākum.
uṇṇutaṟṟoḻitali nikaḻttutaṟkuriyaṉa allāta uṇarvaṟṟa poruḷkaḷi attoḻili nikaḻttiṉavāka ēṟiṭṭuk kūṟutalum ivvakattiṇikkaṇ vaḻaṅkum marapākum.
talimakaḷip puṟattē cellavoṭṭātu kākkum kāval mikutiyāṉa niliyil, eṅkaḷ cuṟṟattār talimakaḷik koṭukka iciyāmikkuk kāraṇam paricapporuḷi vēṇṭi niṟṟalē eṉat tōḻi talimakaṉi nōkkik kūṟutalum vilakkattakkataṉṟu.
kāval mikutiyāl talivikku varuttam nērntapoḻutu, talivaṉpāl uḷavām aṉpum, aṉpiṉ vaḻippaṭṭa maṉi yaṟamum, maṉiyaṟattiṉkaṇ iruntu nukartaṟkuriya iṉpamum, peṇṇiyalpākiya nāṇamum ākiya ivaṟṟiṟ karuttiṉṟi aṭaṅkiyoḻukum nili, paḻiyuṭiyataṉṟātalāl iṟceṟik kappaṭṭa kālattil ivaṟṟippaṟṟi eṇṇutaṟku iṭamilli eṉpar āciriyar.
poruḷīṭṭutal karutip pirintu cellun talimakaṉ, taṉṉōṭu uṭaṉvarak karutiya talimakaḷukku yāṉ pōkum vaḻi vemmimikka pāli nilamām eṉak kūṟi vilakkutalum tavaṟakātu.
muṉṉiya nūlkaḷil akapporuḷākavum puṟap poruḷākavum eṭuttōtappaṭṭaṉavaṉṟit tam kālattu vāḻum cāṉṟōr tamatu anupavattāl teḷintu kūṟuvaṉavum uyarttōr vaḻakkeṉa ēṟṟukkoḷḷat takkaṉavātaliṉ, uyartōr vaḻakkoṭu porutti varuvaṉavellām ceyyuṭkup poruḷākap puṇarkkappaṭum. ulaka vaḻakkiṟkup poruttamillāta kūṟṟukkaḷ akapporuḷoḻukalāṟṟiṟkup payaṉuṭiyaṉavāka varumāyiṉ, avaṟṟi vaḻakkeṉṟē pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkañ ceytalum paḻiyuṭiyataṉṟām. avvāṟu ulaka vaḻakkiṉik kaṭantu varuvaṉavākiya poruṭpakutikaḷic ceyyuḷil amittuk kūṟuṅkāl, nāṇam nīṅkāmikkuk kāraṇamākiya naṉṉeṟippaṭuttuk kūṟutal vēṇṭum.
muṟippeyariṭattu irupālukkum poruntiṉa takuti yuṭiya ellā eṉṉum potuc col, āṇpāl peṇ pāl ākiya avviraṇṭaṟkum oppa vuriyatāy vaḻaṅkum.
tantikkuriya poruḷkaḷāy makkaḷ eytutaṟkuriya poruḷkaḷiṟ cērātaṉavumāy, aṟamum pukaḻuṅ karuti oruvar koṭuppa maṟṟiyōrpāṟ cellātaṉavumāy, uḻavu mutaliya toḻil muyaṟciyāl vārātaṉavumāy, vēṟupaṭṭa piṟarāl valintu koḷḷappaṭātaṉavumāy varum poruḷurimi muṟi, ivvakapporuḷ oḻukalāṟṟiṟ porunti varutal uṇṭu eṉpar āciriyar. caṅkattoki nūlkaḷil taliviyiṉ aṅkaṅkaḷit tōḻi taṉṉuṭiyaṉavāka urimi pārāṭṭik kūṟuvaṉavāka aminta kūṟṟukkaḷyāvum ittakiya urimi muṟikkuc ciṟanta eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷākum.
kiḻavaṉ, kiḻatti eṉpaṉa mutalāka ivvāṟu āṇoru miyum peṇṇorumiyum uṇartti niṟkum orumic coṟ kaḷ, nāṉilattut talivariyum taliviyariyum uṇarttum paṉmiccoṟkaṇṇē niṉṟu paṉmip poruḷiyuṇarttum muṟi, ulaka vaḻakkil nilipeṟṟatu eṉpar aṟiñar.
iṉpam eṉṟu collappaṭum uṇarvāṉatu ellāvuyir kaḷukkum maṉattoṭu poruntivarum viruppatti aṭippaṭi yākakkoṇṭu tōṟṟuvatu eṉpar āciriyar. eṉavē maṉam poruntiyavaḻip parattiyarmāṭṭum iṉpam uḷatāmeṉavum maṉam poruntātavaḻi maṉiviyarmāṭṭum iṉpamiṉṟā meṉavum viḷakkaṅkūṟuvar iḷampūraṇar. ellāvuyirkkum iṉpam potu eṉavē oḻinta aṟaṉum poruḷum ellāvuyirkkum nikaḻā, makkaṭkē ciṟantu varum eṉak karutturippar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
parattiyiṟ pirivu kāraṇamākap pāṇar mutaliyōri ūṭal tīrkkum vāyilākaaṉupputal, marutanilat talivarkkē ciṟappurimiyuṭiyatāyiṉum nāṉilattalivarkkum potuvāka uriyatākum. avvaḻip piriyum pirivu tam ūrik kaṭantu nikaḻvatilli.
ïilÉlhJ ï‹g« Ef®jnyhL kidaw« Ãfœ¤J« cÇikia cWâahf ÉU«òjyhD«, ÉidbrŒjÈš ÉU¥òila M©k¡fŸ ãÇt® vd¡fUâ mŠR« m¢r« kfË®¡F ïašghjyhD«, fsbthG¡f¤ij¥ òw¤jh®¡F btË¥gL¤Jbk‹W mŠR«go njh‹¿a m«gY« myU« M»a ïUtif¡ F¿¥ãdhY«, jiykfdJ tuÉid vâ® neh¡»ÆUªj ÃiyÆš tªj mtDl‹ mstshîj‰F ïayhjgo ïilôW ne®jyhD« jiytndhL cl‹ngh j‰F¿¥ò« ‘V‹ ï‹D« kzŠbrŒJ bfhŸsÉšiy? eṉa avaṉi viṉavuṅ kuṟippum talimakaḷiṭattē tōṉṟum.
varutta mikutiyik kuṟitta niliyil maṉivāḻkkiyil irakkam tōṉṟutalum urittām. talivaṉ paṇintuḻi accamum nāṇamumiṉṟit talivi appaṇiviṉi yēṟṟuk koḷḷutalum, talivaṉ taṉ talimikku māṟākat taliviyip paṇitalum pulavikkālattu uriyaṉavām. kaḷavukkālattut taliviyiṉ nalam pārāṭṭiya talivaṉ, kaṟpukkālattum avaḷatu eḻil nalam pārāṭṭutaṟku uriyaṉ.
veḷippaṭac collappaṭṭa poruḷiṉ puṟattē taṅkiya kuṟippup poruḷi iṟicciyeṉa vaḻaṅkuvatu tamiḻmarapu, iṟiccip poruḷ eṉpatu urip poruḷiṉ puṟattatākit tōṉṟum poruḷ. aḵtāvatu karupporuḷāki nāṭṭiṟkum ūrkkum tuṟikkum aṭiyāki varuvatu eṉpar iḷam pūraṇar. ittakiya iṟiccipporuḷ perumpāṉmiyum akapporu ḷoḻukalāṟṟil talivaṉatu koṭumikūṟum vaḻip piṟappateṉṟum, kūṟavēṇṭuvatōr poruḷiṉ puṟattē pulappaṭṭu apporuṭku upakārappaṭum poruṭṭaṉmiyiṉi yuṭiyateṉṟum kūṟuvar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. iṟiccitāṉē yurippuṟattuvē eṉa iḷampūraṇarum, iṟiccitāṉē poruṭpuṟattatuvē eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum pāṭaṅkoṇṭaṉar.
iṟiccip poruḷākiya itaṉ kūṟupāṭṭiṉi āḻntu uṇaravallārkku veḷippaṭak kūṟappaṭum poruḷiṉ puṟatta tāki varum avviṟicciyiṉuḷḷē uṭporuḷākat tōṉṟum vēṟuporuḷkaḷum uḷa eṉpar āciriyar. eṉavē iṟiccip poruḷ piṟitumōr poruḷkoḷakkiṭappaṉavum kiṭavā taṉavum eṉa iruvakippaṭum eṉpar iḷampūraṇar. karupporuḷ piṟitōr poruṭku upakārappaṭum poruṭṭātalēyaṉṟi, akkarupporuḷ taṉṉuḷḷē tōṉṟum poruḷum uḷa; aḵtu uḷḷuṟi yuvamattiṉ kūṟṟilē aṭaṅkumāṟupōla naṭakkumiṭattu, avvuḷḷuṟiyuvamam aṉṟu iḵtu iṟicci yeṉṟu ārāyntuṇarum nallaṟivuṭiyōrkku eṉa iṟiccip poruḷiṉ kūṟupāṭṭiṉic ciṟitu virittu viḷakkuvar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.1
talivi, talivaṉatu pirivāṟṟātu varuntiyakālattu, pirintu ceṉṟa talivaṉāl vaḻiyiṭik kāṇappaṭṭa karupporuḷkaḷuḷ avaṉ taliviyi niṉintu aṉpu ceytaṟkut takuvaṉavāka aminta cilavaṟṟiṉ nikaḻccikaḷic cuṭṭi iṟiccip poruḷ paṭat tōḻi kūṟutalum taliviyi vaṟpuṟuttum kuṟippiṉatākum.
taliviyi nōkkit talivaṉ pārāṭṭiya pārāṭṭāṉatu, yāṉ ceyyak karutiya poruḷukku ivaḷ iṭiyūṟāvāḷ kollō eṉat talivaṉ taṉ maṉattuṭkoṇṭa accattiyum tāṉ poruḷ īṭṭutaṟ poruṭṭup pirikiṉṟa ceytiyiṉiyum talivikku uṟutiyākap pulappaṭuttuvatākum.
kaṟpiṉ vaḻippaṭṭa talimakaḷ parattiyip pukaḻntu pārāṭṭiṉāḷāyiṉum, avaḷ maṉattakattē talivaṉōṭu ūṭiṉataṉmi yuṇṭeṉpar nuṇṇuṇarviṉōr. maṟṟorut tiyik kuṟittu ivaḷ ittaṉmiyaḷ eṉat talivaṉukkuc colli, avaḷiṭattil ivaṉ ettaṉmiyaṉāyirukkiṉṟāṉ eṉa avaṉatu uḷḷak kuṟippiṉi yuṇartalum talivikkuriya iyalpākum.
talivaṉāl tām aṭinta tuṉpattiṉip parattiyar taṉakkuk kūṟiya vaḻiyum, tāṉ avaratu tuṉpattiṉi uḷḷavāṟu uṇarnta niliyilum taliviyāṉavaḷ, makiḻcciyum pulaviyumākiya kālattaṉṟi ēṉikkālattut talivaṉatu muṉṉiliyil niṉṟu iṭitturittal illi eṉpar āciriyar. eṉavē talivaṉuṭaṉ makiḻntu aḷavaḷāvum nērattil ammakiḻcciyoṭu kūṭṭi avaṉi iṭitturittalum, talivaṉpāl tavaṟukaṇṭu pulakkuṅkālattu attavaṟukaḷōṭu kūṭṭi iṭitturittalum nuṇṇuṇarvuṭiya talimakaḷiṉ iyalpukaḷām eṉa āciriyar aṟivuṟuttiṉārāyiṟṟu. makiḻcciyum pulaviyum eṉa ivviru niliyum allāta ēṉik kālaṅkaḷil talivaṉik kaḻaṟutal, tacicāṉṟa coṟkākkun taliviyiṉ kaṭamikku ūṟuviḷippa tākaliṉ aṅṅaṉam iṭitturittal kūṭāteṉat tolkāppiyaṉār vilakkiya tiṟam uṇarntu makiḻattakkatākum.
talivaṉ yātēṉum oru paruvatti yelliyākak kuṟittup pirintāṉāka, irutiṅkaḷi yelliyākavuṭiya apparuvam tōṉṟiya poḻutē, ap paruvam kaḻintatu pōlat talivi kūṟutalum uṇṭu. avvāṟu kūṟutal, aṟiyāmi yālāvatu varuttattiṉālāvatu mayakkattālāvatu ap paruvattiṟkuriya poruḷkaḷ mikuntu kāṇappaṭutalālāvatu eṉa innāṉku kāraṇaṅkaḷālum nikaḻum eṉpar āciriyar. eṉavē ivvāṟu varuñ ceyyuḷ kālam piḻittuk kūṟukiṉṟatalla eṉa itaṟku viḷakkantaruvar iḷampūraṇar.
tōḻi, taṉṉi irantu kuṟivēṇṭiya talimakaṉic cēṭpaṭuttu vilakkiniṟuttutalēyaṉṟi, meymmi kūṟutalum poymmi kūṟutalum nalvakiyuṭiya nayavurikaḷik kūṟutalum eṉa ivvāṟu palavakiyālum paṭittu moḻitaluṇṭu.
oruvari yoruvar uyarttuc collutaṟkuriya col, talivaṉ taliviyākiya iruvarkkum okkum. aiyuṟṟuk kūṟuñ col talimakaṉukkē yuriyatākum.
talimakaḷukku uṟṟa tuṉpattit tuṭittal, avaḷōṭu ōṉṟittōṉṟum pēraṉpiṉaḷākiya tōḻiyiṉ iyalpātaliṉ, etiratāk kākkum aṟivākiya uḷḷattiṇmi avaḷpāl naṉku amittiruttal vēṇṭum. taliviyiyum talivaṉiyum uyarttuk kūṟum kūṟṟum tōḻiyākiya avaḷukkē oppavuriya tākum.
akapporuḷ oḻukalāṟṟil uriyāṭutaṟkuriya vāyil kaḷākiya pāṇar kūttar mutaliyōr, tām tām collat takuvaṉavaṟṟit tavaṟiṉṟi veḷippaṭiyākak kūṟutal vēṇṭum eṉa ivviyal 45-m cūttirattil āciriyar teḷivākak kuṟittuḷḷār. ākavē mēṟkuṟitta vāyilkaḷ allāta talimakaḷum naṟṟāyum tām kūṟak karutiyavaṟṟi maṟittuc collap peṟuvar eṉavum, varukiṉṟa (46-m) cūttiram maṟittuc collutalākiya uḷḷuṟi kūṟuvatā taliṉ, attakiya uḷḷuṟi pāṇar kūttar mutaliya vāyil kaḷukku illiyeṉa iccūttirattāl vilakkappaṭṭa teṉavum, vāyilkaḷāvār kuṟṟēval muṟiyiṉarā talāṉum, talimakkaḷākiya kēṭpōr periyōrātalāṉum, veḷippaṭak kūṟākkāl poruḷ viḷaṅ kāmiyāṉum, poruḷ viḷaṅkātāyiṉ ivarkaḷatu kūṟṟiṟkup payaṉiṉmi yāṉum vāyilkaḷāvār maṟittuk kūṟātu veḷippaṭavē kūṟutal vēṇṭumeṉavum uriyāciriyar kūṟiya viḷakkam iṅku nōkkattakuvatākum.
uṭaṉuṟi, uvamam, cuṭṭu, naki, ciṟappu eṉa uḷḷuṟi aintu vakippaṭum eṉpar āciriyar. uṭaṉuṟiyāvatu uṭi ṉuṟivatoṉṟic colla ataṉāṉē piṟitoru poruḷ viḷaṅkuvatu. uvamam eṉpatu uvamiyic colla uvamik kappaṭum poruḷ tōṉṟuvatu. cuṭṭu eṉpatu oru poruḷic cuṭṭip piṟitōr poruṭpaṭutal. nakiyāvatu nakiyiṉāṟ piṟitoru poruḷ uṇara niṟṟal. ciṟappu eṉpatu itaṟkuc ciṟantatu ituveṉak kūṟuvataṉāṉē piṟitōr poruḷ koḷakkiṭappatu eṉa viḷakkaṅkūṟuvar iḷampūraṇar.
uṭaṉuṟiyāvatu, nāṉku nilattum uḷavāy an nilattuṭaṉ uṟiyuṅ karupporuḷāṟ piṟitoṉṟu payappa maṟittukkūṟum iṟicci. uvamamāvatu akkaruvāṟ koḷḷum uḷḷuṟi yuvamamum ēṉiyuvamamum. nakiyum ciṟappum paṟṟātu oṉṟu niṉintu oṉṟu colvaṉavum, aṉpuṟutakuna iṟicciyuṭ cuṭṭi varuvaṉavum cuṭṭeṉappaṭum. nakiyāvatu oṉṟu niṉintu oṉṟu kūṟutal. ēṉiyuvamam niṉṟu, uḷḷuṟiyuva mattit taruṅ karupporuṭkuc ciṟappukkoṭuttu niṟpatu ciṟappu eṉṉum uḷḷuṟiyām. ivi aintum oṉṟaṉi uḷḷuṟutti ataṉi veḷippaṭāmaṟ kūṟutaliṉ uḷḷuṟi yeṉappaṭṭaṉa eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
muṭivillāta ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya akapporuḷ oḻukalāṟṟāl ākiya iṉpamaṉittum ittakiya uḷḷuṟip poruṇmiyilē viḷaṅkit tōṉṟumpaṭi ceyta ciṟappum muṉṉic cāṉṟōr vakutturitta paṇpuṭiya collāṭal muṟiyāl viḷintatē eṉpatu āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār karuttākum.
talivaṉ taṉtaṉmi yeṉpatoṉṟiṉṟi nantaṉmi yeṉak karututaliṉ, yām oṉṟi niṉintu oṉṟu kūṟiṉum avaṉ accoṟkaḷik kēṭṭu vekuḷātu iṉpameṉak koḷvaṉ eṉṟa karuttāl taliviyum tōḻiyum avaṉuṭiya kuṟikaḷik kuṟippākap pulappaṭutti iṭitturikkum niliyiṟ kūṟiya uḷḷuṟip poruṇmiyiṉik kēṭṭa talivaṉ ivi iṉpantarum eṉṟē ēṟṟuk koḷvāṉātalāl1 ittakiya uḷḷuṟip poruṇmiyāl talimakkaḷatu iṉpavuṇarvu vaḷarntu ciṟattal kāṇalām.
maṅkalattāṟ kūṟuñ collum, iṭakkaraṭakkik kūṟuñ collum, kuṟṟamaṟṟa āṇmi kāraṇamākac colliya moḻiyum ākiya iviyellām collāṟ poruḷ paṭāmiyāl muṟkūṟiya uḷḷuṟiyiṉkaṇ aṭaṅkum eṉpar āciriyar.
talimakkaḷukku ākātaṉaveṉṟu meyppāṭṭiyalil vilakkappaṭum ciṉam, pētimi, poṟāmi, vaṟumi ākiya nāṉkum yātāṉu moru poruḷic ciṟappittal kāraṇamāka avarpāṟ cārttiyurikkappaṭum.
aṉṉi eṉṟa collāl tōḻi taliviyiyum talivi tōḻiyiyum aḻittalum, eṉṉi eṉṟa collāl avviruvarum talivaṉi aḻittalum uḷa. iccol vaḻakkukaḷ colliṉālum ataṟkuṟuppākiya eḻuttiṉālum poruḷ tōṉṟāta marapiṉiyuṭiyaṉa eṉpar āciriyar.
oppu, uru, veṟuppu, kaṟpu, ēr, eḻil, cāyal, nāṇ, maṭaṉ, nōy, vēṭki, nukarvu eṉa iṅṅaṉam varum collellām, nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟa vaḻakkiyal marapiṉālē poruḷi maṉattiṉāl uṇariṉallatu itaṉatu vaṭivam ituveṉap poṟikaḷāl tāṉum uṇarntu piṟarkkum teriyak kāṭṭa muṭiyātaṉavākiya piḻampilporuḷkaḷi uṇarttuvaṉavākum. imiyāk kaṇkaḷiyuṭiya tēvarulakilum kaṭalcūḻnta innilavulakattilum mēṟkuṟittaṉavākiya apporuḷkaḷ illāta kālam eṉpatu illāmiyāl (ekkālattum uḷḷamiyāl) oppu mutal nukarvu īṟākac collappaṭṭa avi yāvum kaṭpulaṉākiya vaṭivamillātaṉavāyiṉum eṉṟum uḷporuḷkaḷeṉavē koḷḷattakkaṉa eṉpar āciriyar.
iṅṅaṉam āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉārāl oppu mutalāka eṭutturikkappaṭṭa ipporuḷkaḷi uṇmi māttira muṇarttip piḻampu uṇarttappaṭātaṉa eṉṟa pakuppiṉuḷ iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyaluriyāciriyar aṭakkik kāṭṭiṉami iṅku niṉikkat takuvatākum.

meyppāṭṭiyal
maṉattiṉāl uyttuṇariṉallatu aimpoṟikaḷāl uṇarntukoḷḷa muṭiyāta poruḷkaḷ cilavaṟṟi mēl poruḷiyalil iṟutiyil tokuttōtiṉār. vaṭivamillā taṉavākiya apporuḷkaḷiyum poṟi vāyilāka maṉaṅkoḷḷutaṟku ētuvāvaṉa meyppāṭukaḷākum. ulakattāratu uḷḷa nikaḻcci avaratu uṭampiṉkaṇ tōṉṟum kaṇṇīrarumpal, memmayir cilirttal, viyarttal, naṭukkam mutaliya puṟakkuṟikaḷāl kāṇpōrkkup pulaṉākuntaṉmi meyppāṭeṉappaṭum.
oruvaṉ puli mutaliya koṭiya vilaṅkukaḷikkaṇṭu añciya niliyil, avaṉuḷḷattilē iṉṉatu ceyvareṉṟu oṉṟun tōṉṟātu kalaṅkum kalakkamum, piṉ evvāṟēṉum tappi maṟital vēṇṭumeṉṟa karuttum, avaṉatu uṭampiṉ kaṇṇē naṭukkamum, viyarttalum uṇṭātal iyalpu. ivaṟṟuḷ accattiṟkētuvākiya puli mutaliyaṉa cuvippaṭu poruḷ eṉappaṭum. avaṟṟik kaṇṭatumutal avaṉuḷḷattilē nīṅkātu niṉṟa accam cuviyeppaṭum. atukāraṇamāka avaṉuḷḷattilē tōṉṟum kalakkamum maṟitaṟkaruttum kuṟippeṉappaṭum, akkuṟippiṉvaḻi avaṉatu uṭampilē veḷippaṭṭut tōṉṟum naṭukkamum viyarttalum viṟal eṉappaṭum. viṟali vaṭanūlār cattuvam eṉa vaḻaṅkuvār. naṭukkamum viyarppum ākiya cattuvaṅkaḷ, accamuṟṟāṉākiya avaṉukkēyaṉṟi, añci ōṭi varum avaṉikkaṇṭa ēṉiyōrkkum naṉku pulaṉāvaṉa. kuṟippum cuviyuṇarvum ākiya ēṉiyavi avaṉ maṉa nikaḻccikaḷākum. accamuṟṟāṉ maṉattē nikaḻum accam, avaṉuṭampil tōṉṟum naṭukkam, viyarttal mutaliya puṟakkuṟikaḷāl kāṇpōrkkup pulaṉākun taṉmi meyppāṭeṉak koḷḷappaṭum. meyyiṉkaṇ tōṉṟutaliṉ meyppāṭāyiṟṟu eṉpar iḷam pūraṇar. mey - uṭampu. paṭutal - tōṉṟutal. paṭu eṉṉum mutaṉilit toḻiṟpeyar pāṭu eṉa nīṇṭu niṉṟatu.
iṉi, mey eṉṟa collukkup poruḷiṉ uṇmit taṉmi eṉap poruḷkoṇṭu,1 meyppāṭu eṉpataṟkup poruḷiṉ pulappāṭu eṉap poruḷ virittalum uṇṭu. meyppāṭu eṉpatu poruṭpāṭu aḵtāvatu ulakattār uḷḷa nikaḻcci āṇṭu nikaḻntavāṟē puṟattārkkup pulappaṭu vatōrāṟṟāṉ veḷippaṭutal ataṉatu ilakkaṇaṅkūṟiya ōttum ākupeyarāṉ meyppāṭṭiya leṉṟāyiṟṟu eṉap pērāciriyar kūṟum viḷakkam iṅku niṉikkat takuvatākum.
makkaḷatu akavāḻvum puṟavāḻvum ākiya ulakiyal vaḻakkilē pulappaṭṭut tōṉṟum immeyppāṭukaḷip puṉinturi vakiyākiya nāṭaka vaḻakkiṟkum pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkamākiya ceyyuḷ vaḻakkiṟkum aṅkamākakkoḷḷutal toṉṟu toṭṭu varum tamiḻilakkaṇa marapākum. immarapiṉi uḷaṅkoṇṭa tolkāppiyaṉār, immeyppāṭukaḷip pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkamākiya ceyyuḷukkuriya uṟuppukkaḷuḷ oṉṟākak koṇṭu ivviyalil virittu viḷakkukiṉṟār. ivviyal irupattēḻu cūttiraṅkaḷāl iyaṉṟatākum.
paṇṇittōṉṟiya muppattiraṇṭu poruḷkaḷiyuṅ kuṟittu avaṟṟiṉ puṟattu nikaḻum poruḷkaḷ patiṉāṟeṉṟu colluvar. mēṟcollappaṭṭa patiṉāṟu poruḷum eṭṭākiyaṭaṅkum pakutiyum uṇṭu eṉa meyppāṭukaḷik kuṟittu nāṭaka nūlār koṇṭa pākupāṭṭiṉi ivviyaliṉ mutaliraṇṭu cūttiraṅkaḷāl tolkāppiyaṉār kuṟip piṭṭuḷḷār, paṇṇi eṉpatu viḷiyāṭṭu eṉṟa poruḷil vaḻaṅkum uric collākum.1 accol viḷiyāṭṭiṉi yuṭiya kūṭṭam eṉṟa poruḷil iṅku āḷappeṟṟuḷḷatu. paṇṇiyiyuṭiyatu paṇṇiyeṉṟāyiṟṟu eṉpar iḷampūraṇar. muṭiyuṭi vēntarum kuṟunila maṉṉarum mutalāyiṉār nāṭaka makaḷir āṭalum pāṭalum kaṇṭuṅ kēṭṭuṅ kāmam nukarum iṉpa viḷiyāṭṭilē, naki mutaliya cuvikaḷukkuk kāraṇamākiya cuvippaṭu poruḷkaḷum, avaṟṟi nukarntavaḻi uḷavām cuviyum, accuvi paṟṟit tōṉṟum maṉakkuṟippum, akkuṟippiṉvaḻi meyyiṉkaṇ veḷippaṭum cattuvamum ākiya ivi ciṟantu tōṉṟutal iyalpātaliṉ, paṇṇit tōṉṟiya eṇṇāṉku poruḷum eṉak kuṟittār āciriyar. viḷiyāṭṭāyattiṉkaṇ tōṉṟiya muppattiraṇṭāvaṉa, naki mutaliya cuvikaḷukku ētuvākap piṉṉark kūṟappaṭum eḷḷal mutal viḷiyāṭ ṭīṟākavuḷḷa cuvippaṭu poruḷkaḷ eṉavum, avaṟṟik kuṟitta puṟaṉāvaṉa vīram, accam, iḻippu, viyappu, kāmam, avalam, uruttiram, naki, naṭuvu nilimi eṉṟum, vīrakkuṟippu, accakkuṟippu, iḻippukkuṟippu, viyappukkuṟippu, kāmakkuṟippu, avalakkuṟippu, uruttirakkuṟippu, nakikkuṟippu, naṭuvunilik kuṟippu eṉṟum collappaṭṭa patiṉeṭṭiṉum naṭuvu nilimiyum ataṉ kuṟippum oḻittu ēṉiya patiṉāṟumām eṉavum, ivi patiṉāṟiṉiyum cuviyuḷ aṭakkic cuviyeṭṭum ākki nikaḻtal uṇṭu eṉavum kūṟuvar iḷampūraṇar.2
oṉpatu cuviyuḷ uruttiram1 oḻittu eṭṭaṉiyuṅ kūṟuṅkāl, cuvikkappaṭum poruḷum, ataṉi nukarnta poṟiyuṇarvum, atu maṉattuppaṭṭavaḻi uḷḷattu nikaḻum kuṟippum, kuṟippukkaḷ piṟanta uḷḷattāṟ kaṇṇīrarumpalum meymmayir cilirttalum mutalāka uṭampiṉkaṇ varum vēṟu pāṭākiya cattuvaṅkaḷum eṉa nāṉkākki, ac cuvi yeṭṭōṭuṅ kūṭṭi, oṉṟu nāṉku ceytu uṟaḻa muppatti raṇṭām eṉpatum, eṉavē cuvipporuḷum, cuviyuṇarvum, kuṟippum, viṟalum eṉa nāṉku vakippaṭṭu nikaḻum immuppattiraṇṭu poruḷkaḷiyē paṇṇit tōṉṟiya eṇṇāṉku poruḷum eṉa iccūttirattil tolkāppi yaṉār kuṟittaṉar eṉpatum pērāciriyar karuttākum.
iṉi, cūttirattiluḷḷa paṇṇi eṉṟa collukkut tokuti eṉap poruḷkoṇṭu, eḷḷal, iḷami, pētimi, maṭaṉ eṉṟāṟpōla nannāṉkāyp paṇṇikūṭi varum muppattiraṇṭu poruḷum eṉa viḷakkaṅ kūṟutalum uṇṭu.2
naki, aḻuki mutaliya cuvikaḷukku ētuvākiya cuvip poruḷkaḷi nāṭaka araṅkilē niṟutti, avaṟṟik kaṇṭu kuṟippum cattuvamum nikaḻttukiṉṟa kūttaṉiyum araṅkilē koṇarntu niṟutti, piṉṉar avaṉ ceykiṉṟa meyppāṭṭiṉi aviyiluḷḷōr kaṇṭu uṇarvatāka varukiṉṟa muṟimi yellām nāṭaka vaḻakkiṟkēyuriya pakutiyākum. appakutiyellām iyaṟṟamiḻ nūlil uṇarttattakkaṉa alla eṉak karutiya tolkāppiyaṉār meyppāṭu paṟṟiya nāṭakanūṟkōṭpāṭu kaḷip piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟal eṉṉum uttipaṟṟi ivviyaliṉ mutaliraṇṭu cūttiraṅkaḷāl tokuttuc cuṭṭiṉār.3 nāṭaka vaḻakkiṉum ulakiyal vaḻakkiṉum pāṭutaṟkaminta pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkup paṟṟit tām uṇartta eṭuttukkoṇṭa meyp pāṭukaḷi ivviyaliluḷḷa ēṉiya cūttiraṅkaḷāl virittu viḷakkukiṉṟār.
naki, aḻuki, iḷivaral, maruṭki, accam, perumitam, vekuḷi, uvaki eṉac collappaṭṭa eṭṭum meyppāṭu eṉṟu colluvar āciriyar. iṅkē kūṟa eṭuttukkoṇṭa eṇvaki meyppāṭukaḷum muṭiyuṭi vēntarum kuṟunila maṉṉarum mutalāyiṉōr nāṭaka makaḷir mutaliyōr nikaḻttum āṭalum pāṭaluṅ kaṇṭuṅ kēṭṭum kāmanukarum iṉpa viḷiyāṭṭiṉ nikaḻccikaḷōṭu oruvāṟṟāl toṭarpuṭi yāṉavē ? eṉpār, viḷiyāṭṭup poruḷiṉatākiya nakiyeṉṉum meyppāṭṭi mutaṟkaṇvittār. nakikku maṟutaliyāvatu aḻukiyātaliṉ ataṉi ataṉpiṉ vittār. aḻukiyum iḷivaralōṭu okkumātalāl aḻukiyiṉ piṉ iḷivarali vittār. tām iḷivanta niliyil tammiṉum uyarntavaṟṟi yeṇṇi viyattal makkaḷatu iyalpātaliṉ iḷivaraliṉpiṉ maruṭki vittār. viyappākiya maruṭki paṟṟiyum accam piṟattaliṉ accatti ataṉpiṉ vittār. accattiṟku maṟutali vīramātaliṉ accattiṉpiṉ vīratti vittār. vīrattiṉ payaṉākap piṟappatu vekuḷiyātaliṉ vīrattiṉpiṉ vekuḷiyi vittār. vekuḷikku maṟutaliyātalāṉum ellāc cuvikaḷiṉum ciṟantatātalāṉum mutaliṟ kūṟiya naki yuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭi yatātalāṉum uvakiyi iṟutikkaṇ vittār eṉa nakimutal uvakiyīṟakavuḷḷa eṇvaki meyppāṭukaḷiṉ vippu muṟikkup pērāciriyar kūṟum kāraṇaṅkaḷ niṉikkat takkaṉavākum.
eṇvaki meyppāṭukaḷuḷ nakiyeṉpatu eḷḷal, iḷami, pētami, maṭaṉ eṉa in nāṉkum paṟṟi nikaḻum eṉpar āciriyar. eḷḷal-ikaḻtaṟ kuṟippu. iḷami-viḷivaṟiyāta iḷamparuva iyalpu. pētami aṟiviṉmi. maṭaṉ- perum pāṉmiyum piṟar aṟivikka aṟintu aṟintavaṟṟi nekiḻa viṭāmi. maṭam eṉpatu poruṇmi yaṟiyātu tiriyak kōṭal eṉṟum, pētami eṉpatu kēṭṭataṉi uyttuṇarātu meyyāīk kōṭal eṉṟum ivviraṇṭiṟkum vēṟupāṭu kūṟuvar iṉampūraṇar. meyppāṭākap puṟattē veḷippaṭum nakiyoṉṟē, taṉ tōṟṟattiṟkuriya kāraṇaṅkaḷāka akattē nikaḻum eḷḷal, iḷami, pētimi, maṭaṉ eṉṉum innālvaki maṉak kuṟippukkaḷum pulappaṭutaṟ kuriya niliyil nālvakippaṭṭuttōṉṟum eṉpār, uḷḷappaṭṭa naki nāṉkeṉpa eṉṟār āciriyar. ivvāṟē aḻaki mutal uvakiyīṟākavuḷḷa ēṉiya meyppāṭukaḷum tattam tōṟṟat tiṟkuk kāraṇamāka akattē tōṉṟum cuvipporuḷkaḷuk kēṟpap puṟattē nālvakippaṭa nikaḻum eṉṟa nuṭpattiṉi avaṟṟiṉ iyalpurikkum cūttiraṅkaḷil āciriyar pulappaṭutti yuḷḷār.
iṉi, eḷḷal paṟṟiya nakiyeṉpatu, tāṉ piṟari ikaḻntu nakutalum piṟarāl ikaḻappaṭṭa niliyil tāṉ nakutalum eṉa iraṇṭām. iḷami paṟṟiya nakiyeṉpatu, taṉ iḷami kāraṇamākap piṟarikkaṇṭu nakutalum piṟaratu iḷamikaṇṭu tāṉ nakutalum eṉa iruvakippaṭum. taṉ pētimi poruḷākat tōṉṟuvatum piṟar pētimi poruḷākat tōṉṟuvatum eṉap pētimi paṟṟiya naki iruvakippaṭum. ivvāṟē taṉkaṇ tōṉṟiya maṭami kāraṇa mākavum piṟar kaṇtōṉṟiya maṭami kāraṇamākavum naki tōṉṟumātaliṉ maṭami paṟṟiya nakiyum iruvakippaṭum eṉpar.
iḷivu, iḻavu. acivu, vaṟumi eṉa innāṉku poruḷpaṟṟi aḻuki tōṉṟum. ivaṟṟuḷ iḷivu eṉpatu piṟarāl ikaḻappaṭaṭu eḷiyarātal. iḻavu eṉpatu tanti tāy mutaliya cuṟṟattāriyum iṉpam payakkum nukarcci mutaliyavaṟṟiyum iḻattal. acivu eṉpatu muṉṉiya nalla nilimi keṭṭu vēṟupaṭṭu varuntutal. vaṟumi yeṉpatu pōkan tuykkappeṟāta paṟṟuḷḷam. ivi nāṉkum taṉkaṇ tōṉṟiṉum piṟarkaṇ tōṉṟiṉum aḻukiyām ātaliṉ iviyum eṭṭāyiṉa eṉpar pērāciriyar. taṉkaṇ tōṉṟiya iḷivupaṟṟip piṟakkum avalatti aḻukiyeṉṟum piṟarkaṇ tōṉṟiya iḷivupaṟṟip piṟakkum avalattik karuṇiyeṉṟum kūṟutal marapu.1
mūppu, piṇi, varuttam, meṉmi eṉa innāṉku poruḷ paṟṟi iḷivaral tōṉṟum. mūppu-mutumi kāraṇamākat tōṉṟum taḷarcci. piṇi-nōy. varuttam-payaṉ viḷiyāta vīṇ muyaṟci. meṉmi-āṟṟalum poruḷum iṉṟi eḷiyarām nilimi. ivi nāṉkum taṉkaṇ tōṉṟutalum piṟarkaṇ tōṉṟutalum paṟṟi eṭṭātaluṭiya.
putumi, perumi, ciṟumi, ākkam eṉa innāṉku poruḷpaṟṟi maruṭki tōṉṟum. putumi-putitākak kaṇṭatu. perumi-mikap periyatu. ciṟumi-mika nuṇṇiyatu. ākkam- oṉṟu tirintu oṉṟākiyatu. innāṉkum muṟkūṟiyaṉa pōlat taṉkaṇ tōṉṟutalum piṟarkaṇ tōṉṟutalum paṟṟi eṭṭā vaṉa. ulaka vaḻakkiṉuḷ niṉṟavāṟu nillāmal aṟiviṉit tirittu vēṟupaṭuttuvatu viyappeṉṉum meyppāṭātaliṉ ataṉi matimi cālā maruṭki eṉa aṭipuṇarttōṉār āciriyar. eṉavē tiripiṉṟi iyalpākiya aṟivuṭaṉ kūṭiya niliyil maruṭki tōṉṟutaṟku iṭamilli yeṉpatu pulaṉām.
aṇaṅku, vilaṅku, kaḷvar, tam iṟi eṉa innāṉku poruḷ paṟṟi accam tōṉṟum. aṇaṅkāvaṉa etirppaṭṭāri varuttum iyalpiṉavākiya pēy pūtam mutaliyaṉa. vilaṅkāvaṉa arimā, puli mutalākavuḷḷa koṭiya vilaṅkukaḷ. kaḷvarāvār cōrvu pārttu vañcittuk koṭuntoḻil purivōr. tam iṟiyeṉṟatu tanti āciriyaṉ, aracaṉ, vaḻipaṭu teyvam eṉa ivvurimi muṟiyiṟ paṇikoṇṭu tammi āḷum talivari añcattakkaṉavākiya ivaṟṟik kaṇṭa niliyil uḷḷam naṭukkamuṟṟu añcutal iyalpu. iṅṅaṉam naṭuṅkātu piṇaṅki niṟpāratu maṉattil accam tōṉṟuvatilli yeṉpār piṇaṅkal cālā accam eṉṟār. accattiṟkuk kāraṇamākiya ivi nāṉkum taṉkaṇ tōṉṟuvaṉa piṟarkaṇ tōṉṟuvaṉa eṉa irupāṟpaṭātu piṟitu poruḷeṉa orupāṟpaṭṭē niṟpaṉavām.
kalvi, taṟukaṇ, icimi koṭi eṉṟa in nāṉkum kāraṇamākap perumitam tōṉṟum. perumitamāvatu ellā rōṭum oppanillātu pērelliyāka niṟṟal eṉpar pērāciriyar. eṉavē aṟivu āṇmi poruḷ mutaliya ciṟappukkaḷāl makkaḷ ellārōṭum oppanillātu uyarntu niṟṟal perumitam eṉak koḷḷutal poruntum. kalvi eṉpatu tavam mutalākiya ceyaliṉ tiṟam. taṟukaṇ eṉpatu uḷḷattu uṟutiyākiya vīram. icimi eṉpatu ekkālattum paḻiyoṭu varuvaṉa ceyyāmiyākiya pukaḻttiṟam. koṭi eṉpatu uyirum uṭampum uṟuppum mutaliya ellāp poruḷuṅ koṭuttalākiya vaṇmit tiṟam. ivināṉkum oruvarpāl aminta niliyil avariṭattē perumitam eṉṉum meyppāṭu tōṉṟutal iyalpu. eṉavē itu taṉkaṇ tōṉṟiya poruḷpaṟṟi varum eṉṟār pērāciriyar.
uṟuppaṟi, kuṭikōḷ, ali, koli eṉṟa innāṉkum paṟṟi vekuḷi tōṉṟum. uṟuppaṟiyāvatu kiyi veṭṭutalum kaṇṇit tōṇṭutalum pōṉṟa koṭuñ ceyalkaḷ. kuṭikōḷ eṉpatu piṟaratu kuṭippiṟappiṉ uyarvukkum avaruṭiya cuṟṟattārkkum kēṭu cūḻtal. ali eṉpatu araciyal neṟikku māṟākak kōlkoṇṭu alittal mutaliya tīttoḻilkaḷ. koli eṉpatu piṟaruṭiya aṟivum pukaḻum mutaliya naṉmiḷi aḻittup pēcutal. iṅṅaṉam nālvakippaṭak kūṟappeṟṟa ik koṭuntoḻilkaḷ kāraṇamāka makkaḷatu maṉattē vekuḷi tōṉṟutal iyalpu. ivvekuḷi piṟarkaṇ tōṉṟiya poruḷpaṟṟi varuvatākum.
celvam, pulaṉ, puṇarvu viḷiyāṭṭu eṉṟa innāṉkum kāraṇamāka uvaki tōṉṟum, celvam eṉṟatu celvattāl uṇṭākum nukarcciyiṉi. pulaṉ eṉṟatu kalvip payaṉākiya aṟivuṭimi. puṇarvu eṉṟatu aṉpoṭu puṇarnta aintiṇi maruṅkiṟ kāmap puṇarcciyiṉi. viḷiyāṭṭeṉṟatu yāṟum kuḷamum cōliyum mutalākiya vaṉappumikka iṭaṅkaḷil taṅkit tuṇiyoṭu viḷiyāṭi makiḻum viḷiyāṭṭiṉi. ivi nāṉkum poruḷāka uvakic cuvi piṟakkum.
ulakiyal vāḻviṟ piṟaratu tuṉpattiṉik kaṇṭu kīḻ makkaḷaṭiyum pōli makiḻcci uṇmiyāṉa uvakiyākātu eṉa aṟivuṟuttum nōkkattuṭaṉ allal nītta uvaki eṉa aṭipuṇarttōtiya āciriyaratu pulamittiṟaṉ uṇarttu pārāṭṭat takuvatākum.
mēṟcollappaṭṭa meyppāṭukaḷ muppattiraṇṭum oru pakkamāka, maṟṟorupakkam uṭimi, iṉpuṟal, naṭuvunili aruḷal, taṉmi, aṭakkam, varital, aṉpu kimmikal, nalital, cūḻcci, vāḻttal, nāṇal, tuñcal, araṟṟu, kaṉavu, muṉital, niṉital, verūutal, maṭimi, karutal, ārāycci, virivu, uyirppu, kiyāṟu, iṭukkaṇ, poccāppu, poṟami, viyarttal, aiyam, miki, naṭukkam eṉa varum immuppattiraṇṭum uḷavāvaṉa. ivi mēṟkūṟiya muppattiraṇṭu meyppāṭukaḷuḷ aṭaṅkāta niliyilētāṉ taṉi meyppāṭukaḷākak koḷḷattakkaṉa eṉpar āciriyar.1
iṅṅaṉam ivviyalil 3 mutal 12 variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅ kaḷāl akattiṇikkum puṟattiṇikkum potuvāki nikaḻum meyppāṭukaḷi vakutturitta āciriyar 13 mutal 25 muṭiya vuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷāl akattiṇikkē ciṟappurimiyuṭiya meyppāṭukaḷi virattu viḷakkukiṉṟār. akattiṇiyuḷ kaḷaveṇṇum oḻukalāṟṟiṟkuc ciṟantuvarum meyppāṭukaḷik kūṟat toṭaṅkiya tolkāppiyaṉār, puṇarkkum pālākiya nallūḻiṉ āṇiyāl aṉpiṟ ciṟantārākiya oruvaṉum oruttiyum etirppaṭṭa vaḻi, avvetirppāṭākiya kāṭci toṭaṅkip puṇarcciyaḷavum nikaḻum meyppāṭukaḷ mūṉṟu kūṟukaḷāmeṉavum, avviruvarum meyyuṟṟuk kūṭiya puṇarccikkup piṉ maṟivil nikaḻum oḻukalāṟākiya akkaḷavu veḷippaṭumaḷavum nikaḻum meyppāṭukaḷ mūṉṟu kūṟukaḷā meṉavum, iviyāṟum ovvoṉṟum nannāṉku pakutikaḷiyuṭiyavāki oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāya muṟiyē tōṉṟumeṉavum viritturikkiṉṟār.
talivaṉum taliviyum oruvariyoruvar etirppaṭṭa mutaṟkāṭciyilē, taṉṉit talivaṉ nōkkutaṟkaṇ talivi māṟupaṭātu virumpi niṟkum uḷḷa nikaḻcci pukumukam purital eṉappaṭum. ivvāṟu talivaṉ taṉṉi nōkkiya niliyil accamum nāṇum oruṅku vantaṭitalāl viyarvi yarumpiya neṟṟiyiyuṭiyaḷātal poṟinutal viyarttal eṉṉum meyppāṭām. ataṉ piṉṉart talivaṉiṭattē tōṉṟiya kuṟippukkaḷāl avaṉuṭaṉ aḷavaḷāvi makiḻavēṇṭu meṉṟa viruppam taṉṉuḷḷattē tōṉṟiya niliyilum avviruppam puṟattē veḷippaṭātapaṭi maṟittal nakunaya maṟittal eṉappaṭum. iṅṅaṉam talimakaḷ taṉatu viruppattiṉi maṟit tāḷāyiṉum uḷḷañ citintu niṟiyaḻiyumātalāl taṉ citivu puṟattārkkup pulaṉākātapaṭi taṉatu neñciṉi niṟutta muyalutal iyalpām. attakiyamuyaṟci citivu piṟarkkiṉmi, eṉṉum meyppāṭām. ivvāṟu oṉṟaṉ piṉ oṉṟu tōṉṟu taṟkut takumeṉappaṭa innāṉku meyppāṭukaḷum kaḷaviṟkuriya mutaṟkūṟākum eṉpar āciriyar.
mēṟkuṟitta vaṇṇam talimakaḷ taṉatu maṉacciti viṉip puṟattārkkup pulappaṭāmal maṟitta vaḻiyum, uḷḷa neḻcciyālē uṭampoṭu toṭarpuṭiyatāki vēṟupaṭṭa avaḷatu kūntalākiya muṭi taṉ vayattataṉṟi nekiḻntu tāḻtal kūḻi virittal, eṉappaṭum. kūntalippōlaṉṟik kātiṭip peytu aṇiyappeṟṟa tōṭu mutaliya aṇikaḷuḷ oṉṟu niṟpa maṟṟoṉṟu nekiḻntu vīḻtal kātoṉṟu kaḷital eṉṉum meyppāṭākum. taṉatu uṭampiṉ vēṟupāṭuṇarnta talimakaḷ tōṭupōla nekiḻap peyyappaṭu muṟimiyiṉa vaṉṟic ciṟitu iṟukac ceṟittaṇiyum muṟimiyiṉavākiya kivaḷi, mōtiram mutaliya aṇikalaṉ kaḷik kaḻaṉṟu viḻātapaṭi iṟukac ceṟittuk koḷḷutal ūḻaṇitivaral eṉappaṭum. taṉatu uṭampiṉ nekiḻcciyiyuṇarnta talimakaḷ tāṉ uṭutta uṭiyiṉip pala muṟiyum iṟuka uṭuttuk koḷḷutal uṭipeyarttuṭuttal eṉṉum meyppāṭām. im meyppāṭukaḷ nāṉkum kaḷaviṟ kuriya iraṇṭāṅkūṟeṉpar āciriyar. taliviyiṉ uḷḷac citivaṟintu talivaṉ avaḷi meyyuṟa aṇukiya niliyil nikaḻvaṉa immeyppāṭukaḷātaliṉ ivi kaḷaviṉ iraṇṭām kūṟu eṉa muṟippaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa.
muṟkūṟiyavāṟu uṭi peyarttuṭutta talimakaḷ, uṭi peritum nekiḻnta niliyil taṉkiyāl aṟṟam maṟittal alkul tivaral eṉappaṭum. ataṉiccāra iṭiyil aṇinta kaṭi cūttira mutaliyavaṟṟi nekiḻātu tiruttip pōṟṟik koḷḷutal aṇintavi tiruttal eṉṉum meyppāṭām. ivvāṟutaṉ valiyaṟṟa niliyilum talimakaḷ tāṉ puṇarcciyi vēṇṭātāḷ pōlvatōr vaṉmiyi mēṟkoṇṭu niṟṟal ilvaliyuṟuttal eṉappaṭum. (illāta  vaṉmiyi mikuttal eṉpatu ittoṭariṉ poruḷākum.) iṅṅaṉam talimakaḷ taṉkaṇ uḷatākap paṭittukkoṇṭa vaṉmiyiṉālum taṭukkappaṭātu neñcattiṉ niṟiyaḻitalāl taṉ irukikaḷum talivaṉi muyaṅkum viruppattāl tāmē eḻuvaṉa pōlvatōr kuṟippuṭiyaḷātal irukiyum eṭuttal eṉṉum meyppāṭākum. ivi nāṉkum kaḷaviṉ mūṉṟām kūṟeṉpar āciriyar. mūṉṟu kūṟukaḷākap pakutturitta ippaṉṉiraṇṭum iyaṟkip puṇarcciyākiya mutaṟ kūṭṭattiṟku muṉṉē nikaḻvaṉavām. oruvaṉum oruttiyum etirppaṭṭavaḻi taṉatu maṉakkaruttiṉi nāṇum niṟiyumākiya kuṇaṅkaḷāl puṟat tārkkup pulaṉākātu maṟikkum nikaḻcci peṇmiyiṉ iyalpātalāl iṅkē kūṟappaṭṭa meyppāṭukaḷ perupāṉmiyum talimakaḷ kaṇṇē ciṟantu nikaḻum eṉpar pērāciriyar.
puṇarcci nikaḻnta piṉṉart talimakaṉatu perumiyi niṉintu talivi pārāṭṭum uḷḷak kuṟippiṉaḷātal pārāṭ ṭeṭuttal eṉappaṭum. viḷiyāṭum paruvattu iyalpākiya maṭami nīṅkak kāmap poruṭkaṇṇē ciṟitu aṟivu tōṉṟa uriyāṭuṅ kuṟippiṉaḷātal maṭantapa vurittal eṉṉum meyppāṭām. ivvoḻukkam ciṟitu veḷippaṭum niliyiṟ cuṟṟattār kūṟum kaṭuñ coṟkaḷi muṉiyātu ēṟṟuk koṇṭu itu puṟattārkkup pulaṉākumō eṉa nāṇum uḷḷak kuṟippiṉaḷātal īramil kūṟṟam ēṟṟalar nāṇal eṉappaṭum. talimakaṉ aṉpiṉāṟ koṭutta kiyuṟip poruḷkaḷi maṟukkātu ēṟṟukkoṇṭu avaṟṟip pārāṭṭum uḷḷamuṭiyaḷātal koṭuppavi kōṭal eṉṉum meyppāṭām. iṅṅaṉam muṟiyē oṉṟaṉpiṉoṉ ṟākat tōṉṟutaṟkuriya ivi nāṉkum kaḷaviṉ nāṉkām kūṟeṉpar āciriyar.
uyiriṉuñ ciṟanta nāṇuṭiyaḷākiya talimakaḷ, taṉṉik kuṟittu ayalār kūṟum paḻiccoṟkaḷukku nāṇi, ivvoḻukalāṟṟiṉi nam peṟṟōrkkut terivippōmā aṉṟit teriviyātiruppōmā eṉat taṭumāṟip piṉ oruvakiyāl ārāyntu, taṉ kulattiṟkum oḻukkattiṟkum peṇtaṉmikkum  ēṟṟavakiyāl colla vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟit terintu koṇṭu iṉṉavāṟu nikaḻntateṉṟu taṉtōḻikku uṭampaṭutalum avaḷ vaḻiyākac cevilikku uṭampaṭutalum ākiya kuṟippiṉaḷātal terintuṭampaṭutal eṉappaṭum. talivaṉuṭaṉ muṉṉarp palanāḷ pakalum iravum aḷavaḷāvi makiḻntavāṟu pōṉṟu kūṭi makiḻtalit taṉakku iyalpākiya accamum nāṇamum maṭaṉum kāraṇamāka maṟukkum kuṟippiṉa ḷātal tiḷippu viṉi maṟuttal eṉṉum meyppāṭām. talimakaḷ peṟṟōrāl iṟceṟikkappaṭutal kāraṇamākat tāṉ talivaṉatu kūṭṭatti maṟutta ētattiṟku nāṇiyum añciyum avaṉ muṉṉar veḷippaṭātu maṉiyakattē maṟintoḻukuṅ karuttuṭiyaḷātal karantiṭat toḻital eṉappaṭum. iṅṅaṉam talivaṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟātu maṟintoḻukuvāḷ orunāḷ etirpārāta niliyil avaṉik kāṇaluṟṟapoḻutu elliyaṟṟa perumakiḻcci yuṭiḷātal kaṇṭavaḻi yuvattal eṉṉum meyppāṭām. ivi nāṉkum kaḷaviṉatu aintāṅ kūṟeṉappaṭum.
pūvuñ cāntum pūṇun tukilum mutalāyiṉa koṇṭu taṉṉip puṟattē alaṅkaritta niliyilum talimakaḷ taṉ aṉ piṟkiṉiya talivaṉik kūṭap peṟāmiyāl taṉ akattē makiḻcciyiṉṟi neñcaḻintu cōrtal puṟañ ceyac citital eṉappaṭum. cuṟṟattār palarum cūḻa avarkaḷ naṭuvē tāṉ vāḻum niliyilum talivaṉatu tuṇiyiṉṟi varuntutalāl tāṉ taṉiyaḷ eṉṟu aṟivikkum karuttiṉaḷātal pulampit tōṉṟal eṉappaṭum. taṉimiyuḷḷattaḷākiya talivi kiyuṅkaḷavumākap piṭippaṭṭa kaḷvarip pōṉṟu tāṉ colluvaṉavaṟṟi maṉat taṭumāṟṟantōṉṟac collutal kalaṅki moḻital eṉappaṭum. taṉatu maṉakkalakkatti yaṭakkik koṇṭu pēcum niliyilum taṉatu ceyalaṟṟa taṉmi tōṉṟak kūṟutal kiyaṟavurittal eṉappaṭum. taṉimi vikaṟpamākiya ivi nāṉkum kaḷaviṉ āṟām kūṟeṉpar āciriyar.
kiyaṟavuri tōṉṟiyataṉ piṉṉar nikaḻvaṉa oru talik kāmamākiya kikkiḷikkum ovvāk kāmamākiya peruntiṇikkum meyppāṭāvaṉavaṉṟi naṭuvaṇin tiṇi yeṉappaṭṭa naṟ kāmattukku ēṟpuṭiyaṉa alla eṉavum, kaḷavoḻukkattiṉuḷ kiyaṟavurittal eṉṟa elliyiṉ mīṟiya meyppāṭukaḷ kūṟap paṭāveṉavum aṟivuṟuttak karutiya āciriyar, ciyaṟavurittal eṉṟa meyppāṭṭiṉik kaḷavoḻukkattiṉ iṟutikkaṇ vittār. talimakaṉpāl tōṉṟum kuṟippuc cila paṟṟit talimakaḷpāl pala kuṟipput tōṉṟumātalāl talimakaḷiṭattut tōṉṟum meyppāṭukaḷē ciṟantaṉaveṉṟu iṅku variyaṟut turikkappaṭṭaṉa.
mēṟcollappaṭṭa aṟuvakik kūṟṟiṉavākiya irupattu nāṉku meyppāṭu pōlvaṉa piṟavum nilippaṭṭa avi tamatu uṭpakutiyāki varuvaṉa piṟavum nilipeṟṟa oḻukalāṟākiya naṭuvaṇin tiṇikkēyuriya nimittamām meyppāṭukaḷākum eṉpar āciriyar.
maṉṉiya viṉi eṉappaṭṭa puṇarcci, talimakaḷukku āṟṟāmi nērntaviṭattu mēl aṟuvakippaṭak kūṟappaṭṭa meyppāṭukaḷi muṟiyē nimittamākak koṇṭu vārāmiyum urittu eṉpar āciriyar.
kaḷaviṟkuriyaṉavāka mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa mutalāṅkūṟu mutal āṟāṅkūṟu muṭiyavuḷḷa aṟuvaki meyppāṭukaḷiyum oṉṟu mutal āṟu avattikaḷ eṉavum, avaṟṟiṉ piṉ uḷavām uṉmattam, maṟattal, mayakkam, cākkāṭu eṉpaṉa muṟiyē ēḻu eṭṭu oṉpatu pattām avattikaḷ eṉavum pakutturippar iḷampūraṇar. piṟkūṟiya nāṉkum akaṉintiṇi yiṉpattiṟku uriyaṉa aṉmiyil ivaṟṟiyuñ cērttup pattavattikaḷeṉa āciriyar variyaṟi kūṟātoḻintār eṉpatu pērāciriyar karuttākum.
